After the rows, `cloneVolumeData` answers `unavailable` "Copying the volume data to the clone needs …" and a mount inside the clone's own directory "Writing the clone's file mounts to the server needs …" (Coolify stores, then queues both). **Danger Zone.** Coolify soft-deletes the application and queues `DeleteResourceJob`, which stops and removes what is on the server and then deletes the rows. An application nothing was ever deployed of — status `exited`, no container known, no deployment record — has nothing on any server and is deleted at once with what `deleteLocalResource()` and the `forceDeleting` hook remove: the backup schedules of its volumes and directories (with their executions and S3 links), its volume, file and directory mounts, environment variables, settings row, secret manager link, scheduled tasks (with their executions), tag links, previews, deployment records, and the row; the environment's and the project's "has resources" guards and the destination's "attached" check then no longer see it (tested). Any other application answers 501 "Stopping the application and removing its containers, volumes, networks and configuration files from the server needs …" and keeps every row. The pages `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/application/{app_uuid}/persistent-storage`, `/tags`, `/resource-operations` and `/danger` are ported from `service/storage.blade.php`, `service/file-storage.blade.php`, `shared/storages/all.blade.php`, `shared/tags.blade.php`, `shared/resource-operations.blade.php` and `shared/danger.blade.php` inside the configuration frame (`<x-modal-confirmation>` gains its checkbox step, `<x-forms.checkbox>` and the listbox's disabled option are added to the shared components). Departures and deferrals, all pages: the account password step of the confirmations is left out (deploy-core cannot check it), the audit trail is not ported, authorization is org-wide (`placeResource`, `createAnyResource` and `delete` are `deploy.resource.write`), rows are addressed by id (Coolify's own keys), "Coolify" in the copy reads "Freeploy". Storage: volume and directory backups are not ported — the Backup column shows a schedule that exists, there is no "Configure Backup" action, a schedule still blocks a delete; everything that reads a Compose file is not ported (external volumes, stale and driver-option entries, `useVariablePath`): a Docker Compose application's volumes are read-only and not deletable here; the "Is this based on the Git repository?" checkbox (shown with Preserve Repository) is not rendered, though `instantSave` accepts the flag; the content field is a textarea also for YAML (no Monaco) and is disabled where Coolify makes it read-only; the four "Add mount" dialogs use the shared dialog; `FileStorageChanged` broadcasts are not ported; a blank content is stored as null and a content is not trimmed. Tags: the name is compared as it is stored, so "Prod" finds "prod" (Coolify compares what was typed, misses the stored tag and fails on its unique index); the application's tags are listed oldest first (the pivot has no order of its own); the error toasts carry the name as text, without Coolify's `<span>`. Resource Operations: targets are addressed by uuid (`destination_uuid`, `environment_uuid`), not by id; after a clone or a move the page opens the application's Resource Operations page, where the `unavailable` notice shows (Coolify opens the configuration with `#resource-operations`); "Migrate to another server" shows in Coolify's development mode only and is not ported; a clone does not copy the secret manager link and queues no automatic DNS records; a copied file mount, a copied environment variable and a copied volume each get their own uuid (Coolify keeps the source's uuid on mounts and variables, which the unique `uuid` indexes of the folded schema refuse); two volumes that would get the same name in the clone refuse the whole clone (Coolify fails in the middle and keeps the part it had copied); `Str::limit` counts characters, not display width. Danger Zone: an application that may have something on a server cannot be deleted yet, also when its server does not answer — Coolify then deletes the rows and leaves the server as it is, and its "Server is not reachable" notice says so; the notice is not shown, a "Not available yet" notice takes its place for such an application; the previews are deleted with the application (Coolify soft-deletes them and leaves the rows); the volume rows go whatever `delete_volumes` says (the `forceDeleting` hook), and the options otherwise have nothing to act on yet.
- **Application source, servers, webhooks, scheduled tasks and rollback (DP-58).** Five more of an application's configuration pages, without SSH and without a migration, over the Git columns of `deploy.applications`, `deploy.additional_destinations`, `deploy.scheduled_tasks`, `deploy.scheduled_task_executions` and `application_settings.docker_images_to_keep`: `GET`/`PATCH …/deploy/applications/{uuid}/source`, `POST …/source/private-key`, `POST …/source/change`, `DELETE …/source/https-credentials`, `POST …/source/host-keys/fetch`, `DELETE …/source/host-keys/{id}`; `GET`/`POST …/servers`, `POST …/servers/stop`, `POST …/servers/{network_id}/promote` and `…/redeploy`, `DELETE …/servers/{network_id}?server_id=`; `GET`/`PATCH …/webhooks`, `POST …/webhooks/{provider}/generate`; `GET`/`POST …/scheduled-tasks`, `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE …/scheduled-tasks/{task_uuid}`, `POST …/scheduled-tasks/{task_uuid}/toggle` and `…/execute`; `GET`/`PATCH …/rollback`, `POST …/rollback/images` and `…/rollback/rollback`. All of it is a new handler (`handlers/application-more.ts`, dispatched by one line of `handleApplications`) inside `withOrg`, policy `deploy.resource.read`/`write` with 404 on denial (Coolify's `update`, `deploy`, `delete`, `useTeamCredentials` and `placeResource` are all `write`; `loadImages`, which asks for `view`, is a read), reads carrying `canUpdate` and `canDeploy`. The SQL is new files of `@saas/db/deploy` (`application-source.ts`, `additional-destinations.ts`, `scheduled-tasks.ts` — every function takes the task's owner, `application` or `service`, so services reuse it —, `application-rollback.ts`), tested on PGlite with row-level security; `deleteApplication` (DP-55) now also removes the application's `additional_destinations` rows (Coolify's foreign key cascades; the scheduled tasks and their executions were removed already). Ported from `app/Livewire/Project/Application/{Source,Rollback}.php`, `app/Livewire/Project/Shared/{Destination,Webhooks}.php`, `app/Livewire/Project/Shared/ScheduledTask/{All,Add,Show,Executions}.php`, `app/Rules/ManualWebhookSecret.php`, `Application`'s `deploymentType`, `customRepository`, `gitSshHost`, `gitBranchLocation`, `gitCommits`, `gitWebhook`, `additionalServersUnavailableReason` and `proxyMismatchReason`, `convertGitUrl`, `generateDeployWebhook`, `generateGitManualWebhook` and the known-hosts helpers of `bootstrap/helpers/applications.php`, with Coolify's and Laravel's messages word for word. **Git Source.** `submit()` in its order: the known-hosts lines ("Each line must be \"host key-type key\" …"), the credentials ("Enter the password or token for this username."), the hosts with published keys ("Freeploy always uses the published host keys of github.com, ssh.github.com, bitbucket.org, altssh.bitbucket.org. …"), then `validate()` — repository and branch required, `ValidGitBranch`, the commit SHA pattern, the lengths and patterns of the credentials — and `ValidGitRepositoryUrl(allowRepositoryPath: true)` only for a changed repository (both rules are DP-54's, reused); the three fields are trimmed, an empty commit is `HEAD`, the host keys are written only when they differ from what the form was loaded with (`loadedGitSshKnownHosts`, so a key a deployment saved meanwhile stays), the HTTPS password is an `encrypted` cast stored with the org's key bound to `applications.git_https_password` and never sent back (`hasGitHttpsPassword`). The pinned keys of GitHub, GitLab.com and Bitbucket Cloud (`GIT_PINNED_HOST_KEYS`, extracted from the pinned source by script) are listed for a repository on those hosts and the saved lines below them, each with `ssh-keygen`'s SHA256 fingerprint (Web Crypto; the test checks GitHub's three documented fingerprints) and the sha1 of its line as id; a saved key is removed by that id. The source is resolved as DP-54 prescribes: `source_id` 0 with GithubApp's morph class is `PUBLIC_GITHUB_SOURCE`, anything else a row of `github_apps`, `gitlab_apps` or `gitea_apps`; the other sources offered are `Team::sources()` (not public) that are usable (`app_id` set, or `isConnected()`), by name. `setPrivateKey` and `changeSource` are rows. **Servers.** The primary server, the additional destinations (the `additional_destinations` pivot: application, server, standalone network, status) and the networks the application can be added to, by `loadData()`'s rules (usable servers that can host resources, not the application's networks, its primary server or its additional servers). `addServer` (`syncWithoutDetaching`: a network is attached once) and `promote` (one transaction: the main destination changes, the network leaves the pivot, the former main destination joins it) are pure rows, refused in Coolify's words — "Failed to add server." / "Failed to promote." / "Failed to deploy." with "The server X is not reachable. …", "The server X cannot host resources.", "Docker Compose applications cannot use multiple servers.", "Applications with persistent storage cannot use multiple servers …", "The primary server uses TRAEFIK and X uses CADDY. …", "You are trying to remove the main server.". **Webhooks.** The deploy webhook and the four manual webhooks as Coolify builds them — `/api/v1/deploy?uuid={uuid}&force=false` and `/webhooks/source/{github,gitlab,bitbucket,gitea}/events/manual` (none when the application has a Git App source; a GitLab App source shows `/webhooks/source/gitlab/events`) — answered as paths: the host is the environment's api-edge origin, which deploy-web derives (`edgeBase()`) and deploy-core-worker does not know. **The endpoints that receive these URLs do not exist yet**: the Git webhooks are DP6, the deploy API is DP9; the page shows where they will be. The secrets are DP-49's four `encrypted` columns, decrypted only for who may update; `submit()` checks and writes only the secrets that changed (`ManualWebhookSecret`: "The GitHub webhook secret must be at least 16 characters.", an unchanged short secret keeps saving), `generateSecret` stores a `Str::random(40)`. **Scheduled Tasks.** CRUD with Add's and Show's rules and attribute names ("The run in a shell field must be true or false." on Add, "The use shell field …" on Show), the timeout from 60 to 36000, `validate_cron_expression` through DP-42's validator (the words `every_minute`, `hourly`, `daily`, `weekly`, `monthly`, `yearly` and their `@` forms, or a cron expression; "Invalid Cron / Human expression."), Add storing the fields as typed and Show trimmed, enable and disable, delete with the executions; the command is "Hidden (only admins can view)" and an execution's output absent for who may not update. **Rollback.** `docker_images_to_keep` (`integer|min:0|max:100`, "Settings saved.") and the server's "Disabled by the server" notice. **What needs the SSH executor, in Coolify's own order of row and command**: `promote` stores the rows and answers `unavailable` "Refreshing the status of the application's containers on the server needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." (`refreshServers()` runs after the transaction); `changeSource` stores the source and answers `unavailable` "Reading the repository's id from the Git provider needs the Git App connection, which is not available yet." (Coolify updates the row, then asks the provider's API with the App's credentials — DP6; `repository_project_id` stays as it was); `removeServer` stops the application on that server before it detaches the network, so it answers 501 "Stopping the application on the server needs …" and keeps the row unless nothing of the application can be on any server (the Danger Zone's rule: status `exited`, the pivot's status `exited`, no container known, no deployment ever queued), in which case the row goes at once; `stop` and `redeploy` on one server answer 501 ("Deploying the application to the server needs …") after Coolify's own checks (404 for a server the application is not on, "Before deploying to multiple servers, you must first set a Docker image in the General tab."); "Fetch current host key" answers 501 "Reading the host keys of the Git host needs …" (after "The repository does not use SSH."), so there is nothing to accept or decline; "Execute now" answers 501 "Running the scheduled task now needs …" and records no execution; the images of the Rollback page cannot be listed: `GET …/rollback` carries `images: null` with `imagesUnavailable` "Listing the application's images on the server needs …", "Reload images" answers 501 with the same text and `rollbackImage` 501 "Rolling back to an image needs …" — the page shows a "Not available yet" notice where the list would be and never Coolify's "No rollback images". **The scheduler that runs tasks is not part of this**: nothing creates an execution, so a task reads "Not run yet" and its executions "No executions yet"; when DP-56's queue exists, `redeploy`, `rollbackImage` and `executeNow` are the three places to wire. Pages, hand-ported from `source.blade.php`, `destination.blade.php`, `webhooks.blade.php`, `scheduled-task/{all,add,show,executions}.blade.php` and `rollback.blade.php` inside the configuration frame: `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/application/{app_uuid}/source`, `/servers`, `/webhooks`, `/scheduled-tasks` (a task opens in the dialog, `?task_uuid=` as Coolify's `#[Url]`) and `/rollback`. Departures and deferrals: the account password step of "Remove server" is left out and the audit trail is not ported (as DP-55); Source — private Git Apps are DP6, so the lists of other sources are empty until one exists, the "Git app" button (`getInstallationPath`) is not shown, source cards carry the generic sources icon instead of the provider's logo, `is_system_wide` sources of other orgs do not exist (a row belongs to one org), the providers' published keys are the pinned ones only (Coolify also reads GitHub's and Bitbucket's over HTTPS on "Fetch current host key" and caches them), `setPrivateKey` changes the key only (Coolify saves the form's unsaved fields with it), the "Connect source" links go to `/sources`, which is not ported; Servers — the status beside a server is `<x-status-summary>`'s summary as a badge without its popover, the restart-limit warning is not shown, `removeServer` carries the server in the query (the edge forwards no body with a DELETE), the "Failed to deploy." description is Coolify's sentence without its documentation link; Webhooks — the "Documentation" button (Coolify's API authorization page) and the API-disabled notice are not shown (API tokens are DP9), the "Source settings" button of a GitLab App is not shown (DP6), an empty secret is stored as null (Coolify stores an encrypted empty string; both disable the webhook); Scheduled Tasks — a Docker Compose application's service names are not read from its Compose file, so the container is the free-text field for every application, the folded schema has no `consecutive_failures`, `failing_since` and `disabled_reason` (Coolify's `2026_11_03_192735` columns: the scheduler's task must add them by migration; until then nothing disables a task and "Disabled because it keeps failing" never shows), the schema's `name`, `command`, `frequency` and `container` are `VARCHAR(255)` and a longer value is refused with Laravel's `max` message where Coolify lets the database fail, executions show their stored times as text (no server-timezone formatting, duration or "x ago"), have no polling, paging or download, and an invalid expression always answers the component's own "Invalid Cron / Human expression." (in Coolify `CronExpression`'s constructor can throw its own message first); Rollback — the audit of the setting is not ported, an absent `dockerImagesToKeep` keeps the stored value. "Coolify" in the copy reads "Freeploy".
- **Deployment queue and the Docker image deployment (DP-56).** Coolify's deployment queue and its deployment of a Docker image application, as code that is verified and that cannot deploy anything yet. **Nothing can be deployed until W3:** the engine needs the SSH executor (`env.SSH_SESSION`), which deploy-core-worker does not have bound, and a Workflow binding (`env.DEPLOYMENT_WORKFLOW`) that this task does not add — no `wrangler.template.jsonc` and no `component.yaml` changed. The engine has run against a fake executor and Coolify's PHP goldens only, never against a server. *Built.* `packages/db/src/deploy/deployments.ts` over `deploy.application_deployment_queues` (no migration): `queue_application_deployment`'s admission (duplicate commit skipped, `deployment_queue_limit`, `concurrent_builds`, one running deployment an application), `next_queuable`, `start_queued_deployment` and `start_next_queued_deployments`, `fail_unattended_deployment`, the status transitions with `finished_at` and `deploy_started_at`, the log in Coolify's stored JSON, the history query with its search and filters, and what the job reads (application, settings, destination, server, storages). `apps/deploy-core-worker/src/deployments/`: `compose.ts` (`generate_compose_file`), `yaml.ts` (Symfony's `Yaml::dump`), `docker-run-options.ts` (`convertDockerRunToCompose`), `runtime-env.ts` (`generate_runtime_environment_variables` and the log redaction's secrets), `logs.ts` (the log entry, `decode_remote_command_output`, the two exports), `git-ssh.ts`, `php.ts` (the PHP built-ins the ports need), `engine.ts` (`ApplicationDeploymentJob` for a Docker image: the steps start, detect, prepare, configure, deploy, health, drain, remove_old, post, failed, finish, with a plain JSON state between them so a run can stop after any step and go on), `session-executor.ts` (the engine's executor over ssh-executor's session), `sql.ts` (the store), `service.ts` (queue, history, one deployment, cancel, force start, debug logs) and `workflow.ts` (`ApplicationDeploymentWorkflow`: one `step.do` an engine step, `step.sleep` for the waits, `fail_unattended_deployment` when a step cannot run). Routes, in `handlers/deployments.ts` under `/v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/applications`: `POST /{uuid}/deploy` and `/{uuid}/restart` (201 `queued` with the deployment's page, 200 `skipped` with Coolify's toast, 409 when the queue is full, 501 for another build pack, 503 with the reason when the job cannot be started — the deployment is then failed at once, as Coolify's `fail_undispatchable_deployment` does), `GET /{uuid}/deployments` (`?skip&take&pull_request_id&search&sort&filters`), `GET /{uuid}/deployments/{deployment}`, `GET …/logs` (`?timestamps=0&search=`, or `?all=1` for who may update), `POST …/cancel`, `…/force-start` and `…/debug`. **Without `SSH_SESSION` Deploy and Restart are not handed to these routes: they keep DP-49's 501 "not available yet" and store nothing**, and cancel and force start answer 501; the history, one deployment, its logs and the debug switch are served either way. deploy-web: `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/application/{app_uuid}/deployment` (the history: search, the filter menu with status, source, server and pull request, the sort by start, the pages and page size, the empty state; reloads every 5 s on the first page) and `…/deployment/{deployment_uuid}` (the log viewer: follow, fullscreen, the status, Force start and Cancel, Find in logs with its match count and Ctrl+F, View → Timestamps and Debug logs, Export → copy, download, download full log; reloads every 2 s until the deployment is finished, failed or skipped); the heading's Deploy and Restart open the deployment's page when one was queued and show the "Deployment skipped" toast. *Verified.* `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/deployments/` holds 149 deployments and 154 function cases run through Coolify's own code (`emit.php`: the real models on SQLite in memory, the real `queue_application_deployment()` and `ApplicationDeploymentJob` on the sync queue, `Process::fake()` in place of SSH; the README has the command). 131 compose files and their `.env` files are compared byte for byte; 144 runs are replayed through the engine and must start the same processes with the same standard input in the same order and end with the same log, statuses, sleeps, notifications, queued jobs and exception; the helpers are compared case by case. `tests/db` covers the queue on PGlite with RLS, `deployments-api.test.ts` the routes with a fake executor binding. *Departures.* Prose that names Coolify reads "Freeploy" in log lines and toasts; names on the server stay Coolify's (the `coolify` network, `coolify-proxy`, `/data/coolify`, `COOLIFY_*`, the `coolify.*` labels, the links to coolify.io). The helper container runs Coolify's published image, `docker.io/coollabsio/coolify-helper:1.0.19` (an engine setting: Freeploy publishes no helper image of its own — to be decided before the first real deployment). A failed deployment's two hidden log lines with a PHP file and line are not written. A file's content for a command's standard input travels base64-encoded inside the command batch, because the executor's session takes a batch and no separate input. There is no SSH process id on the deployment and no Horizon job id. An application with hand-edited labels and none stored is refused with a message where Coolify's job dies with a PHP TypeError. The Workflow reads the application again at each step, so a setting changed during a deployment takes effect from the next step (Coolify's job holds the models it loaded). `.env` values are escaped with Coolify's current rules only (no application in Freeploy has the legacy escaping flag). The history shows a commit without the link to the Git host, and the log page does not show the history beside the logs (Coolify's embedded index), the image scan result or the "SSH host key changed" callout. Cancel removes the helper container through the executor and reports a cleanup failure as a 422 while the deployment stays cancelled. *Deferred.* Binding the executor and exporting and binding the Workflow (after W3; `ApplicationDeploymentWorkflow` is not exported from the worker's entry, and nothing imports it); a first deployment to a real server, which is the only test of the commands' effect; Stop (`StopApplication` — the route stays 501); the three places DP-58 left for the queue — a server's redeploy on the Servers page (the engine deploys to the application's own server only), Rollback (needs the server's image list) and a scheduled task's Execute (not a deployment) — all still answer 501; every other build pack, build servers and additional servers, preview deployments, Swarm, a non-root server user (`sudo`), a file storage with content (the engine refuses such an application), `preserveRepository`, the image scan gate, the retry of a command after a dropped SSH connection, the configuration snapshot and diff of a deployment, cancelling the deployments a newer one supersedes (webhooks and the API, DP6 and DP9), the cache of Git hosts' published SSH keys; and everything the job only asks for — the deployment notifications, `GetContainersStatus` (so an application's status changes only where the job itself sets it), `CleanupHelperContainersJob` and the broadcast events are recorded as requests that nothing serves yet.
- **Deployments for the Dockerfile, Nixpacks and static build packs (DP-61).** DP-56's engine also deploys the build packs that build on the application's server — a Dockerfile in a public Git repository, the pasted Dockerfile of a "simple Dockerfile" application (DP-54), Nixpacks and a static site — for a deployment that is no preview, on one standalone Docker server reached as root, without a build server. **It still cannot deploy anything:** the SSH executor and the Workflow binding are as DP-56 left them (W3), no wrangler or component file changed, and nothing has been built or deployed on a real server; the engine has run against a fake executor and Coolify's PHP goldens only. *Built.* `apps/deploy-core-worker/src/deployments/build.ts`, pure functions of plain row data: `set_coolify_variables`, `generate_coolify_env_variables` for a build, `generate_env_variables`, `generate_buildtime_environment_variables` (the Nixpacks plan's variables, Coolify's, the user's; literal and multiline values single-quoted, plain ones double-quoted so `$VAR` expands), the launcher of `save_buildtime_environment_variables` for dotted names, `wrap_build_command_with_env_export`, `analyzeBuildTimeVariables` with `EnvironmentVariableAnalyzer`'s table, `generate_build_secrets` and `generate_secrets_hash` (HMAC-SHA256), `dockerfileInstructions`, the ARG lines of `add_build_env_variables_to_dockerfile`, `modify_dockerfile_for_secrets`, the two static Dockerfiles, `addHostFlags`, `generateGitImportCommands` with `setGitImportSettings` for a public repository (shallow clone, a pinned commit, a short SHA, submodules with their SSH URLs rewritten, LFS), `gitLsRemoteCommand`, `nixpacks_build_cmd` with `generate_nixpacks_env_variables`, the plan rules of `generate_nixpacks_confs` (the build arguments joined to the plan's variables, a dotted name left out, curl and wget joined to the apt packages, Laravel's two variables, Elixir's and Node's notices) and PHP's `json_decode`/`json_encode(JSON_PRETTY_PRINT)` for the plan (a PHP array as an ordered map, `{}` written as `[]`, ints and floats kept apart, `/` and non-ASCII escaped). `engine.ts` gained the steps `restart`, `source`, `build` and `env` between `detect` and `deploy` (a Docker image still goes start, detect, prepare, configure, deploy): `deploy_dockerfile_buildpack`, `deploy_simple_dockerfile`, `deploy_nixpacks_buildpack`, `deploy_static_buildpack` and `just_restart` in their own orders, with `check_git_if_build_needed` (the branch's head by its exact ref, the commit saved on the deployment, `reuse_commit_message`, the helper restarted with the commit when build secrets are on), `clone_repository` with `import_repository` (a clone that ends with exit code 255 is cleaned up and tried again, three times in all, after 2 and 4 seconds — the wait is the Workflow's, the step goes on where it was), `withGitHints`, `save_git_ssh_known_hosts`, `generate_image_names`, `check_image_locally_or_remotely`, `should_skip_build`, `generate_nixpacks_confs`, `save_buildtime_environment_variables`, `generate_build_env_variables`, `add_build_env_variables_to_dockerfile`, `build_image` in every variant (BuildKit with secrets, BuildKit, the legacy builder; `--no-cache` for a forced rebuild or a disabled cache, `--pull` for a pasted Dockerfile, `--target`, a Dockerfile at its own location, a base directory, `--add-host`; a build served as a static site through its own build image), `build_static_image`, `parseHealthcheckFromDockerfile`, `push_to_docker_registry`, and `prepare_builder_image` with the build secrets in the helper's environment. `packages/db/src/deploy/deployment-builds.ts` (no migration): what a build reads of `deploy.applications` and `application_settings` and of its server's role, and what it writes — the commit and the commit message of a deployment, the health check columns, the SSH host keys under the row's lock. *Wired.* `POST …/applications/{uuid}/deploy` and `…/restart` queue a `dockerfile`, `nixpacks` or `static` application (with `git_commit_sha` as its commit) when `SSH_SESSION` is bound; **it is bound in no environment, so they keep answering DP-49's "not available yet" and store nothing**. Refused with 501 and the reason before anything is queued, and again by the engine before it runs anything: a Docker Compose application, Railpack, "Use a Build Server", a server for deployments only (Coolify builds such an application on a build server), "Preserve Repository During Deployment", and every Git source but a public repository — a deploy key, a Git App, HTTPS credentials (DP6). *Verified.* `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/deployments/` now holds 435 deployments made by Coolify's own PHP (the emitter of DP-56, extended: the public GitHub source as `GithubAppSeeder` creates it, earlier deployments with their configuration snapshot, what the user changed since, and under `build` the rows a build reads and what it leaves): 286 are builds — 79 of a Dockerfile in a repository, 55 of a pasted Dockerfile, 85 of Nixpacks, 67 of a static site; 226 end finished, 56 failed (a failing build, clone, `ls-remote`, push, helper, `nixpacks plan`, an invalid name, a secret without a source, an unsupported web server image, an unhealthy container) and 4 cancelled. `deployments-engine.test.ts` replays all 286, and the 143 Docker image runs that DP-56 replays (its note says 144; the suite always had 143): each must start Coolify's processes with Coolify's standard input in Coolify's order — every Dockerfile, build script, build-time and runtime `.env` file, Nixpacks plan and compose file byte for byte — and end with the same log, statuses, commit, commit message, health check columns, saved host keys, created variables, sleeps, notifications, queued jobs and exception. **Every one matches; no golden run is left out or compared loosely.** Each build is replayed a second time one step at a time with the state kept as JSON. `tests/db` covers the new SQL on PGlite with RLS; `deployments-api.test.ts` the queueing and each refusal. *Departures.* Coolify's configuration snapshot is not ported (as DP-56 said), so `pendingDeploymentConfigurationDiff()` is always what Coolify answers after a deployment without a snapshot: **a Freeploy deployment whose image exists logs "The last deployment stored no configuration snapshot. Rebuilding image." and builds again — the "image exists, skip build" branch is in the engine and replayed against the goldens (nothing changed, the build changed, a restart), but no Freeploy deployment reaches it until the snapshot is stored and compared.** The key of the build secrets hash is derived from `DEPLOY_ENCRYPTION_KEY` and the org (HMAC), where Coolify uses its `APP_KEY`. A base directory that is no safe path fails the deployment in the engine's first step, where Coolify's job constructor makes it "Deployment could not be started". The unavailable build packs and sources are refused before the queue, where Coolify has no such answer. A query Coolify leaves without an `ORDER BY` (the build arguments, the ARG lines, the `NIXPACKS_` variables) is read in the order the variables were created. `key NOT LIKE 'NIXPACKS_%'` is matched as Postgres matches it (case-sensitive, `_` any one character). A variable the job creates has no `order`, so it stands after the ordered ones as in Postgres; the goldens were made on SQLite, which puts it first, and the one case that could differ sorts by name. The plan's JSON keeps Coolify's shape except where PHP's arrays renumber integer-like keys of `variables`. "Coolify" in the prose of the new log lines reads "Freeploy" (the Git host hints, the host key notice); `COOLIFY_*`, `coolify.deploymentId` and the paths stay. *Cut.* **Railpack** — the pinned Coolify has it (`deploy_railpack_buildpack`, `build_railpack_image`, the generated `railpack.json`); none of it is ported and the build pack is refused. *Deferred.* Everything DP-56 deferred, unchanged: the bindings and a first real deployment, the notifications, `GetContainersStatus`, the cleanup jobs, additional servers (so `pull_image_for_additional_server` and the additional-server branches of `should_skip_build`), previews, Swarm, non-root servers, file storages with content (`write_missing_content_files`), the image scan steps (`rememberDockerfileBaseImage`, the gate), the retry of any command but the clone after a dropped connection; and of this task: storing and comparing the configuration snapshot, private Git sources with `update_repository_project_id` and the Bitbucket pull request commit (DP6), build servers and `selectBuildServer`, "Preserve Repository" (`docker cp` of the checkout), the cache of the Git hosts' published SSH keys (the pinned keys are the built-in ones), remote secrets (a `{{vault.KEY}}` reference fails the build with Coolify's message, as there is no secret manager), Docker Compose (DP-59 and after), and the pages — no deploy-web file changed: the deployment pages of DP-56 show a build's log as they show any other.

## Notes on DP6

- **Git sources and webhooks (DP-72).** Coolify's GitHub App source, private repositories through a GitHub App and with a deploy key, and the GitHub push and pull request webhooks, as code that is verified and that cannot deploy anything yet. **Nothing can be deployed until W3**, and **DP6's gate cannot be met here**: "opening a PR on a test repository creates a preview, updates the commit status, and closing it cleans the preview up" needs the SSH executor and a test VM (W1, W3). Today a pull request makes and removes the preview *row*, and the commit-status requests are built but never sent. No migration, no `wrangler.template.jsonc`, no `component.yaml` and no binding changed: api-edge reaches deploy-core-worker over the existing `DEPLOY_CORE_WORKER` binding. *API* (`apps/deploy-core-worker/src/handlers/sources.ts`, route `ORG_DEPLOY_SOURCES_RE`, inside `withOrg`, policy `deploy.resource.read`/`write` with 404 on denial): `GET`/`POST …/deploy/sources` (Team::sources and Create::createGitHubApp; a system-wide source is refused with 403), `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE …/sources/github/{uuid}` (Change: the form, instantSave, delete), `POST …/{uuid}/{manual,permissions,test,sync-name}`, `POST …/sources/github/redirect` (the manifest code exchange) and `…/install` (the installation callback; an installation on another account copies the App into a new source), `GET …/{uuid}/repositories` and `…/repositories/{owner}/{repo}/branches`. The App's client secret and webhook secret, and a deploy key, are encrypted with the org's key bound to their column. `sources/jwt.ts` signs Coolify's RS256 JWT with Web Crypto (a PKCS#1 key is wrapped as PKCS#8); `sources/github.ts` is `githubApi()` and the helpers of `bootstrap/helpers/github.php` behind an injectable fetch, with an installation token cached for 50 minutes. The manifest setup state is signed (HMAC from `DEPLOY_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, 60 minutes) and stateless, where Coolify keeps it in the session; it is not single-use. *Private repositories*: New Resource's `private-gh-app` and `private-deploy-key` types are creatable (`applications/catalogue.ts`), and `deployments/engine.ts` clones them as `ApplicationDeploymentJob` does — the installation token fetched at deploy time into the clone URL (and `repository_project_id` stored), or the key written in the helper container behind `GIT_SSH_COMMAND`; only GitLab and Gitea sources are still refused before anything is queued. Changing an application's source to a GitHub App now asks GitHub for the repository id. *Webhooks*: api-edge's `git-webhooks-facade.ts` takes `POST /webhooks/source/{github,gitlab,gitea,bitbucket}/events[/manual]` on the public origin (Coolify's paths; 413 over 25 MB), forwards the raw body and the provider's headers, and `handlers/git-webhooks.ts` answers. A delivery names no org, so the App or the applications are found across orgs with `asPlatformAdmin()` — org ids and row ids only — and everything after, the secret included, is read inside that org's `withOrg()`. The signature is `hash_hmac` compared with a constant-time `hash_equals`; push and pull_request are matched as Coolify matches them (branch, watch paths, `[skip ci]`, branch deletion, forks), a preview row is made for an opened pull request, skipped deployments are recorded, and every answer is Coolify's — except that where Coolify queues a deployment Freeploy answers "Deploying the application needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." No Freeploy server is reachable, so a delivery for a real application answers "Server is not functional." as Coolify does. `sources/pr-updates.ts` builds the requests of `ApplicationPullRequestUpdateJob` and `SyncGithubDeploymentJob` as pure functions; nothing sends them. *Pages* (deploy-web): `/sources`, `/source/github/{uuid}` with Permissions, Resources and Danger Zone (and the manifest and manual registration before the App is registered), the `/webhooks/source/github/redirect` and `/install` pages GitHub sends the browser back to, and New Resource's two private repository forms (Smart Scan stays "Not scanned yet"). *Verified* against Coolify's own PHP (`tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/sources`: 169 function cases, 56 webhook deliveries, 11 pull request updates and 15 deployment syncs; and 21 `ghapp.*` and 19 `deploykey.*` runs added to `fixtures/deployments`, the 435 earlier runs unchanged); the JWT is also verified with the system `openssl`. Every key in the fixtures is a labelled test key. *Departures*: the webhook receiver lives in deploy-core-worker, not a `webhooks-worker`; the pull request job runs inline after the answer; a closed pull request's preview row is deleted at once when no deployment of it was ever queued, and kept otherwise; one installation token per delivery or step, where Coolify asks for two; a non-numeric target id answers "No GitHub App found."; GitHub's `<br>`-joined error reads with newlines; Smart Scan is not run after the branches load; Coolify's DNS pinning and redirect policy for a GitHub Enterprise host are not ported. *Cut*, in the task's order: Bitbucket, Gitea and GitLab sources and webhooks (their paths answer 501 "… webhooks are not available yet."). *Not ported*: the GitHub OAuth login, `workflow_job` runner configurations, the failed-signature throttle (429), Docker Compose preview domains. **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted and built; no App has been registered with GitHub.

## Notes on DP7

DP7 work was started while DP4–DP6 remain open: what is left of those milestones is blocked on the SSH executor (WAITING W1/W3), and creating and configuring a database needs no server.

- **Standalone databases: create and General (DP-57).** Coolify's eight standalone database engines — PostgreSQL, Redis / Valkey, MongoDB, MySQL, MariaDB, KeyDB, Dragonfly, ClickHouse — created from the New Resource page and configured on their General page, without SSH: nothing is started. One implementation with the engine as a parameter; what differs between engines is data (`apps/deploy-core-worker/src/databases/engines.ts`, `DATABASE_TABLES` in `packages/db/src/deploy/databases.ts`). SQLite stays not creatable (it was not in this task's eight). **API** (`handlers/databases.ts`, its own route `ORG_DEPLOY_DATABASES_RE`, inside `withOrg`, policy `deploy.resource.read`/`write` with 404 on denial; the read carries `canUpdate`, `canManage`, `canDelete`): `POST …/deploy/databases { type, project_uuid, environment_uuid, destination, server_id, database_image }` is `Resource\Create::createResource()` with `create_standalone_<type>()`: a public id, the name `<type>-database-<uuid>`, `Str::password(length: 64, symbols: false)` for each password column (one letter and one digit, the rest from both sets, shuffled) encrypted with the org's key bound to `<table>.<column>`, the image for PostgreSQL and Redis (which ask for one first, as `Select::DATABASE_IMAGE_STEPS`), `supabase_admin` as the user of a Supabase image, then the created hook's volumes through DP-55's storage SQL (`postgres-data-<uuid>` on `/var/lib/postgresql` from PostgreSQL 18 on and `/var/lib/postgresql/data` before, read from the image as Coolify's pattern reads it — a registry port included; `redis-data-`, `mongodb-configdb-` and `mongodb-db-`, `mysql-data-`, `mariadb-data-`, `keydb-data-`, `dragonfly-data-`, `clickhouse-data-`), and for Redis the `REDIS_PASSWORD` and `REDIS_USERNAME` variables (encrypted as `environment_variables.value`); every other column keeps the schema's default. `GET …/databases/{uuid}` is Configuration, General and StatusInfo: the fields of the engine, the internal and public connection URLs, the SSL state; for a member who may not update, a password is `''`, the configuration, the custom Docker options and the init scripts are null and the URLs are null, as `syncData()` and `StatusInfo::refresh()` leave them. `PATCH …/databases/{uuid}` is `General::submit()` for the fields given, or — named by `action` — `instantSave` (the Access listbox), `instantSaveAdvanced` (the Log drain listbox) and `instantSaveSSL`; `POST`/`PATCH`/`DELETE …/init-scripts` are PostgreSQL's `save_new_init_script`, `save_init_script` and `delete_init_script`; `POST …/ssl/regenerate`, `POST …/keep-current-data-volume` (ClickHouse), `POST …/{start,restart,stop}` and `DELETE …/databases/{uuid}` (Coolify's query fields). Rules and messages are each General component's own `rules()`, `messages()` and `$validationAttributes` (KeyDB, Dragonfly and ClickHouse have no attributes, so their `required` messages read "The keydb password field is required."), on values coerced as PHP coerces them for the component's typed properties; an unchanged credential is not held to its pattern (`enforcePattern`), PCRE's `$` before a final newline is kept. The connection URLs are the models' `internal_db_url` and `external_db_url` accessors as pure functions (`databases/urls.ts`), with `rawurlencode()`, `formatHostForUrl()`, `sanitize_string()` for the image and PHP's `version_compare()` for Redis's "has users from 6.0" rule; `/etc/ssl/certs/coolify-ca.crt` and `/etc/mongo/certs/…` stay Coolify's (paths inside the container). KeyDB, Dragonfly and ClickHouse do not store their credentials once the database has started, as their `syncData()`; Redis's username is written only from Redis 6.0 on and a locked password is neither validated nor overwritten. The environment's resources list databases after applications, engine by engine and by name, with the model's status accessor (`exited:unhealthy`); `environmentHasResources`, `projectHasResources`, `destinationIsAttached` and `listDestinationResources` already counted the eight tables and are now tested with a row of each. **Verified against Coolify's own PHP** (`tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/databases`, emitted by booting Coolify at `91b7e3e`): the rows of the eight `create_standalone_*()` helpers run for real against an in-memory SQLite (17 cases: which columns are set and to what, the volumes, Redis's variables), 116 connection URL cases, 874 validation cases through Laravel's validator, the models' tables, morph classes and `encrypted` casts, and the shape of `Str::password`; the SQL (the schema's defaults column by column, the delete with everything it removes, row-level security) in `tests/db` on PGlite. **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted and built; the visual and DOM gates have not seen them. **One migration, `630_deploy_database_public_port_timeout`**: the folded schema stops at `public_port`; Coolify's `2026_02_27_000000_add_public_port_timeout_to_databases` (integer, nullable, default 3600, on the eight standalone tables and `service_databases`) was not folded, so General's "Proxy timeout" field had no column. **What needs the SSH executor, in Coolify's own order of row and command**: Start, Restart and Stop answer 501; making a database public is refused with Coolify's "Public port is required." and "Database must be started to be publicly accessible." and, for a running database, answers 501 "Starting the database's TCP proxy on the server needs …" and stores nothing (Coolify stores, starts the proxy, and puts `is_public` back when that fails); "Private" answers 501 for a database that was started or is public, and is stored — with the ports the form holds — for one that was never started, where no proxy container can exist; the log drain switch is stored (Coolify stores and asks for a restart) unless the server has no log drain ("Log drain is not enabled on the server. Please enable it first.", nothing stored); SSL and its mode are stored as `instantSaveSSL()` does (the certificate is made at the first start); adding an init script and editing one's content are rows only, renaming one and deleting one remove a file on the server first and answer 501, keeping the row; ClickHouse's "Keep current data" answers 501 and its warning never shows (the unnamed volume is read from the container); "Regenerate SSL Certificates" answers Coolify's "No existing SSL certificate found for this database." — and 501 "… is not available yet." if a certificate row exists, which nothing creates before a first start. **Deleting.** A database that was never started — status `exited`, no `started_at` — has nothing on a server and is deleted at once with what `deleteLocalResource()`, the `forceDeleting` hook and the model's traits remove: the backup schedules of its volumes and file mounts, its volumes, file mounts, certificates, database backup schedules (with executions, S3 replicas and S3 links), tag links, variables, image scans with their policy, ignores and notification states, secret manager link, stars, and the row; any other database answers 501 and keeps every row. **Pages**, hand-ported: `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/database/{db_uuid}` (`project.database.configuration`) — the heading (`heading.blade.php`: Start, or Restart with Stop and their dialogs; "Server unavailable" while the server is not functional), the menu of `database/configuration-sidebar.blade.php` with every entry (only General is ported; the others link to their Coolify route and show the not-ported notice), and the General page of the eight `general.blade.php` views as one component with the copy per engine word for word, `database-status-info.blade.php` and `init-script.blade.php`; the New Resource page gains the PostgreSQL and Redis / Valkey image steps and the "Creating …" page of `resource/create.blade.php`; the environment page lists databases with their engine's logo. Departures and deferrals: `server_id` and `destination` are uuids; a type that is not one of the eight, and PostgreSQL or Redis without `database_image`, answer 422 (Coolify shows the type picker or the image step); the destination must be on a server whose role hosts resources, as for applications, and a Swarm destination is not refused by the API (Coolify hides Swarm servers in the picker only; so does the page); `submit()` writes the fields given, and never `is_public` or `is_log_drain_enabled`, which their listboxes save — an instant save stores its own switch (and, for Access, the two port fields) where Livewire's `syncData(true)` also validates and stores every unsaved field of the form; `ssl_mode` must be one of the engine's modes (Coolify lets the column's check constraint fail), and the "only while the database is stopped" rule is the view's, as in Coolify; a value a typed Livewire property cannot hold (null for a credential, a list) answers 422 with Laravel's `string` or `boolean` message where Livewire fails; `config_hash` is not computed (`isConfigurationChanged()` after a first save, the "configuration changed" notice); a `REDIS_PASSWORD` that references a shared variable or a remote secret is used as its text in the URLs, and "locked" means the variable is shown once (Coolify also follows a reference to a locked shared variable); the restart counters, health check columns and `last_restart_*` of Coolify's models are not in the folded schema (the heading has no restart-limit warning, the resources list no restart count); a never-started database is recognized by `started_at`, which only the start action will set — that task must mark the database before it touches the server; the delete options have nothing to act on yet and volumes go whatever `delete_volumes` says (the hook); the account password step of confirmations and the audit trail are left out; authorization is org-wide (`view`, `update`, `manage`, `manageEnvironment`, `delete` are `deploy.resource.read`/`write`). Pages: the heading's actions and the menu stay in the page (Coolify moves them to the top bar and the main sidebar from xl); no status polling, star toggle, "Working" indicator, startup log dialog or configuration checker; the "Details" dialog, the proxy's "View logs" dialog and ClickHouse's data volume warning are not rendered; an init script form saves with its own "Save changes" button (Coolify: the floating unsaved bar per script), and the page stays where it is after an init script or SSL change with unsaved edits kept; KeyDB's configuration helper shows its link as text; the Terminal and Files entries show to who may update; the "Creating …" page shows the refusal and a Back link when the API refuses; "Coolify" in the copy reads "Freeploy".
- **Starting, stopping and restarting databases (DP-62).** Coolify's start, restart and stop of a standalone database and the TCP proxy of a public one, for all eight engines, as code that is verified and that has started nothing. **Nothing can be started until W3:** the routes need the SSH executor (`env.SSH_SESSION`), which deploy-core-worker does not have bound; no `wrangler.template.jsonc`, no `component.yaml` and no migration changed. The engine has run against a fake executor and Coolify's PHP goldens only, never against a server. *Built*, in `apps/deploy-core-worker/src/databases/`: `start-plan.ts` — `buildStartPlan()`, a pure function from plain row data (the database with its decrypted columns, its volumes, file mounts and variables, the destination's network, the server's address, Docker version and log drain, the CA certificate) to the docker-compose file and the ordered command list of `StartPostgresql`, `StartRedis`, `StartMysql`, `StartMariadb`, `StartMongodb`, `StartKeydb`, `StartDragonfly` and `StartClickhouse`: the image, container name, restart policy, `defaultDatabaseLabels()`, each engine's health check (with `healthCheckPortArguments()` reading a port out of the configuration, PostgreSQL's `-h 127.0.0.1` for a Supabase image, Redis's `-e` from 6.2 on, MongoDB's `mongosh`-or-`mongo` shell line), the environment (each engine's credentials unless a variable of that name exists, `PGUSER`/`PGPASSWORD`, the `COOLIFY_*` variables, the value formatting of `HasSecretManager` including the escaping of variables saved before exact escaping), networks, volumes (named, host path, file mounts, the configuration file, PostgreSQL's init scripts, MongoDB's init directory, the CA file), ports, memory and CPU limits, `cpuset`, ClickHouse's `ulimits`, the custom Docker options (`generateCustomDockerRunOptionsForDatabases()`: `--ip`/`--ip6`, a custom ulimit replacing the default), the log drain, the SSL command-line flags per engine and — for MongoDB — per `ssl_mode`, the quoting of a Redis, KeyDB or Dragonfly password (and the unquoted argument a legacy database keeps); then `mkdir`, the CA file, every file as `echo '<base64>' | base64 -d | tee`, `chmod`/`chown`, the README, the pull, the `chown` of the certificate through a one-off container, `docker stop` (`--time` or, from Docker 28, `--timeout`), `docker rm -f`, `docker compose up -d`. `proxy-plan.ts` — `StartDatabaseProxy`/`StopDatabaseProxy`: the nginx stream configuration (IPv4 or both listeners, the upstream as a variable, `proxy_timeout` with its one-hour default), the compose file (`Yaml::dump($compose, 4, 2)`, for which `deployments/yaml.ts` gained the indentation parameter) and the six commands. `content-files.ts` — `EnsureContentFilesOnServer` and `LocalFileVolume::saveStorageOnServer()` for a file: the one state check of all content files, the confinement check inside the database's directory and the symlink check outside it, an empty directory replaced, `chown`/`chmod`, and Coolify's warnings. `start-engine.ts` — the steps `check` (`StartDatabase::queueStart()`: server functional, ClickHouse's unnamed data volume, the CA), `start` (`DatabaseStartJob`: the certificate rows, the content files, the plan, the one batch through `DatabaseStartCommandExecutor` with its log per complete line), `status` (`ResourceStatusRefresher`: up to eight inspections two seconds apart while the health check says `starting`) and `failed` (`markFailed()`), with a plain JSON state between them; `RestartDatabase` (its checks, then the start, whose script stops the old container only after the pull), `StopDatabase` (stop and remove, `exited`, the proxy of a public database), the proxy's start (a taken port makes the database private again and asks for the notification) and stop, and `regenerateSslCertificate()`. SSL as Coolify does it: `Server::ensureCaCertificate()` makes the CA with DP-48's generator when the server has none and writes it to the server, the database's certificate is generated when it has none, with its row and its file mounts (`server.crt` 644 and `server.key` 600, or MongoDB's `server.pem` 600); a start without SSL deletes both. The health check settings and the restart counters are migration 660's columns (DP-60): the plan reads `health_check_enabled`, interval, timeout, retries and start period from the row; a start and a restart reset `restart_count`, `last_restart_at` and `last_restart_type` once they are past Coolify's checks (a refused start keeps them), and a stop resets them unless its body says `dockerCleanup: false` — the page's checkbox, which `Heading::stop()` passes as `resetRestartCount` — writing nothing, as Eloquent does, when they are reset already; the goldens hold all of it. `start-sql.ts` and `packages/db/src/deploy/database-operations.ts` are the engine's reads and writes in Postgres (no migration), `operations.ts` what a route answers. *Routes.* `POST …/deploy/databases/{uuid}/start`, `/restart` and `/stop` answer 501 "… needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." and store nothing while `SSH_SESSION` is absent — always, today — and so does the Access listbox of `PATCH` for a database that runs or is public. With the binding they run the engine and answer 200 with the start's log (`activity`), the status, the proxy's outcome and what was left (`deferred`), or 422 with Coolify's refusal ("Server is not functional", the ClickHouse and CA messages, "Database stop failed: …"); the Access listbox stores the switch, then starts or removes the proxy. `POST …/ssl/regenerate` now does the rows where it answered 501: a new key and certificate with the old one's names and paths, its row and file mounts replaced, and `unavailable` in the answer while the files cannot be written to the server (no database has a certificate before a first start, so no user reaches this yet). *Verified against Coolify's own PHP* (`tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/database-start`, DP-56's method: the real models on SQLite in memory, the real `StartDatabase`, `DatabaseStartJob` run as a worker runs it, `RestartDatabase`, `StopDatabase`, `StartDatabaseProxy` and `StopDatabaseProxy`, `Process::fake()` in place of SSH): 644 runs — 461 starts (PostgreSQL 65, Redis 62, MySQL 58, MariaDB 55, MongoDB 58, KeyDB 59, Dragonfly 55, ClickHouse 49) and 183 restarts, stops and proxy runs — with 1,553 SSH processes and 267 different compose files. The replay starts every one of those processes with the same text in the same order, which holds each command list and each compose file byte for byte, and ends with the same answer, log, rows, sleeps, queued jobs, events and notifications: **644 of 644 match, none excluded.** Read with a rule, each documented in the fixture's README: a generated certificate (a fresh key and a random serial cannot be reproduced) is masked the same way on both sides wherever its text stands, and held to Coolify's subject, issuer, validity, curve and signature; `started_at` (below); the start's own job in the list of queued jobs; and the `echo 'Warning: …'` lines, which name Freeploy. `tests/db` covers the SQL on PGlite with RLS; `database-operations-api.test.ts` the routes with and without a fake binding. The worker's SQL-backed loader and store (`start-sql.ts`) were run once end to end against PGlite in a scratch test (a PostgreSQL start with SSL and a public port, a Redis start, a regeneration, a stop) that is not committed — `tests/db` cannot import the worker and `tests/deploy-core-worker` has no Postgres — and that was before the rebase onto migration 660, so the health check and restart counter reads and writes are covered by `tests/db` (on the schema at head, `deploy-schema.ts`) and by type-checking, not by a run of the loader. *Departures.* **`started_at` is set before the first command of a start reaches the server** (Coolify sets it from the page, once it sees a started database running): DP-57's delete relies on a database without it having nothing on any server, so a failed start leaves it set. **The start runs inside the request and its log is in the answer, not stored**: Freeploy has no queue for it and no activity table, so there is no log to follow or to reopen, and Coolify's guard against a second start, restart or import of the same database (`DatabaseOperationReservation`, `ResourceStartActivity`, the stale and superseded starts) is not ported — two starts at once are not refused. A stop answers with StopDatabase's sentence (Coolify queues the stop and shows "Gracefully stopping database."). The file of a new certificate, which Coolify writes in a queued `ServerStorageSaveJob`, is written by the route at once, before the start checks which files are missing, so a regenerated certificate replaces the file an older one left. A failed proxy start or removal from the Access listbox fails the request as a whole and stores nothing (Coolify puts `is_public` back and keeps the form's ports); the proxy container of a database that never started is not removed (there is none). A warning about a content file reads "Freeploy"; names on the server stay Coolify's (`/data/coolify/databases/<uuid>`, `/data/coolify/ssl/coolify-ca.crt`, the `coolify.*` labels, `COOLIFY_*`, the CA's common name). A `{{vault.KEY}}` reference stops a start with Coolify's "… no secret manager source is configured." unless the variable is literal — there is no secret manager. A server with a non-root user is refused with a message of Freeploy's, and one whose key the executor cannot use with another. *Deferred.* Binding the executor (W3) and a first start on a real server, which is the only test of the commands' effect; a Workflow for the start (inside a request a long image pull holds the request open) with a stored log, the page's side of it (the log dialog, status polling, the "configuration changed" notice) and the guard against concurrent operations; deploy-web is unchanged — its heading still expects the 501; `RegenerateSslCertJob` after a new CA (named in the answer's `deferred`); `sudo` and the `DOCKER_CONFIG` prefix for a non-root server; Swarm; the pull of a scanned image build (`ImageStartPin`) and the image scan after a start; `NotifyResourceStoppedJob`'s expected-stop mark and the Docker cleanup after a stop (which the page's Stop never asks for: it passes its checkbox as `resetRestartCount`); the restart counters' other writers (the status job that counts crash restarts, `restart_limit_reached`) and the page's display of them; the broadcast events and the "proxy disabled" notification, which are handed over and have no receiver; `config_hash`; SQLite; a service's database and its proxy; and everything DP-57 left that still needs the server — deleting a started database, renaming and deleting an init script's file, ClickHouse's "Keep current data" — which still answer 501.
- **Database environment variables, storage, tags, resource operations and danger zone (DP-64).** The configuration pages Coolify renders for a standalone database with its shared components and `:resource="$database"` (`database/configuration.blade.php`), for all eight engines, without SSH and without a migration (every column is in the head schema: the two switches on each engine's table, the limits, the backup schedules' S3 pivot). **Generalised, not copied.** DP-51's store, service and handler already took the resource type: `ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLE_RESOURCES` (`packages/db/src/deploy/environment-variables.ts`) gains one entry per engine keyed by Coolify's `type()` (`standalone-postgresql` … `standalone-clickhouse`, `databaseEnvironmentVariableResourceType()`), the morph class from DP-57's `DATABASE_TABLES`, a select over the engine's table (no build pack, Git repository, Dockerfile or Compose file; the database's server for the `server` scope) and its own switches (`environmentVariableSettingColumns()`: an application's four on `application_settings`, a database's `include_coolify_default_variables` and `redact_all_env_values_in_logs` on its own row, as `saveCoolifyDefaultVariables()` and `saveLogRedactionSetting()` write them; the SQL refuses another column). DP-55's storage, tags, Resource Operations read and Danger read run on a `ResourcesContext` (`applications/resources.ts`: `ResourceRowsStore` — volumes, mounts and tags, bound to one morph class — and a loader giving a `LoadedResource`: its frame, workdir, Compose and Swarm flags, `git_based()`, the multiple servers reason, `Server::isFunctional()` and whether nothing of it is on a server); the application's handler wraps its unchanged store (`applicationResources()`), and `sharedResourcePageOp()` is the one route table of those pages for both handlers; answers carry `application` or `database` as their frame. DP-52's Resource Limits are `resourceLimits()`/`resourceLimitChanges()` over any row's `limits_*` columns; `strLimit()` takes Str::limit's ending. The application pages and their API behave as before, and their suites (`application-resources`, `environment-variables`, `application-settings`, `tests/db`) pass unchanged; tests/deploy-core-worker went to 3411 tests (2 skipped, as before) and tests/db to 1031. **API**: `…/deploy/databases/{uuid}/environment-variables[/…]` (every DP-51 route, `handleEnvironmentVariables` with the database's type — the engine is looked up first, in its own org-confined transaction), `…/storages[/volumes|files/{id}]`, `…/tags[/{id}]`, `GET …/resource-operations`, `POST …/resource-operations/clone` and `…/move`, `GET`/`PATCH …/resource-limits`, `GET …/webhooks`, `GET …/danger`, `POST …/danger/flush` (the delete stays DP-57's `DELETE …/databases/{uuid}` with its never-started rule): `handlers/database-resources.ts`, dispatched by one `if` of `handleDatabases`, the databases route now four segments deep, inside `withOrg`, policy `deploy.resource.read`/`write` with 404 on denial, reads carrying `canUpdate`, `canManage`, `canDelete`. **What a database does differently, ported:** its variables get no preview copy (the created hook is the application's), `showPreview` is false and the developer view has no preview text; the order and build secrets switches are an application's only (the view's `$resourceClass === Application`), and sending one for a database answers what Coolify's `instantSave()` raises on a resource without a settings row — 422 'Attempt to assign property "is_env_sorting_enabled" on null' — with nothing stored; Redis's `REDIS_PASSWORD` and `REDIS_USERNAME` are `$is_redis_credential` on the page (the name fixed and marked required, the value marked required, no flag listboxes, no Build time / Runtime marks in the table) and, as in Coolify, `Show::submit()` refuses nothing for them on the server; the storage directory is `workdir()` = `/data/coolify/databases/{uuid}` (`databaseWorkdir()`), with no Swarm flag (`Storage::mount()` looks for one for an application only), no preview suffix controls and the plain helper text, and the data volume DP-57 created is a volume like any other (Coolify lets it be renamed and deleted; nothing marks it); the clone (`databases/resources.ts`, `cloneTo()`'s database branch) replicates the row column for column with a new uuid, `clone_resource_name()` (the old `-clone-…` suffixes dropped, `Str::limit` to 255 minus the suffix, no ending), status `exited`, `started_at` null, the chosen environment and destination and `last_online_at` now (the saving hook) — credentials, public port and configuration as stored: Coolify generates no new password or port —, then its tags, its volumes under `cloneTo()`'s names (a known data prefix with the clone's uuid; else the source's uuid replaced; else `<clone uuid>-<name>`, which is what MongoDB's `mongodb-configdb-`/`mongodb-db-` volumes get, as in Coolify; the created hook's volumes are not made since Coolify deletes them first), its file and directory mounts, its backup schedules with their S3 destinations (`copyS3StoragesTo()`; executions are not copied), and its variables with the creating hook's version `4.7.6` (Coolify saves them with events); move sets the environment; Resource Limits are `ResourceLimits` on the database's own row with the same defaults, rules and messages; Webhooks is the deploy webhook only (`webhooks.blade.php`'s branch for what is not an application); the Danger Zone's label is "resource", and Redis, KeyDB and Dragonfly have "Flush all data". **What needs the SSH executor, in Coolify's own order:** everything DP-55 answers for an application's storage answers the same for a database (a managed file mount is stored with "Writing the file to the server needs …", a directory inside the database's directory answers 501 "Checking the path on the server needs …", deleting the Docker volume or a mount on the server answers 501 and keeps the row — Coolify itself fails there for a database, reading `$this->resource->service->server`); a clone stores its rows and then reports "Copying the volume data to the clone needs …" when `cloneVolumeData` and the database has volumes, and "Writing the clone's file mounts to the server needs …" for a managed mount inside the clone's own directory; `flush()` answers 501 "Flushing the database's data on the server needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." (404 for another engine). **Tests:** `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/database-resources.test.ts` (32: routing and the four-segment route through `route()` and `handleDatabases`, authorization, PostgreSQL and Redis end to end on every page, the clone's names and rows, and a table-driven pass over the eight engines: every page's frame, the variables' type and morph class, the volume names of a clone, flush), `tests/db/src/deploy-database-resources.test.ts` on the head schema (27: the variables' resource row, insert, list and the two switches per engine and the refused column, move, the replica column for column per engine, the variables' and backup schedules' replicas with the S3 pivot, the limits per engine, row-level security), `apps/deploy-web/test/routes.test.ts` (the seven routes). **Pages:** `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/database/{db_uuid}/{environment-variables,persistent-storage,tags,resource-operations,resource-limits,webhooks,danger}` are the applications' page components with a `kind` prop (`EnvironmentVariablesContent`, `StoragesContent`, `TagsContent`, `ResourceOperationsContent`, `ResourceLimitsContent`, `DangerContent`; `useResourceSection()` and `ResourceWorkspace` give the frame — the database's heading and menu, whose entries now open these pages), in `pages/DatabaseSharedPages.tsx`; a clone or a move opens the database's Resource Operations page. **Not in this task:** Servers (`shared/destination` stops, redeploys and promotes on servers), Healthcheck (`database/health`, the database's own component), Metrics, Logs, Terminal, Files, Security, the import and Backups pages (Backups is DP-65's; the storage page's "New Scheduled Backup" action for a database is not rendered); the secret manager link (`secret-manager-links` on the variables page, `cloneSecretManagerLinkTo()` on a clone); the database's tags in the environment's resources list (DP-57's `databaseResource()` still answers none); the account password step of the confirmations; the audit trail; `configurationChanged`. **Departures:** every DP-51 and DP-55 departure holds for a database too (org-wide authorization, `viewSensitive` as write, rows by id, a copied mount, variable or schedule gets its own uuid where Coolify keeps the source's — the unique indexes refuse it —, two volumes that would share a name refuse the whole clone, targets by uuid, the "Server is not reachable" notice not shown); the environment variables' engine lookup runs before the policy is asked, so a database that is not the org's answers 404 without a policy call (a denial answers the same 404); a database's clone reads `cloneVolumeData` and reports its copy after every row is stored, where Coolify stops the source and copies the volumes between storing the volumes and the mounts; `flush` is `POST …/danger/flush` and answers 404 for an engine without it (Coolify's view shows no button); the webhooks page, like the application's, shows no Documentation link and no API-disabled callout; "Coolify" in the copy reads "Freeploy". **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted and built.
- **S3 storages and backup schedules (DP-65).** Coolify's Integrations → Storage for S3-compatible storages and a standalone database's backup schedules, configuration only: nothing runs a schedule (the scheduler and `DatabaseBackupJob` are not ported). No migration: `500`/`660` already have `s3_storages`, `scheduled_database_backups`, the two pivots and the executions with their replicas. **API** (inside `withOrg`, `deploy.resource.read`/`write`, 404 on denial): `handlers/s3-storages.ts` with its own `ORG_DEPLOY_STORAGES_RE` — `GET`/`POST …/deploy/storages`, `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE …/storages/{uuid}`, `POST …/{uuid}/validate`, `GET …/{uuid}/resources`, `POST …/resources/{backup}/move { storage_uuid }`, `DELETE …/resources/{backup}`; `handlers/database-backups.ts`, dispatched by one early `if` of `handleDatabases` — `GET`/`POST …/databases/{uuid}/backups`, `GET`/`PATCH`/`POST`/`DELETE …/backups/{backup}` (`PATCH` is `submit`/`instantSave`, or `action` `toggleEnabled` / `toggleS3`; `POST` is Back up now; `DELETE` takes `delete_associated_backups_locally` / `_s3`). **Storages**: `Create`'s and `Form`'s rules, messages and attribute names (the S3 type of `S3Storage::connectionRules()`, `normalizeEndpoint()` on create, the saved secret kept when the form leaves it empty), the key and secret encrypted (`s3_storages.key` / `.secret`, fp1) — the key is returned to who may update, the secret never; a storage is stored, usable, only after the connection test works, with the URL style that worked (create and update, as `Create::submit()` and `Form::submit()`'s rolled-back transaction); Validate tests the form's unsaved values and stores only `is_usable`. **The connection test** is `S3Storage::testConnection()` for S3: the endpoint through a port of `SafeWebhookUrl::validate()` (PHP's `FILTER_VALIDATE_URL` and `parse_url()`, the blocked host names, the private / reserved / link-local / special-use IPv4 and IPv6 ranges, IPv4-mapped IPv6, every resolved address), then `ValidS3BucketName`, then one ListObjectsV2 as Flysystem's `listContents('', false)` makes it — `GET /{bucket}/?list-type=2&prefix=&delimiter=%2F` (path-style; the endpoint's path kept), or `{bucket}.{host}` for a bucket that is one lower-case DNS label (a dotted or upper-case bucket stays path-style, as the SDK's endpoint rules), the SDK's three attempts for a 5xx or a failed exchange, 15 s — signed with **AWS Signature Version 4 on Web Crypto** (`s3-storages/sigv4.ts`, no dependency), then `listFilesWithAddressingStyleFallback()` and `toUserFriendlyConnectionException()`. The failure message is Coolify's: Flysystem's `Unable to list contents for '', shallow listing` around the SDK's `Error executing "ListObjectsV2" on "…"; AWS HTTP error:` and `XmlErrorParser`'s `Code (client|server): Message` (an empty body: `NotFound` for 404 and the status line; a body that is not XML: "Unable to parse error information…"). **Verified** (`tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/s3`, README there): the signer against 21 cases of AWS's published SigV4 test suite (canonical request, string to sign, Authorization; botocore's `aws4_testsuite`), and against the Authorization header aws-sdk-php 3.403.6 computed for each of 19 ListObjectsV2 requests Coolify's disk sent to a fake endpoint (`fake-s3.php`), with the host, path and query of each; every failure answer's message; `S3Storage::testConnection()` itself in 10 cases (fallback that works and that fails, no fallback from virtual-hosted style or for another error, bucket and endpoint refused); 74 validation cases of the two forms; Laravel's answers for `BackupEdit`'s rules (22 cases), `validateDatabasesBackupInput()` (15) and `validate_cron_expression()` (48) — all emitted by Coolify's own PHP (`emit.php`). The fake endpoint is never the network in tests: the HTTP exchange and the host resolver are injected. **Delete and Resources**: deleting a storage is never refused — Coolify's "used by backups" guard is the dialog's warning ("N backup schedule(s) will stop saving to S3"), and `detachFromBackups()` runs first for database and volume schedules (their other destinations kept, the primary moved, `save_s3` off when none remains), the storage's replicas marked `s3_storage_deleted` with `refreshS3Summary()` on their executions, and `s3_cleanup_pending` cleared where nothing is left; the Resources tab is `Resources::moveBackup()` and `disableS3()` with their toasts, for database schedules. **Backup schedules**: created as `CreateScheduledBackup::submit()` (frequency `required|string`, "Invalid Cron / Human expression.", enabled, S3 off, `databases_to_backup` = the database the container creates for PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB and ClickHouse; Redis, KeyDB and Dragonfly have no backup solution: "Scheduled backups are not supported for this database type.", and their Backups page goes back to General); saved as `BackupEdit::syncData(true)` in its order — `validateDatabasesBackupInput()` first, then `customValidate()` (only usable storages of the org, or the schedule's own, may be destinations; "Select at least one usable S3 storage."; no local-copy deletion without S3; "Invalid Cron / Human expression"), then the `#[Validate]` rules with Laravel's messages (the toast is ValidationException's "… (and N more errors)"), then the row and `syncS3Storages()`; `toggleS3()` ("Select a usable S3 storage before enabling S3 backups.") and `toggleEnabled()` with their toasts; the per-engine "what to back up" (All / Specific databases for PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB; MongoDB's `db:col1,col2|db2`; ClickHouse's list); the server's timezone ("Instance timezone" without one). **Cron**: `helpers/cron.ts` accepts exactly what Coolify's `validate_cron_expression()` accepts in the goldens (written from that behaviour); it is a clean-room implementation, written from the golden cases and crontab(5) without reference to poliander/cron (GPL-3.0), the library Coolify calls; the earlier port in `servers/settings-validation.ts` (server settings, scheduled tasks) accepted more — month and weekday names in ranges (`MON-FRI`), `L`, `W`, `#`, `?`, `@midnight` — which Coolify refuses; DP-68 replaced it with this module. **Not available yet**: Back up now — "The database must be running to start a backup.", then 501 "Backing up the database needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet."; deleting a schedule's files — 501 "Deleting the local backup files needs the SSH executor…" and "Deleting the backup files from S3 is not available yet." when files exist (none can before a backup runs), else the schedule, its executions, replicas and destinations are deleted; SFTP, FTPS and WebDAV storages — 501 "SFTP storages are not available yet." (the Type listbox offers them, as Coolify); executions are read from the table (`GET …/backups` lists them) and are empty: no row is ever made up. DP-57's database delete already removed a database's schedules with their executions, replicas and destinations (checked, and tested again with a schedule made by this SQL). **Pages**, hand-ported: `/storages` (table and grid, search, New storage in a dialog with `<x-forms.domain-input>`, the URL style following the provider until chosen — Coolify has no `/storages/new`), `/storages/{uuid}` General, `/resources`, `/danger`, and `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/database/{db_uuid}/backups` inside DatabaseChrome (counts, Schedules, Executions' empty state, the schedule's dialog with General and Danger Zone opened by `?schedule=`, which `…/backups/{backup_uuid}[/executions|danger|s3|retention]` redirects to, as Coolify's routes do); the sidebar already had Storage (`storages`, `storages/*`). **Departures**: Coolify's policy gives create, update, delete and Validate to team admins and the backup controls to `manageBackups`; here `deploy.resource.write` (and the team-wide write check before adding a storage to a schedule is that same check); no instance allowlist of internal targets and never localhost, so the private-target message stops after its first sentence; the endpoint is not pinned to the addresses validated (a Worker cannot pin, nor reach private addresses); "Skip TLS certificate verification" is stored but a Worker cannot turn verification off; an exchange that fails before an answer carries the runtime's error where Coolify shows cURL's, and a timeout reads "Request timed out after 15 seconds…" so that Coolify's 15-seconds message applies; the SDK's retry jitter and its empty `X-Amz-User-Agent` header are left out (the tests sign the SDK's header set to show the signatures agree); the failure email (`unusable_email_sent`) is not sent; the connection is tested outside the database transaction (a read, the test, the write); the API takes `endpoint` as one string, the page composes it from the parts as `DomainUrlParts::compose()`; storages and schedules are named by uuid (`storage_uuid`, `s3StorageUuids`), not id; audit events are not written; the password step of the delete dialogs is left out; volume backup schedules have no page (they are only detached); service databases' schedules are not covered; Import backup is not ported (its button is absent); the executions list has no sorting, paging, detail dialog, download or cleanup actions (there are none to act on); "ago" is computed in the browser; a storage that is not found goes back to the list; the docs link stays Coolify's ("Coolify Docs"), other copy reads "Freeploy". **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted and built; the visual and DOM gates have not seen them.

## Notes on DP8

- **Service templates and the Compose parser (DP-59).** The first task of DP8: Coolify's one-click service catalogue, its Compose reader and writer, and `serviceParser`, in TypeScript and held to Coolify's own PHP; then the rows, the API and the New Resource page's Services section.
