No migration (`deploy.services`, `service_applications`, `service_databases` are in `500_deploy_core`). **YAML** (`apps/deploy-core-worker/src/compose/`): `yaml.ts` reads a file with the `yaml` package (eemeli/yaml 2.9.1, ISC, the one dependency added; syntax only, failsafe schema) and types every plain scalar as Symfony's `Inline::evaluateScalar` does, with Symfony's merge-key, duplicate-key and alias rules, and prints with a port of Symfony's dumper for `Yaml::dump($x, 10, 2)`; `php.ts` and `support.ts` are PHP's value model (`PhpArray`: an ordered map with integer and string keys, `PhpFloat`) and the Laravel helpers the parser's results depend on. Two things Symfony does that YAML does not are reproduced because Coolify's files are what Symfony makes of them: a plain scalar continued after an empty line is an error, and a block scalar that ends its collection near the end of a file with a comment header loses its final line break (Symfony's line count; 8 of the 390 templates). Verified: 21 dump cases and 101 parse cases (scalars, words, dates, quoting, keys, empties, depth past the inline level, anchors, merges, the line-break rule from both sides) against goldens emitted by PHP 8.3.6 with symfony/yaml v8.1.11, and **all 390 files of `templates/compose` round-trip byte-equal** (`Yaml::dump(Yaml::parse($raw), 10, 2)`, compared by sha256). **Catalogue** (`src/services/catalogue-build.ts`, `catalogue.ts`, `scripts/build-service-catalogue.mjs`): a port of `generate:services`; 359 entries, since 31 of the 390 templates are marked `# ignore: true`. Every field of every entry equals what Coolify's generator writes from the templates at 91b7e3e (golden from running that generator; the Compose text by sha256), in the same order, and the JSON text has PHP's `JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES` form. It is **not** byte-equal to Coolify's committed `templates/service-templates-latest.json`: that file is older than its templates (351 entries identical field for field, 4 with a changed Compose file — gotenberg, netbird-client, supabase, trigger — and 4 missing: n8n-with-postgres-worker-and-ai-assistant, netbird, netbird-proxy, wordpress-with-openlitespeed). The catalogue is **bundled into the Worker** as a module holding the JSON text (parsed on first use), not a static asset: deploy-core-worker has no assets binding and a service must be created from a catalogue that cannot change under it; the bundle goes from 519.87 KiB (158.76 KiB gzip) to 2072.80 KiB (530.04 KiB gzip), against Workers' 3 MiB gzip limit on the free plan. **Parser** (`src/services/parser.ts`, `compose-helpers.ts`, `magic-values.ts`, `store.ts`): `serviceParser` with everything it calls — the magic variables (`SERVICE_FQDN_*`, `SERVICE_URL_*` with ports and paths, `SERVICE_PASSWORD*`, `USER`, `BASE64*`, `REALBASE64*`, `HEX_*`, the two Supabase JWTs with an own SHA-256/HMAC), `isDatabaseImage`, volume strings and long volumes, bind mounts and file storages, external and network volumes, networks, ports, `${VAR:-default}` handling with nested defaults, required variables, Coolify's default variables, the service-specific domains of MinIO, Logto and Garage, `ServiceApplication::getRequiredPort`, the labels through DP-53's generators, `env_file`, the cleanup of `content:` from the raw file, and `Create.php`'s creation with `applyServiceApplicationPrerequisites` — as a synchronous function over a `ServiceStore` (rows under Coolify's column names; `MemoryServiceStore` is the implementation, a caller with a database loads and writes back) with the randomness, the uuids and the clock injected. Verified against real PHP **with a database**: Coolify's `testing` connection (SQLite in memory) with all its migrations run, each service created as `Resource/Create.php` creates it, `parse(isNew: true)`, the prerequisites, then `parse()` again. **359 of 359 services match on both parses**: `docker_compose` and `docker_compose_raw` byte for byte and every column of every row (573 applications, 219 databases, 5849 environment variables, 791 volumes, 125 file storages); 34 of them match again on a server with Traefik and on one with Caddy, "generate exact labels" on and a wildcard domain. Random values cannot be seeded in PHP, so each generated value is replayed from the golden into the port's source after its shape is checked; nothing is masked in the comparison, and the Supabase JWTs are computed by the port. The emitters, the goldens (gzipped: the full set is 11 MB) and the exact commands are in `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/{compose-yaml,services}`. Running the comparison also found that the emitter needs the array cache: with Coolify's default Redis cache unreachable `Service::getRequiredPort()` silently answers null. Since DP-56 the deployment job's `Yaml::dump($value, 10)` (`src/deployments/yaml.ts`) runs on this dumper too — it keeps its own value model and hands the values over, with Symfony's default indentation of 4 — and every DP-56 golden still passes byte for byte; one of them found a quoting rule this dumper had wrong (PHP's `/u` patterns match Unicode white space with `\s`, so `a\u2003b` is quoted), now fixed and pinned by a PHP-emitted case. **Rows and API**: `@saas/db/deploy` `services.ts` (`insertParsedService` writes the service, its parts, variables and volumes in creation order; `findServiceByUuid`, `listEnvironmentServices`, the parts, the server's settings), tested on PGlite at the head schema (DP-63's helper; every row carries a uuid, and the unique indexes of the volumes hold by construction) with row-level security; in deploy-core-worker `GET …/deploy/services/templates` (`Select::loadServices`: names, links, logos, flags and categories equal to PHP's for all 359, without the Compose files), `POST …/deploy/services` `{ type: "one-click-service-<name>", project_uuid, environment_uuid, destination_uuid, server_uuid? }` (the parsed rows stored in one transaction, variable values and file contents encrypted under the org's key; a build server or a destination that is not the org's answers 404) and `GET …/deploy/services/{uuid}`, with `deploy.resource.read`/`write`. **Web**: the New Resource page lists the services with Select's search, sort and category filter (`ResourceCreateServices.tsx`), a chosen service is created on Coolify's "Creating …" page and lands on `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/service/{service_uuid}`, a first page of Freeploy's own that lists the service's applications and databases. **Departures**: the list's documentation and website links drop Coolify's `?utm_source=coolify.io` and `?ref=coolify.io` (the catalogue itself keeps them, to stay equal to Coolify's); a card links the template's own documentation, not Coolify's docs site; the list carries no `logo_cdn_url` and a card never loads a logo from Coolify's GitHub repository, as Coolify's page does when a logo is missing — the UI loads nothing from another host; `template_last_updated_at` comes from Coolify's committed catalogue where the Compose text is unchanged and is null for the other 8, because the pinned checkout is a shallow clone and the generator's `git log` has no history to read; the catalogue is not refreshed from Coolify's CDN; paths on the server stay `/data/coolify/services/<uuid>` and labels stay Coolify's, as for applications; sanitising of the image name (`strip_tags`) is not applied before the Pocketbase and service-type checks. **Deferred**: nothing is deployed and nothing reaches a server (no `.env`, no file storage written); the service's configuration pages, its environment variables, storages, logs, terminal and deploy controls, and services in the environment's resource list; a service from a pasted Compose file (`docker-compose-empty`) and Compose applications (`applicationParser`); re-parsing a stored service through the API (the parser does it, no route calls it yet); the `logging:` section of a server with a log drain (the context takes it, nothing builds it); shared variable references in a service's values (the context takes a resolver, the API passes none); parser versions 2 and earlier; the service logos (9.4 MB in Coolify): a card shows Coolify's placeholder image (`svgs/default.webp`, vendored) for a logo Freeploy does not ship, which is every service logo for now; the DP8 "10 templates deploy on a test VM" criterion.
- **Service configuration (DP-66).** The pages Coolify renders for a one-click service (`project.service.configuration` and its shared components with `:resource="$service"`), without SSH and without a migration (every column is in the head schema). **Editing the Compose file, held to PHP.** Saving is `StackForm::submit()` on the stored rows (`src/services/stack-form.ts`): Coolify's rules and messages (the Docker Compose Raw / Name / description rules, each template field's own rules with its label as attribute, the locked fields skipped), `validateDockerComposeForInjection()` ported whole (`compose-validation.ts`: services, volume strings and long volumes, service and top-level networks with `${VAR:-default}`, external volumes; the network checks are new to Freeploy, the rest reuses DP-59's helpers), the fields written with `saveExtraFields()` (the value mutator and the saving hook: edge line breaks, `preserve_compose_null`, `is_shared`), then DP-59's `parseService()` again on the rows already stored; `rows.ts` loads the service's parts, variables (decrypted), volumes and file mounts into the parser's `MemoryServiceStore` and writes back only what changed — a loaded row updated by id, a variable or file mount the parser removed deleted, a new part inserted before the volumes that refer to it — so parts are reconciled by name (a removed one marked `is_removed_from_compose`), never duplicated. `extra-fields.ts` is `Service::extraFields()` for all 29 image cases, the default case and the PostgreSQL/MySQL/MariaDB database cases, with its quirks (the first true `case` wins; kong has no `break` and falls into MinIO's; Vaultwarden's fields are a Collection, so a `$SERVICE_…` value is never resolved there; a field whose variable is missing has a null key and value) and `StackForm::mount()`'s grouping, ordering and locking. **Goldens**: `fixtures/services/emit-reparse.php` boots Coolify as DP-59's emitter does, signs the team owner in, creates each template's service and saves an edited file through `Livewire::test(StackForm::class)->call('saveCompose', $raw)`, recording the toasts; `gen-reparse-cases.mjs` writes `reparse-cases.json`: **32 edits of 32 templates** (5 images changed, 2 of them to a database image; 4 services added with a SERVICE_URL_ variable, a generated password, a `${VAR:-default}`, a named volume and a bind-mounted file; 4 services removed; 5 SERVICE_URL_/FQDN_ ports changed; 5 times variables added; 5 times volumes added; 2 combined edits), on templates whose Compose file Freeploy ships unchanged and that have no `content:` volume. PHP saved 25 and refused 7 (a required template field whose variable does not exist — Vaultwarden's SIGNUP_ALLOWED, several "Database Name" fields): `service-reparse.test.ts` creates each service with the port, loads the same fields, saves the same text and gets the same toast, and for all 32 every column of every row and both Compose files equal PHP's byte for byte (random values replayed as in DP-59; `services.config_hash` left out). DP-59's 359-template parser goldens pass unchanged; their row helpers moved to `src/service-golden-rows.ts`. **API** (`handlers/service-configuration.ts`, dispatched by `handleServices` for a known page; the services route now five segments deep; inside `withOrg`, `deploy.resource.read`/`write`, 404 on denial, reads carrying `canUpdate`, `canManage`, `canDelete`): `GET`/`PATCH …/services/{uuid}/configuration` (the frame — status aggregated from the parts' stored statuses as `Service::containerStatus()` and ContainerStatusAggregator do, `isDeployable`, the template's documentation — and General; PATCH takes `name`, `description`, `fields` by variable key and, from the Compose dialog, `dockerComposeRaw`), `PATCH …/configuration/network` (instantSave, "Service settings saved."), `PATCH …/configuration/label-escape` (EditCompose::instantSave, "Service updated successfully"), `POST …/actions/{start|force-deploy|stop|restart|pull-restart}` (501), `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE …/parts/{part_uuid}` (Index: `action` submit, settings, exclude, logDrain, maxRestartCount, publicAccess, restart), `…/environment-variables[/…]` (DP-51's handler with the type `service`: `App\Models\Service`, its Compose file as written, its `server_id` for the `server` scope, and — as for a database — the default variables and log redaction switches on its own row), `GET …/storages` and `PATCH`/`DELETE …/storages/{part_uuid}/{volumes|files}/{id}`, `GET`/`POST …/tags`, `POST`/`DELETE …/tags/{id}`, `GET`/`DELETE …/danger`. The environment's resources list (`…/projects/{uuid}/environments/{env_uuid}/resources`) now ends with the services, sorted by name, with their status, server status and tags. SQL in `@saas/db/deploy` `service-configuration.ts` (the service with its server and destination, the parser's rows, writes limited per table to the columns that may be written and confined to the service's own rows, a part deleted with its volumes, file mounts, backup schedules and tags, `deleteService()` with every child Coolify's `DeleteService::deleteLocal()` and `DeleteResourceJob` remove — the parts' volume backup schedules with executions and S3 pivots, each part's volumes and file mounts, a database part's backup schedules with executions, S3 pivots and replicas, the parts' tags and stars, the scheduled tasks with executions, the variables, the service's tags, stars, secret manager links and image scan rows —, the org's domain owners, the environment's services with their parts' statuses). **Pages**: General (`ServiceShow.tsx`: Service details with "Edit Compose file" — the source and "Preview generated Compose" with locked values replaced by `<locked>`, "Escape special characters in labels", "Save changes" —, the network listbox, Service configuration's fields, Compose resources with each part's card), each part's settings (`ServicePart.tsx`), and `ServiceSharedPages.tsx`: Environment Variables, Tags and the Danger Zone are the applications' components with a `kind` "service" (`ResourceKind`, `ServiceSectionData`, `ResourceWorkspace`), Persistent Storage is one section per part on DP-55's volume and file components; `ServiceChrome.tsx` is the heading and the sidebar with every entry of Coolify's (Domains, Backups, Runtime Logs, Terminal, Files, Analytics, Scheduled Tasks, Webhooks, Resource Operations, Maintenance Mode and Security link to their Coolify route and land on the not-ported notice). **What needs the SSH executor:** a save stores the rows and reports "Writing the Compose file and its .env file to the server needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." (Coolify's `saveComposeConfigs()` runs after the commit); Deploy, Force Deploy, Force Cleanup, Restart, Restart (pull latest) and Stop, and a part's restart, answer 501; a database part's public access is stored and reports that stopping its proxy is not available yet (making it public needs the database running, as in Coolify, so it is refused first); deleting a part or the service answers 501 "…needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." once any part has left `exited` (or has run), and otherwise removes the rows. **Tests:** `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/service-reparse.test.ts` (34), `service-configuration.test.ts` (28: every route, the save through `rows.ts` adding and marking parts, the refusals, the part pages' domain rules and conflicts, public access, storage read-only, tags, danger, the variables' type, authorization, routing through `handleServices` and `route()`, the status aggregation, the redaction, the summary, the network checks, the resources list), `tests/db/src/deploy-service-configuration.test.ts` on the head schema (9, row-level security included), `apps/deploy-web/test/routes.test.ts` (the service routes); tests/deploy-core-worker went to 4158 tests (2 skipped, as before) and tests/db to 1081. **Departures:** a validation error answers 422 with ValidationException's summary as its message ("The Signup Allowed field is required. (and 1 more error)", what Coolify's toast shows) and the messages per field in `details`; the `email` and `url` rules of template fields are approximations (one "@" with text around it; `servers/validation.ts`'s `isUrl`); `ensureDockerComposeAccepted()` is never run (Coolify skips it when the server is not functional and on any SSH failure; Freeploy has no SSH); `config_hash` and `isConfigurationChanged()` are not computed (Coolify's `finally` writes it from timestamps); `isPasswordHiddenForMember` and `@can('manageEnvironment')` are `deploy.resource.write`; the generated file's redaction covers locked variables of the service, not locked shared variables its Compose file references; `isDeployable` reads a required variable's stored value (a shared variable reference counts as a value); a part's settings are a page of its own (`/service/{uuid}/{stack_service_uuid}`, Coolify's `project.service.index`, which redirects to a dialog on the configuration page), and an application part's domains are edited there with `submitApplication()`'s rules (`applications/domains.ts`'s validation and normalisation, ports into `domain_port_overrides`, the sslip and multiple-domains warnings, conflicts against the org's applications and service applications, confirmed with `forceSaveDomains`) where Coolify edits them on the service's Domains page; the documentation link is the template's own without `?utm_source=coolify.io`, none without a template; the Compose editor is a plain textarea (no Monaco, no "Use plain-text editor" switch), and the Details dialog (resource-details) is not rendered; a service's logo in the resources list is Coolify's placeholder, as on DP-59's cards; "Coolify" in the copy reads "Freeploy" ("Connect to the predefined Freeploy network"). **Not in this task (cut or deferred):** Resource Operations (cloning and moving a service: the first cut of this task), the service's Domains page, a part's port-requirement confirmation (`getRequiredPort()` / `portRequiresConfirmation()`), the DNS record queue of a domain change, re-parsing after a part's save (Coolify's `updateCompose()` and `generateDockerCompose`; the next General save parses), converting a part between application and database, a database part's public URL, the Compose-specific parts of the Environment Variables page (variables hard-coded in the Compose file, the service filter, "Used by Compose"), the external volumes list of Storage, the secret manager link, the account password step of the confirmations, the audit trail, "Validate" in the Compose dialog (shown only for a service without a template, which Freeploy cannot create). **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted and built.
- **The remaining resource pages, part one (DP-97).** deploy-web gains the resource console pages whose data and services already existed, ported as DP-5x/DP-6x's were; no binding, dependency or migration. **Service**: Scheduled Tasks (`…/service/{uuid}/scheduled-tasks`, project.service.scheduled-tasks.show) is ScheduledTask\All, Add and Show with `:resource="$service"` — `applications/scheduled-tasks.ts` now runs on a `TaskHost` (an application's or a service's task rows), its application functions unchanged; the containers are the service's applications' then databases' names (Add's listbox, the first chosen), the timezone is the server's setting, and an empty container is refused with the toast Livewire gives for Add's undeclared `$subServiceName` ("Property [$subServiceName] not found on component: [project.shared.scheduled-task.add]"); Execute now answers 501. Webhooks (`…/webhooks`) is the deploy webhook (`/api/v1/deploy?uuid=…&force=false`). Resource Operations (`…/resource-operations`) is the shared page with kind "service": where it can go, Move (`moveTo()`, DP-86's `moveService()`, "Environment not found."), and Clone, which answers cloneTo()'s own checks ("Environment not found.", "Destination not found." for a missing, foreign or build server's destination) and then 501 "Cloning a service is not available yet." with nothing stored — clone_service() replicates the service and runs Service::parse() on the copy, and Freeploy's parser is held to PHP for a created or saved service, not a replicated one, so the clone could not be shown to be Coolify's (`services/clone.ts`). A part's `…/{stack_service_uuid}/advanced` (project.service.index.advanced) is DP-66's part page, whose form holds advanced-settings.blade.php. `…/service/{uuid}/tasks/{task_uuid}` and `…/application/{uuid}/tasks/{task_uuid}` redirect to `…/scheduled-tasks?task_uuid=…`, as routes/web.php's redirect with get_route_parameters() does, and the list opens that task (All's #[Url]). **Database**: Healthcheck (`…/database/{uuid}/healthcheck`) is Database\Health — the five columns (`findDatabaseHealth`/`updateDatabaseHealth`, every engine), #[Validate]'s boolean / integer min:1 / min:0 with Laravel's messages and the property as the attribute ("The health check interval field must be at least 1."), shown as handleError()'s toast of the ValidationException; submit's "Healthcheck updated. Restart the database to apply the changes." and toggleHealthcheck()'s "Healthcheck enabled|disabled. …", which also saves the form's values; config_hash is not kept, as on the other database pages. Servers (`…/servers`) is destination.blade.php's branch for what is not an application: the primary server card, read from the database's own GET. **Tags** (`/tags/{tagName?}`, tags.show): Tags\Show and CreatesTags — the org's tags with their applications' and services' counts (soft-deleted resources left out), sorted by name, the list's search, sorts, grid and table and pages; a tag's deploy webhook (`/api/v1/deploy?tag=…`), its applications and services with their links, its queued and running deployments (polled, sortable as setDeploymentSort()/strcasecmp()); New tag with createTag()'s rules and messages (an empty name fails `required` alone, "A tag name cannot contain spaces.", the sanitized length, "A tag with this name already exists."); Delete (the tag and its links); Redeploy all toasts "Mass deployment started." for a tag without resources and otherwise answers 501. **API** (inside `withOrg`, `deploy.resource.read`/`write`, 404 on denial): `…/services/{uuid}/webhooks`, `…/scheduled-tasks[/{task}[/toggle|/execute]]`, `…/resource-operations[/move|/clone]` (handleServiceConfiguration), `GET`/`PATCH …/databases/{uuid}/healthcheck` and `POST …/healthcheck/toggle` (handleDatabaseResources, `databases/health.ts`), and `…/deploy/tags[/{name}[/redeploy]]` (`handlers/tag-pages.ts`, a new route of deploy-core-worker's router, each segment decoded; SQL in `@saas/db/deploy` `tag-pages.ts`). **Tests**: tests/deploy-core-worker `service-configuration.test.ts` (+9), `database-resources.test.ts` (+4), `tag-pages.test.ts` (9); tests/db `deploy-database-resources.test.ts` (+8, every engine) and `deploy-tag-pages.test.ts` (3); deploy-web `routes.test.ts` (+2) and `resource-pages.test.ts` (4). **Departures**: the health page also shows the field errors inline; the Servers card shows every status as a badge (Coolify's running/restarting/stopped components otherwise); the Tags list remembers grid or table in the browser (Coolify on the user), and the canvas view it may redirect to is not ported. **Not in this task (cut)**: the service's Domains page (Service\Domains, 1839 lines with its DNS providers), the volume backups of an application (`…/backups[/{backup_uuid}[/executions|/danger]]`) and of a service (`…/storage-backups[…]`, VolumeBackups and the service's backups index that also lists its databases' schedules), a service database's `…/{stack_service_uuid}/backups[…]` (all of which Coolify redirects to that index), an application's `…/preview-deployments` (Previews: GitHub's pull requests, per-preview domains and Docker tags, and actions that run on the server), and on the Tags page "Connect to resources" and "Connect in canvas". **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted, tested and built.
- **The remaining resource pages, part two (DP-99).** Two of the pages DP-97 cut, now that their data exists; no binding, dependency or migration. **Storage backups of an application** (`…/application/{uuid}/backups`, project.application.backup.index; `…/backups/{backup_uuid}[/executions|/danger]`, .show/.executions/.danger; `…/{s3|retention}` redirect to the schedule as routes/web.php's Route::redirect()s do): Backup\Index (the schedules with their target, type, schedule, status, S3 and last run, the counts, the Alpine list's search, type filter and sorts — `lib/volume-backups.ts`), Create (an application's volumes by name, then its directory mounts that are not host files by path; without the archive checkboxes the rows go, "Storage backup schedule deleted."). **Of a service** (`…/service/{uuid}/storage-backups`, project.service.volume-backups.index): VolumeBackup\Index and Create (each part's volumes, named after `{service_uuid}_`, and directories with "(directory)", typed by the part's headline name; "…owned by this service."), a schedule opened in Index's modal (openSchedule() takes update; ?backup_uuid=), its databases' schedules listed (ScheduledDatabaseBackup of the service's databases) with Back up now's "The database must be running to start a backup."; `…/storage-backups/{backup_uuid}[/…]` redirects to the list as Show::mount() does, `…/import-backup[/{stack_service_uuid}]` and `…/{stack_service_uuid}/import` as routes/web.php does, and `…/{stack_service_uuid}/backups[/{backup_uuid}[/…]]` as DatabaseBackups::mount() does (no such database: the service; isBackupSolutionAvailable() false and not migrated: the part; else the storage backups). **Preview Deployments** (`…/application/{uuid}/preview-deployments`, project.application.preview-deployments): Previews — the preview settings of a Git application (togglePreviewDeployments, savePreviewSettings, "Settings saved."), load_prs() as Coolify asks GitHub (githubApi() of `/repos/{slug}/pulls` with DP-72's client and injectable fetch, the public source without a token, sorted by number, the remaining rate limit; its refusal is githubApi()'s message; "Loading pull requests is only supported for GitHub App sources."), Configure (add(): validatePreviewInput()'s `url:http,https|max:2048` and dockerImageTagRules(), the row created as add() creates it, generate_preview_fqdn(generateWithoutApplicationDomain, keepExistingDomains) — generateUrl() of the server for an application without a domain —, "Preview added."), the manual Docker image preview (its validateOnly() messages, "Manual Docker Image previews are only available for Docker Image applications.", "Both pull request id and docker tag are required."), a preview's Docker tag (save_preview(), "Preview saved." and the redeploy reminder), Delete ("Preview not found.", soft-deleted with "Preview deletion started. It may take a few moments to complete." on a server that is not functional, as DP-86's API); Preview\Form (`required`, the spaces removed, "Preview url template updated.", Reset, the generated pattern); PreviewDomains' rows (internal port and DNS status) with Generate ("Domain generated.") and Remove ("Domain removed.", the DNS statuses of the rows left kept). **501, nothing stored** (the console's endpoints run in one withOrg transaction): Back up now ("Running the storage backup …"), a service database's Back up now, a recovery, downloading an archive, deleting a local archive (of a schedule or an execution), add_and_deploy and the manual preview (checked, then "Deploying the preview …" before the row), Deploy/Redeploy (with or without cache), Stop, and deleting a preview of a functional server. **API** (inside `withOrg`, `deploy.resource.read`/`write`, 404 on denial): `…/applications/{uuid}/backups[/{backup}[/run|/executions/…]]` and `…/services/{uuid}/storage-backups[/…]`, `…/services/{uuid}/database-backups/{stack_service_uuid}` (`handlers/volume-backup-pages.ts`, SQL in `@saas/db/deploy` `volume-backup-pages.ts`, whose busy/archives/delete queries DP-88's /api/v1 DELETE …/backups now shares), and `…/applications/{uuid}/previews[/…]` (`handlers/application-previews.ts`, SQL in `preview-pages.ts` beside DP-72/DP-86's preview functions). **Tests**: tests/deploy-core-worker `volume-backup-pages.test.ts` (16) and `application-previews.test.ts` (11), over the API fixture in PGlite with row-level security; tests/db `deploy-volume-backup-pages.test.ts` (5); deploy-web `volume-backups.test.ts` (4) and three expectations of `routes.test.ts` that named these pages as not ported. **Departures**: a schedule's sections are tabs inside the application's frame (Coolify swaps the main sidebar for the backup menu);
