the service's Show redirect checks the schedule through the list; a preview's Docker tag is saved on Enter (Coolify on change); the restart-limit warning is a badge. **Not in this task (cut)**: a service's Domains page (Service\Domains, 1839 lines; not attempted for time — its core fields need DP-52/DP-74's rules per service application and its DNS-provider parts entangle the same component); on the service backups page "New database backup" (CreateScheduledBackup with a service), "Import backup" and BackupExecutions (the service's executions across schedules); PreviewDomains' Add and Edit (the unknown-port confirmation and the DNS check job of Coolify's console component — not the API's update, whose conflict checks the console does not apply — so DP-86's update would not be exact), and a Docker Compose application's previews (add and the domains answer 501: generate_preview_fqdn_compose() needs the application Compose parser, as DP-86's API says); "Back up now" stays 501 until the SSH executor (W3). **Not checked in a browser or on stage**: the pages are type-checked, linted, tested and built.
- **A service's Domains (DP-102).** `…/service/{uuid}/domains` (project.service.domains, `ServiceConfiguration` with `<livewire:project.service.domains>`) is ported to `pages/ServiceDomains.tsx` (its groups and search in `lib/config-subpages.ts`) over `GET`/`PATCH /v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/services/{uuid}/domains` (`handlers/config-subpages.ts`, `services/domains-page.ts`; withOrg, deploy.resource.read / write, a denial a 404 that runs nothing); no migration (`service_applications.domain_dns_statuses`, `redirect`, `noindex_domains`, `domain_port_overrides` and the redirect switches are in the head schema; `packages/db/src/deploy/config-subpages.ts` reads and writes the DNS statuses). *Ported*: the domains of every application of the service grouped by application (sorted by name, the failed DNS checks first), each group's Redirect HTTP to HTTPS (shown with an https domain) and Redirect type with Coolify's helpers and "HTTP to HTTPS redirect updated." / "Redirect type updated."; Add domain — the Service application listbox ("name (image)"), the domain input, Generate domain (filled from the server's wildcard domain or sslip.io with the application's required port, nothing stored, "Service application or server not found.") — with addDomain()'s order: `ValidationPatterns::applicationDomainRules()`, "Select a service application.", "Please enter a valid domain URL.", "Domain {url} is already configured for this service.", the www/non-www counterpart added when the application redirects; the Domain settings dialog (the read-only Service application with "Domains stay on the service they were added to. Remove and re-add to move.", address, Search engine indexing, www redirect, Regenerate hostname) with updateDomain()'s port, override, counterpart and noindex handling and "Please enter a single valid domain URL."; Remove domain with the dialog's copy; setServiceRedirect() with ensureWwwNonWwwPairsConfigured() and assertRedirectDomainsPresent()'s message (its `<br><br>` as line breaks, as the application's page); the domain conflict dialog (409, Proceed anyway repeats with forceSaveDomains) and the port requirement dialog (`portRequiresConfirmation()` against `getRequiredPort()`, now exported from the parser as `serviceApplicationRequiredPort()`; "Use port {n} anyway" repeats with forceRemovePort); every save as saveDomainListForApp() saves — the first `validateApplicationDomains()` error a toast, ServiceApplication's saving hook, `updateCompose()` and `Service::parse()` on the rows in memory, written back by `writeServiceRows()` — with the sslip and "Some services do not support multiple domains…" warnings; the toasts in Coolify's order. *Departures*: a check Coolify queues (CheckDomainDnsJob) is stored as the application page stores it — "DNS check could not be started." with "Some DNS checks could not be started. Try again from the Domains page." / "The DNS check could not be started. Try again from the Domains page." — an sslip.io host accepted, a service without a server skipped; the actions name an application by its uuid, not its row id (the validation attributes keep the id: "The selected service redirects.{id} is invalid."); an update or removal of a domain the application does not have answers 404 "Domain not found." where Coolify returns silently; the port dialog's 409 carries its own text; "Inherited from the Coolify service port" reads "Freeploy service port". *Cut*: managed DNS (Cloudflare Domain Connect, DNS providers, the manual records dialog, the remove dialog's "Also delete the DNS record created by Coolify" checkbox), the favicons, buildSuggestedWwwRows() / addSuggestedDomain() (buildDomainRows() never adds suggested rows), toggleNoindexDomain() (the page sets indexing in the edit dialog), the DNS-failure confirmation of add/edit (findDnsFailureMessage() needs the DNS check), polling, and the audit log entries (`ui.service_application.updated`).

## Notes on DP9

- **Notification channels (DP-67).** Coolify's six channels — email, Discord, Telegram, Slack (Mattermost too), Pushover, webhook — configured per org, their test sends delivered, and every notification class's message ported and held to Coolify's own PHP. No migration: the six `deploy.*_notification_settings` tables are in the head schema (500/520/660: one row per org, `failure_count`). **Settings** (`packages/db/src/deploy/notification-settings.ts`): the row is created on the first read with the column defaults (Coolify creates it with the team; `use_instance_email_settings` false), updates write only the changed columns and reset `failure_count` (TracksDeliveryFailures' saving hook), plus the failure counter, its reset and turning a channel off; the `encrypted` casts (webhook URLs, tokens, chat and topic ids, SMTP and Resend secrets) are fp1 ciphertexts of the org's data key (`handlers/notifications.ts::sqlNotificationStore`); tests/db on the head schema, row-level security included. **API** `/v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/notifications`: `GET` (which channels are on), `GET|PATCH /{channel}` (`{ action, …properties }` with Livewire's property names: chat channels `submit`, `instantSave`, `instantSaveEnabled` — what the Enable/Disable button calls —, `toggleEnabled`, `instantSavePingEnabled`, `toggleEvent { property }` with TogglesNotificationEvents' 404; email `submit`, `selectEmailProvider` — an incomplete provider saves nothing and shows "Not active yet" —, `instantSave { type }`, `instantSaveDeploymentCommitDetails`, `toggleEvent`), `POST /{channel}/test`; Coolify's NotificationPolicy (view a member, update and sendTest an admin) is deploy.resource.read / write with 404 on denial, and the secrets are null for who cannot update ("Hidden (only admins can view)"). Every rule and message is Coolify's word for word (`notifications/settings.ts`: the #[Validate] rules with Laravel's English messages and `:attribute` as Laravel derives it — "The discord webhook url field is required when discord enabled is true." —, the custom required messages, validateSmtpSettings/validateResendSettings with SmtpPresets::requiresLogin and ValidHostname). Coolify at 91b7e3e has **no** "at least one channel" rule and no "test before enable" rule — a channel enables once its required fields are filled — and neither is added. SafeWebhookUrl is ported without DNS (`notifications/safe-webhook-url.ts`): syntax, scheme, trailing dot, localhost/.local/.internal and literal private, reserved and link-local addresses are refused with Coolify's messages; a host name is not resolved (no DNS in a Worker; a Worker's fetch has no route into an operator's private network) and there is no internal-host allowlist. **Payloads** (`notifications/payloads*.ts`, `messages.ts`, `php.ts`): a pure port of `toMail`/`toDiscord`/`toTelegram`/`toSlack`/`toPushover`/`toWebhook` of all 34 classes a team's channels send, from plain data, with PHP's `json_encode` (default flags), `trim`, `mb_*`, `htmlspecialchars`, `e()`, `data_get` on buttons and Coolify's own quirks kept (ContainerRestarted's list-in-a-list Telegram button that becomes "Click here" with no URL, HighDiskUsage's label-keyed Pushover buttons, TaskSuccess/TaskFailed building a Telegram button they never return, VulnerabilityDigest's and ExploitedProblemFound's channel limits and "And N more."); a `PayloadContext` carries what differs: the product's name where Coolify writes "Coolify" (Freeploy's DP-F brand rule), Discord's footer ("Freeploy"; Coolify's is "Coolify v4.7.6"), the console URL (`DEPLOY_CONSOLE_URL`, unset in the wrangler files this task may not touch, so `https://app.freeploy.dev`), and ForceDisabled's subscription link. **Goldens from real PHP** (`tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/notifications/`, `emit.php` on Coolify 4.7.6 / Laravel 12.72.0 with SQLite in memory): 83 cases over the 34 classes — 81 email subjects and views (GeneralNotification has no email), the test email's rendered HTML twice, 83 payloads per chat channel, the jobs' HTTP requests captured through `Bus::fake()` + `Http::fake()` (83 each for Discord, Telegram with its thread id, Slack, Mattermost, Pushover and webhook, plus Telegram's second request without buttons) and 5 Resend requests of the test email through a local capture of the Resend API — all replayed byte for byte with the brand "Coolify" in the context, **no mismatch** (`src/notification-payloads.test.ts`); no field is masked (the clock, URLs and tokens are fixed). **Delivery** (`notifications/requests.ts`, `delivery.ts`): the jobs' requests exactly (`User-Agent: GuzzleHttp/7`, Telegram's trimmed token and JSON-in-JSON `reply_markup`, the Slack-or-Mattermost switch on `hooks.slack.com`), HandlesNotificationResponse (2xx resets `failure_count`, 429/5xx/network retried — 5 tries 10 s apart, an injectable sleep —, each job's configuration errors counted and not retried, other 4xx only logged, reasons without the body) and TracksDeliveryFailures (3 in a row turn the channel off and the org's other channels get the GeneralNotification); `notifyOrg(env, org, notification)` reads the enabled channels and toggles (getEnabledChannels, the always-send events, no email for "general") and runs each database step in its own short transaction — **wired to nothing that fires today**: deployments, backups and server checks call it once the executor runs them (W3); only "Send test" sends. **Send test**: Discord, Slack, Pushover and webhook are sent once inside the request (Coolify queues the Test notification) and answer "Test notification sent." as Coolify does, with what the endpoint answered beside it; Telegram is synchronous as Coolify's is ("Telegram rejected the test notification." and its reason); email with the team's Resend settings sends Coolify's request to api.resend.com (`mail_from_formatted`, `resend-php/1.18.0`) with EmailChannel's error messages and the 10-second rate limit (per Worker isolate). **Not available yet**: SMTP — a Worker cannot speak SMTP without a socket; it needs Cloudflare's `connect()` TCP sockets with STARTTLS (or TLS on 465) or Email Workers — answers 501 saying so; the hosted email service (`use_instance_email_settings`) answers 501 (no sending domain, W5) — a departure from design §4.3, which sends email through notifications-worker: a team's own provider is sent from deploy-core-worker as Coolify's EmailChannel sends it, and only the hosted service would be notifications-worker's; `notifyOrg` skips email for every class but the test. **Pages** (`apps/deploy-web/src/pages/NotificationSettings.tsx`): the six pages hand-ported from the Blade views — settings-layout, channel-actions, the event grid with its multiselects and Telegram's forum topics, Discord's mention listbox, the email page's service listbox (the hosted wording: Freeploy is isCloud), provider list, "Not active yet", Resend's fields, the SMTP sender/connection/authentication/advanced groups and the test-email dialog — with Coolify's copy where "Coolify" reads "Freeploy"; the sidebar's Notifications submenu was already navbar.blade.php's; the layout's "No notifications enabled" reminder now shows only when `GET …/notifications` says no channel is on (Coolify's `isAnyNotificationEnabled()` without its isCloud() short cut). *Cut*: the rendered email body of every class but the test email (Blade + CommonMark; the email channel's HTML goldens, the first item of the task's cut order) — the port has their subjects and views. *Deferred*: the named SMTP servers (ManagesSmtpServers), SafeSmtpHost's DNS check, toggleSmtp/toggleResend (not called by the page), the throttles (NotificationThrottle, ThrottledNotification) and Reachable/Unreachable's email rate limit, the admins' email and the root team's internal notification when a channel is turned off, the audit log lines, the transactional emails, Telegram's per-class threads for test sends (Coolify's test has none either), and the stage DOM gate of the six pages.

- **API tokens and /api/v1 (DP-70).** Coolify's Keys & Tokens → API Tokens and the part of Coolify's REST API whose resources Freeploy has. *Tokens*: Sanctum's personal access tokens as identity API keys — migration 720 adds `api_keys.abilities` (Coolify's abilities; null for every other key) and `api_keys.token_number` (Sanctum's id, from its own sequence), because the keys could carry an org and an expiry but not abilities or the number clients send. A token belongs to the user who made it (`created_by`, Sanctum's tokenable; a service principal row is made only because `api_keys` requires one, with no membership binding) and to one org. `/v1/organizations/{org}/api-tokens` (identity-worker, forwarded by the org facade) lists the user's tokens newest first, creates one with `validateTokenForm()`'s rules and messages word for word (description required|min:3|max:255, the five expiry choices or Never, abilities from Coolify's list, the ValidationException summary "(and N more errors)", write:sensitive only for owners and admins, "A token cannot have more access than you have…" — a Freeploy owner or admin is Coolify's owner/admin, any other org-wide role Coolify's member with its default Read grant; DP-69's builder losing `deploy.resource.write` agrees), and revokes one (Coolify deletes it; Freeploy marks it revoked). The plain text is Sanctum's (40 random letters and digits and their crc32b), shown once as `<id>|<plain text>`, stored as the SHA-256 of what follows `|`. `GET /v1/auth/resolve` refuses an API token: it authenticates nothing but /api/v1, as its user. *The edge*: `https://<edge>/api/*` (api-edge `src/api-v1/`) in the order Coolify's kernel runs it — ThrottleFailedApiRequests (API_FAILED_REQUEST_RATE_LIMIT, default 30: 401, 403 and 404 without a token, per token hash or IP; blocks of 60, 300 and 900 seconds within an hour; health exempt), routes/api.php's routes or its fallback `{"message":"Not found.","docs":…}`, auth:sanctum before throttle:api (Laravel's middleware priority: a refused request is not counted and its 401 has no rate-limit headers), throttle:api (API_RATE_LIMIT, default 200 a minute per user, or per IP without a valid token; 1000 for /api/health; X-RateLimit-Limit/-Remaining, and Retry-After and X-RateLimit-Reset on the pretty-printed "Too Many Attempts." 429), api.token.team (identity-worker's `POST /v1/internal/api-tokens/resolve`: the `<id>|` form and the bare secret, expiry, a revoked token, a deleted user, "Invalid token." for a user no longer in the org, the "more access than you have" 403), ApiAllowed (API_ENABLED="false" → "API is disabled.", API_ALLOWED_IPS → "You are not allowed to access the API." with checkIPAgainstAllowlist's CIDR, IPv6 and 0.0.0.0 rules), api.sensitive (root or read:sensitive and an owner/admin) and api.ability ("Missing required permissions: …", root passes). The limiters are pure steps, one RateLimiterDO instance per key (the existing binding, two new message kinds), the in-isolate map without it. The edge forwards to identity-worker (teams) or deploy-core-worker (`/v1/internal/api-v1/{org}/…`, reachable only over the binding) as the token's user with its abilities; deploy-core still asks policy-worker (`deploy.resource.read`/`write`), so a token never does more than its user, and a denial is Laravel's 403 "This action is unauthorized.". Every body is Coolify's bytes: `@saas/contracts/coolify-api` has PHP's json_encode (`\/`, `\uXXXX`), serializeApiResponse()'s key order and Coolify's fixed answers; deploy-core reads whole rows (`apiRows()`, `row_to_json` inside withOrg) and gives them Eloquent's toArray() ($hidden, date casts, appends, team_id). *Ported, 24 of openapi.json's 330 operations*: health, version; teams — GET /teams, /teams/{id}, /teams/{id}/members, /team, /team/members (and the deprecated /teams/current aliases); projects — list, create, show, update, delete, environments list, create, update, delete; private keys — list, create (base64 too), show, update, delete; servers — list, show, validate. The other 306 answer Coolify's 404 shape: *cut* (the task's order, from its end) applications, databases, services, deployments, resources, S3 storages, cloud tokens and scheduled tasks; *not ported in the groups done* GET /projects/{uuid}/{environment} (it serializes every application, database and service), the shared-variable `envs` routes, servers create, update, delete, resources, domains and `?resources` (resources need Service::status and domains the FQDN parsing of the cut groups), PATCH /team, everything else of openapi.json. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` — Coolify's real PHP (4.7.6) on PostgreSQL 16 through its HTTP kernel, 100 cases (3 sequences) — replayed byte for byte with a documented masking (dates, request-made uuids, team and member ids, the settings row id, the Sentinel token): 64 operation cases (4 of them the not-ported and departure answers) against deploy-core-worker on memory stores made from `freeploy-rows.json` (the fixture's rows from Freeploy's real schema, held by tests/db), 25 middleware, limiter, health and version cases (and status and headers of the 75 forwarded ones) against api-edge, 9 team cases against identity-worker; `tests/identity-worker/fixtures/api-tokens` — the Livewire page through Livewire's test harness, 25 cases (every create rule and message, the mount flags, revoke). phpJsonEncode writes every golden body back unchanged. *Departures*: a team's and a member's `id` and `team_id`/`current_team_id` are Freeploy's public ids (`org_…`, `usr_…`), not integers, and `current_team_id` is the token's org (Freeploy keeps no current team per user); a team's columns Freeploy's orgs do not have answer Coolify's defaults for a new team, and its description is null (membership-worker's organization answer does not carry DP-69's description); a member's avatar columns are null (the avatar is not on a storage disk); `POST /servers/{uuid}/validate` answers 501 "Validating the server needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." in Coolify's envelope where Coolify answers 201; a server created by Freeploy has no Sentinel token where Coolify generates one; `isCloud()` is not Freeploy's: Coolify's instance settings `is_api_enabled` and `allowed_ips` are the edge's deploy-time variables API_ENABLED and API_ALLOWED_IPS (defaults: on, every address); messages keep their `coolify.io/docs` links. *Deferred*: tokens limited to selected projects, environments or resources (refused with a Freeploy message; the page's option is disabled), a token's Edit, Pause and Roll, expiry warnings, ApiTokenFailingNotification, the audit log lines, the page's "API access is turned off" callout and client pagination, and the stage gate of the page and of the API contract run with the limits on (`api.rate-limit`, `api.failed-request-throttle`, DP-4's scenarios, are the two limiter sequences here).
- **/api/v1 for the deploy resources (DP-71).** Coolify's API for an org's applications and their environment variables, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains the routes with routes/api.php's abilities (`read:sensitive` for the logs, `deploy` for start, restart, stop and their GET `post_required` answer "This endpoint has changed to a POST request." 405), and deploy-core-worker's internal mount answers them in `src/api-v1/applications.ts` on the console's services and SQL stores in one org transaction (a new `sql` runner of the handler; `context.ts`): an application is deleted by the Danger Zone's `deleteApplicationNow()` with Coolify's query options, a variable is created and saved by the Environment Variables page's `createVariable()` and `saveVariable()` (exported, with `environmentVariableContext()` and `applicationResourcesContext()` split out of their runners), so the preview copy, the permanent lock and the NUL check are the model's hooks as before. The bodies are ApplicationsController's: `removeSensitiveData()` — the application's columns without `$hidden` (made visible and decrypted for a token that may read secrets, `private_key_id` hidden from the others), the accessors toArray() reads through (`status` as "exited:unhealthy", `custom_network_aliases`, `custom_nginx_configuration`), the global scope's counts, `server_status` and the relations it loads — `destination.server.settings` and `additional_servers` written as Eloquent writes a loaded relation, in Coolify's column order (`COOLIFY_COLUMNS`, `src/api-v1/columns.ts`, held to the order emit.php reads from Coolify's schema), `settings` for the show — then serializeApiResponse(); a variable's columns and appends (`is_shared`, `is_really_required`, `is_buildpack_control`, `is_coolify`; `value` and `real_value` only for a token that may read secrets and never for a locked one), a variable that create_bulk_envs has just made with only the attributes Eloquent had set. Validation is customApiValidator's ("This field is required.", Laravel's messages with the attribute's spaces, the extra fields, validateIncomingRequest before update and bulk, none before create, so a form body is `$request->all()`: `readApiBody` now parses one), "Bulk data is required." for an absent or falsy `data`, a bulk that stops at the first invalid item keeping the ones before it. `apiRows()` reads six more tables and can order by name (Eloquent's `->orderBy('name')`, the database's collation). *Ported, 12 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (36 in all)*: applications — list (with `?tag`), show, delete, logs, start, restart, stop; their environment variables — list, create, update, bulk update, delete. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains a server and its default destination per team, three applications (a Docker image one, a public repository's Nixpacks one, another team's) and four variables made through Coolify's models, and 53 cases (153 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6); `src/api-v1-resources.test.ts` replays them byte for byte on Freeploy's own database — PGlite with every deploy migration (tests/db's installed copy, mapped by this package's jest and tsconfig: no new dependency), the fixture made by Freeploy's insert functions in Coolify's order, each case rolled back — 52 bodies (one more is the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all 53), the rows of 9 writes against the goldens' `after`, and the column order; tests/api-edge counts 36 routes. The emitter was not faithful to production in three ways, now fixed and the DP-70 goldens re-emitted (their bodies unchanged but for random uuids and dates): `Request::create()` left a JSON body out of the request bag `$request->get()` reads (Request::capture() puts it there), a form body unparsed, and Server's static identity map carried a server made visible for one request into the next. One masking is added: a default destination's `name`, "coolify" in Coolify and "freeploy" in Freeploy (`DEFAULT_STANDALONE_DESTINATION`, a label; the network is "coolify" on both). No mismatch is left. *Departures*: logs, start, restart and stop answer 501 in Coolify's envelope ("Reading the application's container logs …", the console's "Deploying / Restarting / Stopping the application needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet.") where Coolify reads the container or queues the job; delete removes the rows of an application nothing was deployed of at once (Coolify soft-deletes it and queues DeleteResourceJob; same answer) and answers 501 for any other; a Swarm destination is not loaded (Freeploy makes none); the generated Compose file of a Docker Compose application is redacted of its locked variables' values but not of locked shared variables it references, and `real_value` has no legacy escaping (Freeploy never sets `uses_legacy_escaping`) — neither is in the fixture. *Cut*: in the applications group the six create types (`/applications/{public,private-github-app,private-gitea-app,private-deploy-key,dockerfile,dockerimage}`) and PATCH `/applications/{uuid}` — the API takes about a hundred fields of its own (sharedDataApplications, validateDataApplications, the domain checks and conflicts, the settings it applies one by one), more than could be ported exactly here — and every group after it in the task's order: databases, services, deployments, resources, GET /projects/{uuid}/{environment}, servers create, update, delete, resources and domains, S3 storages, cloud tokens, scheduled tasks, PATCH /team. *Deferred*: the audit log lines, ResourceDnsRecords, the tag filter's goldens with a tag (the fixture has none).
- **/api/v1, continued (DP-73).** Coolify's API for an org's standalone databases — the eight engines Freeploy has — and their backup schedules, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains the 24 routes with routes/api.php's abilities (`deploy` for start, restart, stop and their GET `post_required` 405), and deploy-core-worker answers them in `src/api-v1/databases.ts`, in one org transaction, on the console's services and stores: a database is created by DP-57's `createDatabase()` — which now takes the API's fields as `create_standalone_*()`'s `$otherData` (filled after the generated passwords, encrypted where the model casts, a given name, `redis_password` for the REDIS_PASSWORD variable, the image the volumes are made for) —, written through the console's database store (`writableDatabaseColumns()` gains the resource limits, health check and log columns the API writes, `DATABASE_API_COLUMNS`), its connection URLs are `showDatabase()`'s, it is deleted by `deleteDatabaseNow()` (the never-started rule), Redis's password by the General page's variable save (`saveRedisPasswordVariable()`), a schedule by DP-65's stores with `validateDatabasesBackupInput()`, the cron check and `backupSolutionAvailable()`. The bodies are DatabasesController's: `removeSensitiveData()` — the columns but `$hidden` (passwords decrypted, init scripts, configuration and the URLs for a token that may read secrets; `legacy_password_quoting` never), the status accessor, `database_type`, `server_status`, the loaded destination with its server and settings (DP-71's `nestedServer()`) — then serializeApiResponse(); the list's `backup_configs` with `latest_log`, the schedules' list with their `executions`, each with `s3_storage_uuid`/`s3_storage_uuids` (primary first) in Coolify's column order (`COOLIFY_COLUMNS` gains the two tables) and an execution's message only for a token that may read secrets, redacted of the database's passwords and locked variables (and every variable's value when it redacts all) by the SensitiveDataRedactor port (`helpers/remote-process.ts`). Validation is the controller's own, not the console's (`src/api-v1/rules.ts`: the part of Laravel's validator it uses — required, nullable, string, numeric, integer, boolean, array, distinct, min/max/between with their numeric, string and array messages, regex, exists — rule by rule in Coolify's order, and the implicit-rule stop), in Coolify's order of checks: create's first extra-field check answers the field names as a list (Coolify calls a Collection's one-argument `add()`), `is_public` without a port is stored false, the per-engine fields and base64 configuration, the public port range and taken-port checks (`databasePublicPortTaken()`, which also leaves out every database with the same id of any engine, as `isPublicPortAlreadyUsed()` does), tags as `attachTagsToResource()`, Laravel's pretty-printed `abort(403)` for an instant deploy without the deploy ability, a body's `uuid` naming the database (Laravel's `$request->uuid` reads the input before the route). No migration. *Ported, 21 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (57 in all)*: databases — list, show, create PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB, MongoDB, Redis, ClickHouse, Dragonfly and KeyDB, update, delete, start, restart, stop; backups — list, create, update, delete, executions list, execution delete. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains an S3 storage per team, the eight engines made by Coolify's `create_standalone_<engine>()` and another team's PostgreSQL, four schedules (one with an S3 destination) and two executions (one message holding a password), and 142 cases (295 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6); `src/api-v1-databases.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite, the fixture made by `insertDatabase()`, `insertDatabaseBackup()` and the S3 store's functions in Coolify's order): 141 bodies (one more is the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all 142), the rows of 34 writes against the goldens' `after` (and that the three refused writes store nothing), every engine's show for a read and a read:sensitive token, the list with its schedules and latest executions; tests/api-edge counts 57 routes. One masking is added: a uuid the request made where a connection URL names it as its host (`internal_db_url` of a created database). A schedule's numeric retention sizes are read as `::float8::text` in `after` (PDO and PGlite give a numeric differently) and an id a write takes from a sequence is not compared (sequences do not roll back between cases); `application envs`' `after` now reads only the applications' variables (Redis's have the same resource id). No mismatch is left. *Departures*: start and restart answer 501 "Starting / Restarting the database needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." after Coolify's "Database is already running." (stop answers Coolify's "Database is already stopped." for a stopped database and 501 otherwise) where Coolify queues them; an instant deploy (create and update), making a database public (StartDatabaseProxy), making a started one private and a backup that runs now (`backup_now`) answer 501 before anything is stored, where Coolify stores and queues (making a never-started database private is stored: it has no proxy); delete removes the rows of a database that never started at once (Coolify soft-deletes and queues DeleteResourceJob; same answer) and answers 501 for any other; deleting a schedule or an execution whose local file is on a functional server, or with `delete_s3` while S3 copies exist, answers 501 (the fixture's server is not functional and has none, as Coolify's); another team's S3 storage answers "The selected s3 storage uuid is invalid." where Coolify, whose `exists` rule looks in every team, answers "The selected S3 storage is invalid for this team." — telling a token the uuid exists elsewhere; an execution's message is redacted of the database's own secrets and its variables', not of remote secret manager values or shared variables a variable references. *Not ported in the databases group*: POST /databases/sqlite (Freeploy has no SQLite; the fallback 404), logs, imports (upload, create, show), environment variables (list, create, update, bulk, delete), storages (list, create, update, delete) and their backup schedules (set, delete, run), tags (list, create, delete), move, migrate, clone and vulnerabilities. *Cut*, from the end of the task's order: services (list, show, create from a template and the custom Compose answer, update, delete, envs, start, stop, restart), deployments (list, show, by application, cancel, deploy by uuid or tag), GET /resources and GET /projects/{uuid}/{environment}, servers create, update, delete, resources and domains, PATCH /team, S3 storages, cloud tokens and scheduled tasks. *Deferred*: the audit log lines, the stage check of the routes.
- **/api/v1, continued (DP-74).** Coolify's API for an org's services and their environment variables, and for deployments, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains 20 routes with routes/api.php's abilities (`deploy` for a service's start, restart, stop, cancelling a deployment and `/deploy`, and the GET `post_required` 405 of the four), and deploy-core-worker answers them in `src/api-v1/services.ts` and `src/api-v1/deployments.ts`, in one org transaction, on the console's services and stores. *Services*: a service is created from a one-click template by the New Resource page's rows and parser (`newServiceFromTemplate()`, `createServiceFromTemplate()` — DP-59 —, `createOneClickService()` split into `newServiceParserContext()` and `insertParsedRows()`), and from a Compose file the request gives by the same parse (`parseService()` on a new service row: Coolify's API path is `$service->parse(isNew: true)`, the same serviceParser), its name, label escaping and network set before the parse as the controller sets them; a refused url rolls the whole creation back. Its urls are applied as `applyServiceUrls()` applies them — every url checked first (`validateApplicationDomains()`, the duplicates across containers), then each container's domains checked against the org's applications (with their Compose domains) and service applications by name (`checkIfDomainIsAlreadyUsedViaAPI()`, the service being created or changed read as it stands in memory), set through ServiceApplication's saving hook (`setServiceApplicationFqdn()`, now shared with the parser) and written into its variables by `updateServiceCompose()` (a port of `updateCompose()`: the image written back, the container's own SERVICE_URL_/SERVICE_FQDN_ pairs deleted and made again, the copied declarations left out). An update loads the service's rows, changes them, parses again and writes back what changed (DP-66's `loadServiceRows()`/`writeServiceRows()`); `redact_all_env_values_in_logs` and `include_coolify_default_variables` join the service store's writable columns. Delete is `deleteServiceNow()` (the never-started rule) after Coolify's `delete_from_coolify_only` refusal; start answers Coolify's "Service is already running." and stop "Service is already stopped." by the aggregated status (`serviceStatus()`), anything else 501. The bodies are ServicesController's: `removeSensitiveData()` — the columns without `id` (the Compose files only for a token that may read secrets, the generated one redacted of the locked variables), the status accessor and the applications and databases it loads (with ServiceApplication's `url` append, in Coolify's column order: `COOLIFY_COLUMNS` gains the three tables), `server_status` and the server it loads (another instance than the destination's, whose secrets stay hidden), the destination with its server and settings for a token that may read the server's secrets, the list's `laravel_through_key` — then serializeApiResponse(); a variable as DP-71's `variableModel()` (now taking the resource type, and the attributes a just-created service variable has). Validation is the controller's (`Validator::make()` with Laravel's own messages: `src/api-v1/rules.ts` gains `required_without`, `array:name,url` with the controller's message, closure rules, nested wildcards `urls.*.name` and Laravel's `required` message), the extra-field checks, `validateTagsParameter()`, the base64 and injection checks of a Compose file (an invalid YAML is Coolify's 500), the build server and destinations checks. *Deployments*: an application's deployments are read from the queue as `Application::deployments()` pages them (newest first, `skip`/`take` validated as `$request->validate()` answers, the ValidationException summary), logs only for a token that may read secrets; cancel and `/deploy` answer every check Coolify makes before it acts, in its order. No migration. *Ported, 16 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (73 in all)*: services — list, show, create, update, delete, start, stop, restart; their environment variables — list, create, update, bulk update, delete; deployments — by application, cancel; deploy by uuid or tag. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains a service per team (forceCreate with fixed columns, an application and a database, three variables through the model) and five deployments, and 127 cases (422 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6); `src/api-v1-services.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite, the services made by `insertParsedService()`, a case's `setup` SQL run as emit.php runs it): 121 bodies (five more are the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all 127, and one is the catalogue below), the rows of 31 writes against the goldens' `after` — created services with their parts, variables, volumes, tags and generated Compose file — and that the five refused writes store nothing; tests/api-edge counts 73 routes. One masking is added: a uuid the request made where a generated domain names its container after it (`-<new-uuid>.` in a created service's `domains`); the `after` queries write such a uuid as `<uuid>` and the random name of a service made from a Compose file without one as `service-<random>`. DP-71's two delete cases now remove the fixture's deployments first (`setup`), so that they still delete an application nothing was deployed of; `api-v1-resources.test.ts` runs a case's `setup`. *Mismatches*: the list of service types of `POST /services` with an unknown type is Freeploy's catalogue — generated from templates/compose at the pinned commit (DP-59: what Coolify's generate:services writes and its CDN serves), 359 templates — where the emitter's Coolify reads the templates/service-templates.json committed at that commit, 355: the test holds Freeploy's list to Coolify's and the four templates generated since. *Departures*: a service's start and restart, an instant deploy (create and update: refused before anything is stored), cancelling a queued or running deployment ("Cancelling a deployment needs the SSH executor…", the console's), and a `/deploy` that would queue an application, start a service (Coolify answers 500 there without a server) or start a database answer 501 where Coolify queues or starts; a deploy whose resources Coolify answers without deploying (a pull request not found, unauthorized, a docker tag for another build pack) answers as Coolify does; delete removes the rows of a service none of whose parts ever ran at once (Coolify soft-deletes and queues DeleteResourceJob; same answer; `after` reads whether the service is visible) and answers 501 for any other; another team's deployment is not found (row-level security) where Coolify answers 403 "You do not have permission to cancel this deployment." — telling a token it exists; the instance's own domain is not among the conflicts (Freeploy has no instance FQDN). *Not ported in the groups done*: GET /deployments and GET /deployments/{uuid} (they serialize the deployment's whole application with its environment, project and team, and the team is membership-worker's organization, which deploy-core-worker does not read), a service's logs, storages and their backup schedules, tags, DNS, scheduled tasks, move, migrate, clone, vulnerabilities, and its applications' and databases' own routes. *Cut*, from the end of the task's order: GET /resources and GET /projects/{uuid}/{environment}, servers create, update, delete, resources and domains, PATCH /team, S3 storages, cloud tokens and scheduled tasks. *Deferred*: the audit log lines, ResourceDnsRecords, the stage check of the routes.
- **/api/v1, continued (DP-78).** Coolify's API for the deployments in progress and one deployment, every resource of the team and an environment's resources, and for creating and deleting a server and a server's resources and domains, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains 8 routes with routes/api.php's abilities (GET /projects/{uuid}/{environment_name_or_uuid} after /environments and not taking `/envs`, the shared variables' route, which is not ported: a route can now name a path it does not take), and deploy-core-worker answers them in `src/api-v1/deployments.ts`, `resources.ts` and `servers.ts`, in one org transaction, on the console's services and the groups' own serializations. *Deployments*: the queued and running deployments of the org's applications (deleted ones included, as `applicationIdsOwnedByTeam()`), by id, and one deployment, each serialized as DeployController's `removeSensitiveData()` — its columns, `logs` for a token that may read secrets, then serializeApiResponse() — with its application as Eloquent loads it (`applicationRelation()`: every column in Coolify's order but `$hidden`, whatever the token, the accessors, the counts, `server_status`, the destination with its server and settings whose secrets stay hidden); one deployment's application also carries its environment, project and team (`Application::team()`), loaded first. The team is the org as membership-worker shows it to the token's user — `GET /v1/organizations/{org}` over deploy-core-worker's existing MEMBERSHIP_WORKER binding, the way identity-worker's teams read it (no binding added; the pinned loop is unchanged) — serialized by `coolifyTeam()` (`@saas/contracts/coolify-api`, now shared with identity-worker's `teamPayload()`). *Resources*: GET /resources (the team's projects, then their applications, services and databases engine by engine, each with `laravel_through_key`, its status and `type()`) and an environment's resources (its applications, PostgreSQLs, Redis, MongoDBs, MySQLs, MariaDBs and services, as environment_details() loads them) are serializeApiResource(): Eloquent's toArray() unsorted, every hidden attribute made visible for a token that may read secrets (exposeSensitiveFields(): passwords and webhook secrets decrypted, configuration, `legacy_password_quoting`, the connection URLs), the locked values redacted — DP-71's, DP-73's and DP-74's serializations take an unsorted form (`applicationRelation()`, `databaseResource()`, `serviceResource()`) and `COOLIFY_COLUMNS` gains projects, environments and the eight database tables. *Servers*: create is the console's `createServer()` (which now takes the proxy type) after create_server()'s validator (`not_regex` for control characters, nameRules, ValidServerIp, the port range, serverUsernameRules' message, `in` for the role, booleans, the host key's size), the extra fields and `normalizeSshHostKeys()`, the legacy `is_build_server` conflict, the usable build server for a deployments-only role, the proxy types, the org's key and `sshEndpointInUse()`; a given SSH host key is stored trusted (`trustServerSshHostKey()`, packages/db). Delete answers Coolify's checks in its order (the three query flags as FILTER_VALIDATE_BOOLEAN reads them, the resources without `force`, the local server, the provider links and tokens, the deploy ability for `force` and `delete_from_provider`, the resources before `remove_ssh_key`) and deletes a server without resources by the console's `deleteServer()` after detaching its additional applications. A server's resources are `definedResources()` (the primary applications, the additional ones, the databases engine by engine, the services) with each one's status accessor; its domains are the hosts of its applications' and its services' applications' URLs grouped by the server's address, or with `?uuid` an application's `fqdns`; GET /servers/{uuid}?resources (DP-70's not-ported case) carries them too. No migration. *Ported, 8 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (81 in all)*: deployments — list, show; resources — list; projects — an environment's resources; servers — create, delete, resources, domains. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains 63 cases (485 in all; no new fixture rows; `setup` soft-deletes the server's resources where a deletion needs a server without them) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6); `src/api-v1-continued.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite), with DP-70's `servers: show with resources`, `servers: resources` and `servers: domains`, which answered the fallback before (DP-74's file replays the deployments): every body byte for byte (four more are the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all of them; tests/api-edge counts 81 routes), the rows of 13 writes against the goldens' `after` — a created server with its settings and default destination, a deleted server gone — and that the two refused writes store nothing. One masking is added: the `id` of a team nested in a project, as `<team>`; the `after` queries write a generated server name as `<random-name>` and leave out the other team's server. *Mismatches*: none but the departures. *Departures*: deleting a server with its resources (`force`), removing the SSH key from it, deleting it from its cloud provider, stopping the applications that use it as an additional destination and creating it with `instant_validate` answer 501 in Coolify's envelope, after Coolify's refusals and before anything is stored, where Coolify queues DeleteServer or ValidateServer or runs AuthorizedKeys::remove() over SSH (the emitter has no ssh: Coolify's golden there is its own failure, 422); a deleted server's rows are deleted at once (Coolify soft-deletes and queues DeleteServer; same answer; `after` reads whether it is visible); `sshEndpointInUse()` looks within the org, where Coolify looks across every team (the console's existing departure); the team a deployment nests has DP-70's departures (the org's public id, a null description, Coolify's defaults for a new team); Freeploy has no instance public IPs, so a server at host.docker.internal is given as it is in its domains, as Coolify gives it without them; the deployments in progress are a list by id, where Coolify's `sortBy('id')` keeps the collection's keys and writes an object when the database returns them in another order. *Not ported in the groups done*: PATCH /servers/{uuid} (Laravel's url, ipv4 and ipv6 rules, thirteen settings and the proxy change over SSH — more than could be ported exactly here) and PATCH /team (it writes only `is_build_server_fallback_enabled` and `scan_images_on_new_servers`, settings Freeploy's orgs have neither the columns nor the features for — GET /team answers Coolify's defaults for them; DP-69's General page writes the name and description, which the API does not take). *Cut*, from the end of the task's order: S3 storages, cloud tokens and scheduled tasks; the application create types (public, private GitHub App, private deploy key, Dockerfile, Docker image) and PATCH /applications/{uuid}. *Deferred*: the audit log lines (`api.server.created`, `api.server.deleted`), the stage check of the routes.
- **/api/v1, continued (DP-81).** Coolify's API for the team's tags and the tags of an application, a standalone database and a service, the shared variables of a project and of an environment, and S3 storages, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains 27 routes with routes/api.php's abilities (the shared variables' eight before GET /projects/{uuid}/{environment_name_or_uuid}, which no longer needs DP-78's `except`), and deploy-core-worker answers them in `src/api-v1/tags.ts`, `shared-variables.ts` and `s3-storages.ts`, in one org transaction, on the console's stores. *Tags*: TagsController's list (by name), create (authorized first, then validateIncomingRequest(), `required|string|min:2|max:255` with Laravel's own messages and only `name`, the name through strip_tags(), trim() and strtolower(), "The tag name must be at least 2 characters after sanitization.", the 409 of a name the org has), rename (validated before the tag is looked up; the same name writes nothing, as Eloquent saves nothing clean) and delete (the pivot rows first: deploy.taggables has no foreign key, Coolify's cascades); HandlesTagsApi's list, add and remove under /applications, /databases and /services/{uuid}/tags (findTaggableResource() as each controller has it, the resource's 404 message, then the policy; `tag_name` or `tag_names` — never both — with required_without, `array|min:1` and `tag_names.*` string, the names normalized and deduplicated, createOrFirst then syncWithoutDetaching, "Tag not found." and "Tag not found on resource."). A resource's tags come in the pivot's order (`ORDER BY ctid`, packages/db's `listResourceTagsInPivotOrder()`): Coolify's morphToMany query has no ORDER BY and PostgreSQL returns the pivot's order, which the goldens show (a tag attached later comes after an older tag attached first). *Shared variables*: SharedEnvironmentVariablesController for `type` project and environment — resolveProject() and resolveEnvironment() (by name, then uuid) with their 404s, the policy as each route asks it (view and update of the project or environment, then update or delete of the variable), validateEnvPayload() (validateIncomingRequest(), the key trimmed, customApiValidator()'s environmentVariableKeyRules, `value` string, the three booleans, `comment` max:256, only the six fields), keyExistsInScope()'s 409s, create answering `{"id":N}`, update writing only what changed and keeping a lock (the saving hook), delete; a variable is serialized as removeSensitiveData() makes it (the team, scope ids and version hidden, the value decrypted for a token that may read secrets and never when locked) through serializeApiResponse(). packages/db's `insertSharedVariable()` takes `isShownOnce` (the API can create a variable locked) and `apiRows()` reads `shared_environment_variables`. *S3 storages*: removeSensitiveData() (id and private_key_id hidden, the key and secret decrypted for a token that may read secrets) through serializeApiResponse(), the list by name, the 404 then the policy; create authorized first, validateIncomingRequest(), then validateBody() with the controller's rules in order (`type` as Rule::enum, nameRules, descriptionRules, connectionRules(S3): the endpoint's `required|string|max:255`, its credentials and control-character closure and SafeWebhookUrl through the context's resolver, ValidS3BucketName, region, key and secret) and only the allowed fields, then S3Storage::create() as the model stores it (strip_tags() on the name and description, the endpoint, bucket and region trimmed or null, the key and secret trimmed, `use_path_style_endpoint` given or detectPathStyleEndpoint(), `skip_tls_verification`, `is_usable` false unless given); update (the 404, the policy, connectionRules(S3, partial) with the saved endpoint, then only the fields sent, through packages/db's new `updateS3StorageColumns()`); delete (the console's `deleteS3Storage()`: the schedules let the storage go first, S3 turned off where none remains); validate (testConnection(shouldSave): DP-65's `testS3Connection()` — SafeWebhookUrl, the bucket rule, ListObjectsV2 signed with SigV4 and the path-style fallback — through the context's resolver and fetch, `ApiV1Context.s3`, replaceable for tests; then `is_usable`, `unusable_email_sent` and the URL style saved as Coolify's save() writes them, only what changed). `rules.ts` now lets a closure rule wait (SafeWebhookUrl resolves a host) and picks the size message by the rules, as Laravel's getAttributeType() does (an array under `string|min:2` is "characters"). No migration. *Ported, 27 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (108 in all)*: tags — list, create, update, delete; an application's, a database's and a service's tags — list, add, remove (9); a project's shared variables — list, create, update, delete; an environment's — list, create, update, delete; S3 storages — list, show, create, update, delete, validate. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains 153 cases (638 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6) and fixture rows at the end of both fixtures — four tags and five pivot rows, five shared variables (after the servers' four predefined ones, which Freeploy's fixture now makes too so the ids agree) — and emit.php resolves host names through `SafeWebhookUrl::resolveHostsUsing()`; `src/api-v1-tags-variables-storages.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite): every body byte for byte (two more are the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all of them; tests/api-edge counts 108 routes), the rows of 44 writes against the goldens' `after`, that the departure stores nothing, the 403 of a denied policy after the 404 of an unknown resource, that another team's tag is never listed, renamed or deleted, and a connection test that reaches the endpoint (a ListObjectsV2 through the injected fetch, the storage saved usable). One masking is added: the answer of a created shared variable, `{"id":N}`, as `<new-id>` (the sequence's next id, which earlier cases move). The whole goldens.json was emitted again: the earlier cases change only in random values (uuids, ciphertexts, generated passwords and the domains that name a new uuid); three case definitions changed without changing their goldens — the database and service creates' `tags` queries read only the tags after the fixture's (`t.id > 4`), and the created server's `<random-name>` pattern now takes a hyphenated adjective (`old-fashioned-otter-…`, which the DP-78 pattern missed one run in a few). Freeploy's fixture S3 storage now uses path-style URLs, the column default Coolify's forceCreate() leaves (DP-73's fixture had it off; no earlier golden shows it). *Mismatches*: none but the departure. *Departures*: creating an SFTP, FTPS or WebDAV storage answers 501 in Coolify's envelope ("SFTP storages are not available yet.", the console's message) after validateIncomingRequest() and before any validation or write, where Coolify validates and stores it: only S3-compatible storages are ported (they back up through rclone and SFTP); updating or validating such a storage would answer the same, but none can exist. A failed connection test sends no "Backup Storage Connection Error" email (Coolify sends one when transactional emails are on; the emitter has them off), so `unusable_email_sent` stays. *Not verified against a golden*: a validate that reaches an endpoint — the emitter has no S3 endpoint it may reach (Coolify's SafeWebhookUrl pins the request to the resolved address); the answer `{"valid":true,"message":"S3 storage connection is valid."}` is Coolify's text, and the listing is DP-65's, held to Coolify's SDK there. *Cut*, from the task's order: cloud tokens and cloud-init scripts (creating and validating a token call the provider's API; the cloud-init script's ValidCloudInitYaml needs symfony/yaml's parse errors, which the Worker cannot give — the console reproduces only a tab-indented first line — so create and update could not answer exactly), the storages of applications, databases and services, and the scheduled tasks of applications and services. *Deferred*: the audit log lines (`api.tag.*`, `api.shared_env.*`, `api.s3_storage.*`), the stage check of the routes.

a service's part with its service, serviceResource()). *Scheduled tasks*: ScheduledTasksController's list (by name), create (the policy, validateIncomingRequest(), the rules, validate_cron_expression() through DP-68's validator, the deploy permission an enabled task needs, the name through strip_tags(), the defaults; the saving hook's reset of the failure streak written into the answer of an enabled task), update ("At least one field must be provided.", the rules and the cron before the task is looked up, the deploy permission whenever the task stays enabled, only what is dirty, the reset when it is enabled again), delete and executions (newest first, the float cast of `duration`, `worker_host` hidden); removeSensitiveData() hides the ids and, unless the token may read secrets, the command, then serializeApiResponse() with the relation canReadSensitive() loaded (the application, or `application: null` and the service). packages/db's `insertScheduledTask()` takes `enabled`, and `deleteScheduledTask()` can keep the executions: Coolify's scheduled_task_id is a foreignId() without a constraint, so its delete leaves them, as the golden shows (the console still deletes them). *Database variables*: DatabasesController's envs, create_env, update_env_by_uuid, create_bulk_envs and delete_env_by_uuid, as DP-74 answers a service's, on DP-64's `standalone-<engine>` resource types; the list asks the view policy and hides no column beside removeSensitiveEnvData()'s; applications.ts's variableModel() now takes any resource type. *Team variables*: team_envs (viewSharedVariables), team_create_env (the payload validated before authorize('create')), team_update_env and team_delete_env (the variable found, then the policy), keyExistsInScope() with every scope column null, on DP-81's shared-variables.ts. No migration. *Ported, 31 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (139 in all)*: an application's, a database's and a service's storages — list, create, update, delete (12); an application's and a service's scheduled tasks — list, create, update, delete, executions (10); a database's variables — list, create, update, bulk, delete; the team's shared variables — list, create, update, delete. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains 228 cases (866 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6) and fixture rows at the end of both fixtures — three team variables, three volumes, six file mounts, four scheduled tasks and three executions; Freeploy's fixture now moves the volumes' sequence as emit.php's `$advanceSequences()` did, so the databases' data volumes are 2–11 on both sides; `src/api-v1-storages-tasks-envs.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite): every body byte for byte (one more is the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all of them; tests/api-edge counts 139 routes), the rows of 49 writes against the goldens' `after`, that the eleven departures store nothing, the 403 of a denied policy after the 404 of an unknown resource, and that the other team's volume, task and variable are never listed or deleted. One masking is added: the integer `id` of an object whose uuid the request made (a created storage), as `<new-id>`. The whole goldens.json was emitted again: the earlier cases change only in random values, and in the six created shared variables' ids (already masked as `<new-id>`), which the three team variables move; DP-74's service `after` queries leave out the fixture's volume of the web part, so their goldens are unchanged. *Mismatches*: none but the departures. *Reproduced as Coolify answers it*: a service's storage list answers 500 when one of its parts has a file mount — the controller sets `resource_type` to 'application' and then reads the file's `resource`, a morphTo to no class; a duplicate volume name answers the unique index's 500 (a host file at a mount path the resource has is answered the same way; no golden has one). *Departures*: where Coolify writes to the server, Freeploy answers 501 in Coolify's envelope after Coolify's own refusals and before anything is stored — creating a managed file (the created hook queues ServerStorageSaveJob; the emitter fakes the queue, so Coolify's golden is its 201), changing a file's content (the same job; a host file's content is a row only, as saveStorageOnServer() skips host files), creating a directory or a file with a source path (assertHostPathOnServer() checks it over SSH; Coolify's golden is its own 422 from the failed check) and deleting a file or directory inside the resource directory (deleteStorageOnServer(); Coolify's golden is its own 500): eleven cases. A service's resource directory is its workdir only (a parser-version-3 service's Compose source directory is not a second one; the fixture's service is version 5). *Not ported in the groups done*: executing a scheduled task now (POST …/scheduled-tasks/{task_uuid}/execute queues ScheduledTaskJob, which runs the command in the container over SSH), the volume backups of a storage (PUT and DELETE …/storages/{storage_uuid}/backups, POST …/backups/run: rclone and the server) and PATCH /team (DP-78's judgement stands: it writes only settings Freeploy's orgs have neither the columns nor the features for). *Cut*: none of the four groups. *Deferred*: the audit log lines (`api.*.storage_*`, `api.scheduled_task.*`, `api.database.env_*`, `api.shared_env.*`), the stage check of the routes.

Laravel renders `{\n    "message": ""\n}`); Freeploy has no development mode, so this is Coolify's production answer. *Cloning*: clone_by_uuid of an application and a standalone database — validateIncomingRequest(), customApiValidator() (nameRules(required: false), `clone_volumes` boolean) and the fields not allowed, the lookup, the two policies, the deploy permission for clone_volumes, the standalone or Swarm destination of the org on a server that can host resources, placeResource — then the console's cloneApplicationTo() (DP-55) and cloneDatabaseTo() (DP-64), which now take the clone's uuid and the request's name. Opening a preview of a Docker Compose application answers 501 too ("… its domains need the application Compose parser.": generate_preview_fqdn_compose() parses the file as a deployment does, which Freeploy cannot yet; Coolify's golden is its 201); changing a Compose preview's domains is ported. *Not verified against a golden*: the public GitHub source's git type — the emitter has no seeded GitHub App 0, so its golden for a Git application without a git type is the 422 of a missing source; the case gives the application a source that does not exist (id 999) on both sides, and Freeploy answers `github` for id 0, as Coolify with its seeder does. *Not ported in the groups done*: cloning a service (clone_service() copies the parts, their volumes and files, the variables and the Compose files; the console has no port of it, so the route answers the fallback 404), the rollback images and rollback of an application (they read the server's images). *Cut*, from the task's order: the application create types and PATCH /applications/{uuid} (group 4: not attempted — about a hundred fields, the domain checks and the build-pack rules of each type), and GET /audit-events (group 5: events-worker's audit entries have text ids, no level, action, actor name or resource uuid, and Coolify's answer is a Laravel paginator with its URLs, so it would not map onto them exactly; no binding was added). *Deferred*: the audit log lines (`api.application.preview_*`, `api.application.destination_*`, `api.*.moved`, `api.*.cloned`), the stage check of the routes.

- **/api/v1, continued (DP-88).** Coolify's API for a standalone database's and a service's container logs, an application's rollback images and rollback, executing a scheduled task now, the backup schedule of a storage, a server's proxy, Sentinel, log drain, image scanning, Cloudflare Tunnel and host key settings, its Docker registry logins and its transfer, database imports and the org's GitHub Apps, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains 46 routes with routes/api.php's abilities (a route's method may now be PUT; the PATCH of log drains and Sentinel, a registry login and check, a backup run and the import check have a second api.ability, deploy; a transfer's status takes `read` or `write`; an import's status takes digits only, `->whereNumber()`, as a route's `except`), and deploy-core-worker answers them in `src/api-v1/resource-logs.ts`, `rollback.ts`, `scheduled-tasks.ts`, `volume-backups.ts`, `server-settings.ts`, `server-registries-transfer.ts`, `database-imports.ts` and `github-apps.ts`, in one org transaction. *Logs*: DatabasesController and ServicesController logs_by_uuid — no policy (neither controller asks it); a service's `sub_service_name` first ("Sub service name is required."), then the lookup; on a Swarm server the container status helpers list nothing without SSH, so "Database is not running." 400 and "Container not found." 404 are replayed. *Rollback*: rollback_images — the view policy, then GetRollbackImages on a server that is not functional: the registry's images, the commits of the newest ten successful production deployments (not HEAD, not a pull request's) with `max(updated_at)` as Coolify's driver writes it, for an application that pushes to a registry (not a Docker image, Docker Compose or inline Dockerfile application); a service's applications, then its databases), validateUpsertRequest() (customApiValidator() with Laravel's messages, `s3_storage_uuids.*` distinct, the fields not allowed, validate_cron_expression(), local backups off only with S3, the team's usable S3 storages with the primary first, "The s3_storage_uuid must be one of the s3_storage_uuids.", a directory mount only), useTeamCredentials for an S3 storage the schedule does not use yet, then updateOrCreate() on the storage's morph — `$request->boolean()`, `integer()` and `float()` as PHP casts them, the columns the request does not set left to the table's defaults (so a new schedule answers `timeout` null, as the model Coolify answers holds it) — and syncS3Storages() (the pivot, s3_storage_id kept one of them); destroy — "Resource not found.", the delete policy, the storage, the schedule, DeleteScheduledVolumeBackup's 409 for a running backup or a pending recovery, then the schedule with its executions and S3 storages (rows only while no execution holds an archive); run — the checks, then VolumeBackupJob (501). *Proxy*: payload() (the type, status, redirect and certificate resolver, the stored configuration for a token that may read secrets, never read over SSH); update — validateIncomingRequest(), customApiValidator() (SafeExternalUrl for redirect_url, proxyPageSizeRule()'s 1024 KB, the resolver pattern) and the fields not allowed, the server, the update policy, "Invalid proxy type.", the deploy permission for a type that changes, then the redirect fields saved into the proxy (Server's retrieved hook gives redirect_enabled true), the exact labels, the resolver, and changeProxy() (the type upper-cased, status exited, the saved configuration and settings cleared, the Traefik version forgotten); restart — the manageProxy policy (write), "Server is not functional.", then RestartProxyJob (501). *Sentinel*: transform() (isSentinelEnabled(): not a build server, not Swarm, not disabled or transferred away; TrafficIpMode; the token, custom URL and GeoIP key decrypted for a token that may read secrets); update — the update policy, the validator, isValidSentinelToken(), then the fields as the casts compare them, saved; the updated hook restarts Sentinel while it is on (501). *Log drains*: transform(); update — the validator (ValidHostname, ValidFluentBitConfig with the console's port, the CloudWatch patterns), the fields merged into the settings and the four drains' required fields and CloudWatch's exclusivity checked on the merged values, then saved (the keys and the custom configuration encrypted, as the console stores them); on a functional server Coolify then starts or stops the drain (501). *Image scanning*: transform() (the scanner's cache entries empty: no scanner is ever prepared); update — required_without_all, Rule::in() of the server's memory and CPU options (ScannerCommands::optionsUpTo() of server_metadata) with Coolify's messages, the timeouts, the fields not allowed, ensureManagedHere()'s 409, the limits saved, turning it off saved; turning it on prepares the scanner on the server (501). The viewSecurity policy is an admin's, so it is policy-worker's write, as a server's update is. *Cloudflare Tunnel*: show, update (the localhost refusal, `required|boolean`), enable and disable — the setting saved (the updated hook forgets the host key when it changed), and turned off with a previous IP, the IP restored (the saving hook's ip_previous, the saved hook's host key forgotten), with Coolify's messages. *Host key*: reset_host_key's checks (the update policy, "This server was transferred to another Coolify instance.", the key's rules and normalizeSshHostKeys()), then the save and ServerConnectionCheckJob (501). *Registries*: index — the update policy, then for a server that is not functional the console's registriesCard() rows (the registries its resources use, Coolify's absolute links on the console's URL) with Coolify's error line; login (the normalized registry, the username pattern, Coolify's messages), check and logout (the registry's name checked before the server) — the server, the policy, "The server is not reachable. Validate the server first.", then the command (501). *Transfer*: status (the transfer's listed fields, `[]` when none; the migration's log and error only for a user who may update the server); migrate — read:sensitive first (Coolify's 403 message), the server, the transfer policy (write), validateIncomingRequest(), the rules, then validateInstanceUrl() (https, SafeWebhookUrl's message as the answer's), normalizeToken() and hasRunningMigration() (a migration started in the last 30 minutes), then its SSH and target checks (501); the import check — the create policy (write), the query's rules (ValidServerIp, `system_github_apps.*`), then the import checks and the SSH port probe (501). *Imports*: upload — the uploadBackup policy (write), Validator::make() of `upload_id` (uuid) and `file` with Laravel's messages (a JSON body never carries a file), the extension and the 10 GiB size, then the upload Coolify would stage and send to the server (501); an import — the update policy, the rules with Laravel's messages (`required_if` naming the source's value, `prohibited_unless`, `prohibited_if`, `uuid`) and the fields not allowed, the deploy permission, then StartDatabaseImport's refusals (PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB and MongoDB only — a service database by its image —, "The database must be running before an import can start.", a Swarm destination's 501, the server; an upload is never found, as Freeploy stages none; the server path's pattern; the team's usable S3 storage and its bucket, the S3 key and its extension; allowsSourcePath()), then the restore on the server (501); an import's status — the view policy, then "Database import not found." (no import starts, so no activity exists). *GitHub Apps*: run as Coolify Cloud (no system-wide App; is_system_wide not accepted on update; a git source's URL may not point to a private network); list (the org's, removeSensitiveData() with the secrets decrypted for a token that may read them); create — the create policy (write), validateIncomingRequest(), the organization merged in normalized, customApiValidator() (SafeExternalUrl::forGitSource(): Laravel's `url` message, then the rule's), the fields not allowed, the org's private key, then the row (the secrets encrypted under DP-72's column contexts) answered as Eloquent writes a new model (create()'s attributes in order, team_id, the uuid of BaseModel's creating hook, the timestamps, the id, `type`); update — "GitHub app not found", the update policy, `$request->only()`, the rules of the fields set ("Validation error"), the API URL derived from a new HTML URL, the org's private key ("… to your team" without a period), the dirty columns saved, answered as the loaded model in Coolify's column order (`columns` gains github_apps) with the request's values uncast; delete — the policy, the applications that use it (409), the row. packages/db's apiRows() reads `github_apps`. No migration. *Ported, 46 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (248 in all)*: logs of a database and of a service (2); an application's rollback images and rollback (2); executing an application's and a service's task (2); a storage's backup schedule — PUT, DELETE and run for applications, databases and services (9); a server's proxy — show, update, restart (3); Sentinel, log drains, image scanning — show and update (6); Cloudflare Tunnel — show, update, enable, disable (4); host key reset (1); registries — list, login, check, logout (4); transfer status, migrate, the import check (3); a database's and a service database's imports — upload, import, status (6); GitHub Apps — list, create, update, delete (4). *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains 316 cases (1766 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6), no fixture rows (what a case needs is its `setup`, or its `setupCoolify` and `setupFreeploy`: the Git application's finished deployments, a backup schedule, two GitHub Apps); `src/api-v1-logs-backups-servers.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite): every body byte for byte (26 more are the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge with the status and headers of all of them; tests/api-edge counts 248 routes), the rows of 49 writes against the goldens' `after`, that the 13 departures with an `after` store nothing, the 403 of a denied policy after the 404 of an unknown resource, and that a GitHub App's secrets and a log drain's key are stored encrypted. Coolify's absolute links and its private-target message ("Configure allowed internal targets: …") name its APP_URL: Freeploy's console URL (DEPLOY_CONSOLE_URL), which the test gives as the emitter's. No masking is added. The whole goldens.json was emitted again: the earlier cases change only in random values. *Mismatches*: none but the departures. *Departures*: where Coolify runs or queues work on the server Freeploy answers 501 in Coolify's envelope after Coolify's own checks and before anything is stored — a database's and a service's logs (docker ps over SSH; Coolify's goldens are the 500 of the failed SSH step), the rollback images of a functional server (Coolify's golden is the empty list its catch-all gives when SSH fails), a rollback that passes its checks (queue_application_deployment(): three cases, Coolify's goldens its queued 200) or needs the server's images, executing a task (five cases), running a storage backup (three), a redirect change on a functional server (setupDefaultRedirect(): Coolify saves, then its golden is the 500), restarting the proxy, a Sentinel change while Sentinel is on (StartSentinel, queued: Coolify's 200 after saving), a log drain change on a functional server, turning image scanning on (PrepareImageScannerJob, queued), resetting the host key (ServerConnectionCheckJob, queued: Coolify's 200 after saving), the registries of a functional server and a login, check and logout (Coolify's goldens are its 200 with the read error and its 400s of the failed SSH command), a migration that passes the migrator's own checks (three cases; Coolify's goldens are the 422 of its failed target request) and the import check (Coolify's golden is the checks with the failed probe), and an import from the server or S3 that passes StartDatabaseImport's checks (six cases; Coolify's goldens are the 500 of the failed SSH step): 38 cases. POST /servers/import was not in the task's list. *Deferred*: the audit log lines (`api.volume_backup.*`, `api.server.proxy.*`, `api.server.sentinel.updated`, `api.server.log_drains.updated`, `api.server.image_scanning_*`, `api.server.cloudflare_tunnel.*`, `api.server.ssh_host_key.reset`, `api.server.migrate`, `api.github_app.*`, `api.scheduled_task.executed`), the stage check of the routes.
- **/api/v1, continued (DP-90).** Coolify's API for the routes that call an outside HTTP API, on DP-70's pipeline: api-edge's `src/api-v1/routes.ts` gains 26 routes with routes/api.php's abilities, and deploy-core-worker answers them in `src/api-v1/cloud-provider-tokens.ts`, `cloud-catalogs.ts` and `proxy-configuration.ts`, in one org transaction; a provider is asked outside it. *The outside API*: `ApiV1Context.providers` — the fetch (and the sleep between retries) — replaceable for tests; `src/api-v1/cloud-providers.ts` is the four provider services' requests (HetznerService, DigitalOceanService, VultrService, HostingerService: the base URLs, the Bearer token, the query of a GET as http_build_query() writes it, Hetzner's `page`/`per_page` with `meta.pagination.next_page`, DigitalOcean's with `links.pages.next`, Vultr's `per_page=100` with the cursor of `meta.links.next`'s query) sent as Laravel's PendingRequest sends them with `retry(3, …, throw: false)` — a failed answer sent again while the service's `when` allows it (Hetzner: a 429, or a 5xx of a GET or DELETE; DigitalOcean and Vultr: any failure; Hostinger: a GET's 5xx), the last answer returned — and decoded as PHP's json_decode($text, true) decodes it (an empty object is `[]`, an object keyed 0…n-1 a list), so that response()->json() of what Coolify filters is written again byte for byte. The errors: CloudProviderApiException (the code and the field names kept only when they look like identifiers, safeMessage() and toJsonResponse()'s 502), RateLimitException (Retry-After as `(int)` of Laravel's header(), which gives '' — so 0 — for a missing header, never null), Hostinger's `Hostinger API error: …` with the status as its code. *Cloud provider tokens* (CloudProviderTokensController): list — the controller's own isAdminOfTeam() 403 first ("This action is unauthorized."), then the org's tokens withCount('servers') (a soft-deleted server does not count); show; create — the create policy, validateIncomingRequest(), the body's rules (`in:` the four providers) and the fields not allowed, then validateProviderToken() (one GET, ten seconds: Hetzner's /v1/servers, DigitalOcean's and Vultr's /v2/account, Hostinger's virtual machines; "Invalid {provider} token. Please check your API token." 400), then the row with the token encrypted with the org's key under the console's column context (`cloud_provider_tokens.token`, security.cloud-tokens' store), answered `{uuid}` 201; update — the body first, then the token, the update policy, the name saved only when it changes; delete — the policy, "Cannot delete token that is used by servers." 400; validate — the stored token decrypted and asked. removeSensitiveData(): `id` hidden, the token decrypted only for a token that may read secrets. Every ability of the policy is a team admin's: policy-worker's deploy.resource.write.
