A denied `$this->authorize()` is answered as Coolify's exception handler answers an AuthorizationException under api/* — `{"message":"You are not authorized to perform this action.","error":"Unauthorized"}` — which the goldens show (the earlier groups answer Laravel's "This action is unauthorized." for a denied policy; no earlier golden covers a denial, so those answers are unverified). *Catalogs* (HetznerController, DigitalOceanController, VultrController, HostingerController): the query's `cloud_provider_token_uuid` or the deprecated `cloud_provider_token_id` (required_without each other, Laravel's message), the org's token of that provider ("Hetzner cloud provider token not found." 404), the view policy, then the catalog — Hetzner's server types without the deprecated ones, each with the locations where it is in stock and not deprecated (and only those in `location` when given), its images without other types and deprecated ones; DigitalOcean's regions and sizes that are available, its public distribution images; Hostinger's KVM VPS plans (`-vps-kvm<n>`), its SSH keys' and post-install scripts' `data` — and each controller's failures: Hetzner's 502 with the safe message for a provider error and "Failed to fetch Hetzner {resource}." 500 otherwise (a rate limit included), DigitalOcean's and Vultr's 500, Hostinger's 429 with Retry-After, "Hostinger denied access to {resource}: {message}" 403 for a 401 or 403, and its 500. A closure typed `array $item` that meets a scalar is the catch-all's 500, as Coolify's TypeError is. *Proxy configuration* (ServerProxyController::saveConfiguration, SaveProxyConfiguration): the deploy permission (RequiresDeployAbility's message with the controller's reason) before validateIncomingRequest(), the validator, the server, the update policy, a base64 configuration decoded (UTF-8 only, isBase64Encoded()), "The configuration field is required." for blanks, "Server is not functional." 400, then ProxyPortParser::fromConfiguration() — the Compose file read with compose-yaml's Symfony reader, which gains Yaml::PARSE_CUSTOM_TAGS (`parseYamlWithCustomTags()`: a local tag gives a TaggedValue of the value read untagged, '' when empty; the default reader is unchanged), the traefik and caddy services' ports checked as Docker Compose port syntax (short and long syntax, ranges, host IPs, protocols, variables and their defaults) — and ensureDefaultContainerName() (not on a Swarm server), each refusal as Laravel answers a ValidationException; then the configuration and its hash (md5 of its base64) saved into the server's proxy. No migration. *Ported, 26 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (274 in all)*: cloud provider tokens — list, create, show, update, delete, validate (6); Hetzner — locations, server types, images, SSH keys, firewalls, networks (6); DigitalOcean — regions, sizes, images, SSH keys (4); Vultr — regions, plans, operating systems, SSH keys (4); Hostinger — data centers, catalog, templates, SSH keys, post-install scripts (5); a server's proxy configuration (1). *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains 171 cases (1937 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6), five cloud provider tokens at the end of both fixtures, and a per-case `http` fake (Laravel's Http::fake() in emit.php, the same answers in the test's fetch) with the requests Coolify sent recorded (`requests`); `src/api-v1-cloud-providers.test.ts` replays them on Freeploy's database (PGlite): every body byte for byte, the Retry-After header, the requests Freeploy sent (method, URL with its query, the decrypted token — the retries included), the rows of 56 writes against the goldens' `after` (the 18 departures among them), the 403 of a denied policy after the 404 of an unknown token with no provider asked, and that a new token is stored encrypted and decrypts to what was sent (4 more are the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge; tests/api-edge counts 274 routes). The whole goldens.json was emitted again: the earlier cases change only in random values. tests/deploy-core-worker's DP-70 fallback case now names /dns/zones, as /hetzner/locations is answered. *Mismatches*: none but the departures. *Departures*: a proxy configuration that passes Coolify's checks is saved into the proxy, then written on the server over SSH (instant_remote_process(); Coolify's golden is the 500 of the failed command): Freeploy answers 501 in Coolify's envelope after the same save — 18 cases, whose rows are compared all the same. *Not verified against a golden*: a provider's float that is a whole number (PHP writes `4.0`, Freeploy `4`; cases.json cannot carry one), a RateLimit-Reset backoff (it reads the clock), a transfer error (no case makes the fake fail to answer), Hetzner's 429 with RateLimit-Reset, a GET with a JSON body or array query parameters, and a token of another org (row-level security refuses it). *Cut*, from the task's order: POST /servers/hetzner, /servers/digitalocean and /servers/vultr — each validates `cloud_init_script` with ValidCloudInitYaml, whose messages carry Symfony YAML's exact parse errors (`… at line N (near "…")`), and compose-yaml's reader gives none of them (nine of nine probes differ: duplicate keys, unclosed flows, tabs, colons in values, mixed sequences and mappings, missing references, indentation); DigitalOcean's droplet also needs `digitalocean_droplet_id` and `digitalocean_droplet_status`, which deploy.servers does not have (a migration); POST /servers/hostinger (no cloud-init rule, so it could be exact, but it buys a VPS and its replay needs the fixture's private keys stored as real keys — Freeploy's PGlite fixture stores the key file's path — so it was not reached); POST /applications/private-github-app and GET /github-apps/{id}/repositories and …/branches (generateGithubInstallationToken() signs an RS256 JWT with the App's private key: team 1 has no RSA key in the fixture, and the fixture's keys are not stored as keys; not reached); the cloud-init scripts (ValidCloudInitYaml, for the same reason as the server creates). *Deferred*: the audit log lines (`api.cloud_token.*`, `api.server.proxy.configuration_saved`), the stage check of the routes.
- **/api/v1, continued (DP-93).** Two fixes found while porting /api/v1, then the routes the second one blocked. *The policy denial*: Coolify answers a denied `$this->authorize()` under api/* from app/Exceptions/Handler.php — an AuthorizationException without a status of its own becomes 403, `application/json`, `{"message":…,"error":"Unauthorized"}`, the message the policy's own (`Response::deny()`, tags stripped) or, for a policy that answers false or Laravel's default, "You are not authorized to perform this action." The DP-70…DP-88 groups answered Laravel's `{"message":"This action is unauthorized."}`; `COOLIFY_API.unauthorized()` (packages/contracts) is now the handler's answer and takes the policy's message, and `POLICY_DENIALS` holds the nine policy messages of app/Policies (ApplicationPolicy::update and ::uploadBackup, DatabasePolicy::update and ::uploadBackup, ApplicationPreviewPolicy::update, ScheduledTaskPolicy::update, ServerPolicy::placeResource for the Coolify host and for another server, ResourceCreatePolicy::useTeamCredentials). CloudProviderTokensController::index's own `isAdminOfTeam()` refusal keeps "This action is unauthorized." (`COOLIFY_API.controllerUnauthorized()`), and DP-87's two `useTeamCredentials` refusals, which answered the policy's message without `error`, are the handler's now. *Where Coolify asks a policy, and what Freeploy asks*: of the 278 ported routes, 234 call `authorize()` in the controller or a helper of it (and the tag and import routes in their concerns, HandlesTagsApi and HandlesDatabaseImportsApi) — `view` 73, `update` 73, `create` 31, `delete` 27, `placeResource` 18, `useTeamCredentials` 16, `manageEnvironment` 16, `deploy` 15, `manage` 5, `viewSharedVariables` 4, a service database's `$ability` 4, `makeSystemWide` 2, and `viewSecurity`, `viewAny`, `validateConnection`, `transfer`, `stop`, `manageProxy`, `manageBackups` once each; the others read through the team's scopes (the lists, the teams, health and version) or ask none (a GitHub App's repositories and branches). Freeploy's handler asks policy-worker deploy.resource.read for `view`, `viewAny` and `viewSharedVariables` and deploy.resource.write for every other ability — the admin-only reads included (a cloud token's `view`, `viewSecurity`, a service's variables list's `manageEnvironment`, the registries list's `update`); each Freeploy check that stands for one of the nine abilities with a message now passes it (an application's or a database's update, storages, scheduled tasks, tags, previews, additional destinations, move, migrate and clone, volume backups, a database import, PATCH /applications/{uuid}, the create types). *Verified*: 31 golden cases (`denied: <earlier case>, <who>`, made from the earlier case with a member's token), emitted by Coolify's PHP, cover a denial on a route of every group where Coolify's policy can deny the team's own member — a default member's read token where the policy asks for more (a service's variables, image scanning), a member with team-wide read, read:sensitive, deploy and delete stored on its membership (`team_user.access`, `setupCoolify`) where the policy asks for write (an application's, a service's and a database's variable deleted, a tag deleted, a project's and a server's variable deleted, an additional destination removed — ApplicationPolicy's message —, a database started, a scheduled task run — ApplicationPolicy's message —, the proxy restarted, the registries listed), and a member with team-wide read, write and delete where the policy asks for a team admin (a private key created, updated and deleted; a server validated, created and deleted; an S3 storage created and deleted; a team variable created; the email channel updated; a destination created; Docker cleanup, image scanning and the Cloudflare Tunnel updated; the host key reset; a GitHub App created; a cloud token created; a Hostinger server created); `src/api-v1-denials.test.ts` replays them through deploy-core-worker's handler told policy-worker's member answer, byte for byte, with the content type and the rows the earlier case reads, and tests/api-edge checks that the edge refuses those members' deploy, delete and write tokens itself (Freeploy's member has no stored levels). The earlier groups' own denial tests now expect the handler's body. Not reachable for a member of the team whatever its stored team-wide levels, so no golden: DP-70's projects, environments and teams, DP-71's applications (their variables are covered), DP-74's deployments, DP-78's resources, an application's and a service's storages, a scheduled task's create, update and delete, a service's parts, previews, move, migrate and clone, the DP-87 create types and update, a log drain (its controller asks for deploy first), a server transfer (its controller's own read:sensitive check) — each asks only levels a member holding them is allowed; a denial there needs a token scoped to projects (`personal_access_tokens.scopes`), which shows another project's resources as not found, not denied. *Mismatches between Coolify's policies and Freeploy's roles (listed, not changed)*: (1) Coolify 4.7.6 stores levels per member (team_user.access: team-wide or per project, environment or resource) and per token (scopes); Freeploy has neither: its builder and viewer are Coolify's default member (team-wide read), so where Coolify lets a member with stored Deploy, Delete or Write levels deploy, start, stop, delete or change a resource (every `canAccess()` policy), Freeploy refuses — at the edge, as a token beyond the member's access; (2) ServerPolicy::transfer is the owner's alone, deploy.resource.write is an admin's too, so Freeploy lets an admin start a server transfer that Coolify refuses; (3) a Freeploy billing_admin counts as Coolify's member for its tokens (identity-worker's `coolifyRole()`), but policy-worker gives it no deploy.resource.read, so Freeploy refuses the reads Coolify's member is allowed; (4) Coolify's instance resources — a system-wide GitHub App or private key, the instance database, the Coolify host for placeResource — have no Freeploy counterpart (no golden reaches them); (5) Coolify's Deploy level without Write (a member's) does not exist in Freeploy, where deploying needs deploy.resource.write. *Private keys in the API fixture*: DP-71's PGlite fixture (`src/api-v1-database.ts`) encrypted each key's file path and stored `fp-<uuid>` as its fingerprint; it now stores the committed test key's text (`fixtureKeyText()`: the file, or a string inside a JSON file by a path of keys) with its real SHA-256 fingerprint, as DP-70's memory stores and emit.php always did, and the fixture gains team 1's private key 4 (`k4paritygithubappkey0004`), DP-72's RSA GitHub App key read from fixtures/sources/cases.json on both sides (emit.php follows the same path). Coolify's goldens change only where key 4 is listed: `keys: list` and `keys: list, read:sensitive` list it, and `keys: delete`'s `after` keeps it (the other 1934 earlier goldens are unchanged, random values aside); DP-70's memory store's next key id is 5. No earlier Freeploy replay compared a key-derived value of the PGlite fixture, so none changed answer. *Ported, 4 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (278 in all)*: GET /github-apps/{github_app_id}/repositories and …/repositories/{owner}/{repo}/branches (GithubController::load_repositories, load_branches: the App of the org, `GithubApp::installationToken()` — the JWT signed RS256 with the App's decrypted private key at the context's clock by DP-72's jwt.ts, POST {api}/app/installations/{id}/access_tokens with an empty JSON array, retried on a 5xx a second and two apart — then the pages of /installation/repositories until an empty one, sorted by name as Collection::sortBy() compares, or one GET of /repos/{owner}/{repo}/branches after the owner and repository checks, each retried three times 200 ms apart, GitHub's failure answered with its status; a token or key failure is Coolify's uncaught exception, 500 "Server Error"; no policy, as Coolify's controller asks none: `src/api-v1/github-repositories.ts`), POST /applications/private-github-app (create_application('private-gh-app') in `application-create.ts`: the deploy key type's checks with its own rules, then useTeamCredentials, githubAppRepository() — the App by uuid, its token, GET /repos/{slug} with the rate limit's 429, 404 for a repository the App cannot read and 400 for another failure — and the application stored with the slug, the App as its source and GitHub's repository id), and POST /servers/hostinger (HostingerController::createServer in `src/api-v1/hostinger-servers.ts`: the create policy, the body's rules and the fields not allowed, the deploy permission for a post-install script, extra SSH keys or instant validation, the org's Hostinger token and its view policy, the private key, then HostingerService through DP-90's client — the account's machines, the purchase with the key's real public key and the hostname only when it has a dot, the order still waiting for payment (202), a machine charged before a failed setup kept — the server stored with the placeholder IP, the token and the machine, the keys attached, up to ten looks for a public IP a second apart, and the IP saved; RateLimitException's 429 with Retry-After, the 500s). ApiV1Context.providers gains `now` (the clock of a JWT). No migration, no binding, no dependency. *Verified*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/fixtures/api` gains 91 cases (2028 in all) emitted the same way (PHP 8.3.6, PostgreSQL 16, Coolify 4.7.6, database dp93_golden), GitHub and Hostinger faked per case; `src/api-v1-github-apps-hostinger.test.ts` replays the 55 that reach the handler on Freeploy's database (PGlite): every body byte for byte, Retry-After, the requests sent (the JWT of the token request byte for byte — RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5 is deterministic — the installation token, the purchase's body with the public key, the retries) and the rows of 23 writes (5 more are the middleware's, replayed by tests/api-edge; tests/api-edge counts 278 routes). The whole goldens.json was emitted again: the earlier cases change only in random values and the three listings of key 4 above. *Departures*: DP-87's — a GitHub App application with `instant_deploy`, or a Docker Compose one, is refused with 501 before it is stored (Coolify queues the deployment or LoadComposeFile; GitHub was asked the same) — and a Hostinger server validated at once (by default for a token with deploy): Coolify queues ValidateNewCloudServerJob after the purchase and the store, Freeploy answers 501 in Coolify's envelope after the same purchase and the same rows, which are compared. *Not verified against a golden*: a GitHub answer that is not JSON (PHP warns on `null['message']`), GitHub's 5xx followed by a success (the fake answers one way per URL), a Hostinger purchase that fails after a machine was charged (the machines' list is one fake answer), the token of another org (row-level security), Team::serverLimitReached() (Freeploy has no server limit; the cases run self-hosted). *Cut*: none of the four; POST /servers/{hetzner,digitalocean,vultr} and the cloud-init scripts stay cut for DP-90's reason (Symfony YAML's parse errors). *Unsure*: goldens.json now also holds key 4's text (the `keys: list, read:sensitive` answer shows private keys), the same test key that fixtures/sources/cases.json already holds; it was not inlined by hand, but a secret scanner may still flag the new occurrence.
- **Integration tokens (DP-104).** Coolify's Keys & Tokens → Integration Tokens (`/security/integration-tokens`: IntegrationTokens, IntegrationTokenForm, IntegrationTokenEditor) and its `/api/v1/security/integration-tokens` — list, show, create, update, delete, sync-zones: 6 more of openapi.json's 330 operations (284 in all). No migration: `deploy.integration_tokens` (with `metadata`), `deploy.dns_provider_zones`, `deploy.managed_dns_records` and `deploy.secret_manager_links` are in the head schema (500/660, row-level security on org_id); packages/db gains `deploy/integration-tokens.ts`. *The actions* (`apps/deploy-core-worker/src/integration-tokens/`): CreateIntegrationToken's and UpdateIntegrationToken's rules (the capabilities closure, Doppler's `dp.(st|sa).` regex, Infisical's and Vault's `metadata.*`, `capabilities.*` last as Laravel's rule parser appends a wildcard's items), the metadata each provider keeps (empty values dropped; Cloudflare's `automatic_dns`/`proxied_dns` and its system keys), the re-entered secret when a connection setting changes, which changes ask the provider again (`$metadata != $storedMetadata` with PHP's loose ==) and which read a Cloudflare token's zones again, Eloquent's dirty attributes (updated_at untouched when nothing changes), deletionBlocker() and syncZones() (zones updated by provider id, the others dropped unless a managed record uses them, `zones_synced_at`). *The providers* (`providers.ts`, through `ApiV1Context.providers` / the console's injectable fetch): CloudflareTokenValidator (verify, a zone, its DNS records, the account's tunnels), syncZones()'s pages, Doppler's `/v3/me`, Infisical's universal-auth login and Vault's lookup-self with X-Vault-Token and X-Vault-Namespace, Laravel's timeouts; a failed transfer is a ConnectionException (Cloudflare's: 502 / the "Could not connect…" toast; the secret managers' validate() answer false), and Infisical's and Vault's base URL goes through SafeExternalUrl first (DP-81's resolver; a ValidationException the API's store does not catch, its update and the editor do). The token is encrypted with the org's key (`integration_tokens.token`, as the cloud token port does) and never returned; neither are the settings in the API (tokenData()). *Console*: `/v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/integration-tokens[/{uuid}[/refresh-zones]]` in withOrg, deploy.resource.read for the list and the editor, deploy.resource.write for the rest, a denial a 404 that runs nothing; the Livewire rules with Laravel's own messages (`The metadata.base url field…`, `The new token field…`) and the components' messages(), the toasts verbatim; deploy-web's `IntegrationTokensPage` (`IntegrationTokensPageKey`), its form and editor dialogs (provider switch, Tunnel selects DNS, the Cloudflare template link, zones with managed record counts, Refresh zones). *Goldens*: 98 cases and 5 member denials (2131 in all), emitted on the booted Coolify checkout; emit.php's fixture gains six integration tokens and four zones through the models, `Http::failedConnection()` for an answer with `failedConnection`, and the Vault headers in `requests`; the earlier goldens are kept as they were (re-emitted, they differ only in random values). *Departures*: the provider is asked before the transaction, not inside it (the same rows are left); the list breaks a `created_at` tie by id (Coolify's `latest()` has no tiebreak); the console's Refresh zones on a token that is not Cloudflare's toasts "Only Cloudflare tokens have DNS zones." (Coolify's button is Cloudflare's only), and its transfer error's toast is the fetch's message (Coolify's: cURL's); policy: Coolify's viewAny/view are a team admin's, the console reads with deploy.resource.read as the cloud tokens port does (the API asks deploy.resource.write for every ability, as DP-90 does). *Cuts*: the audit log entries (`ui.`/`api.integration_token.*`), secret manager links, managed DNS records and the DNS record jobs themselves (only read by deletionBlocker() and the zone counts), the MCP tool and the global search entry.

## Notes on DP10

- **Team pages, members and invitations (DP-69).** Coolify's team pages over Freeploy's organizations, members, role assignments and invitations — no second membership store: all of it is membership-worker's (`src/handlers/team.ts`, the queries in `packages/db/src/membership/team.ts`), reached through api-edge's org facade (`org` rate-limit family). **Roles.** Coolify's roles at 91b7e3e are owner, admin and member (no viewer); Freeploy stores them as owner, admin and **builder**, and shows its own other org roles (viewer, billing_admin) by their names, ranked as members. **Permission matrix.** `tests/policy-worker/fixtures/role-matrix/emit.php` asks Coolify's Gate, in a scratch copy of the real app (team, owner, admin, and a member with the default grants, team-wide Read), for 78 abilities of ten policies (servers, private keys, projects, environments, applications, databases, services, S3 storages, notifications, team) and `src/role-matrix.test.ts` holds policy-worker's decision for owner/admin/builder to it: **62 abilities × 3 roles compared, all equal; 16 skipped** for want of a route (server transfer, proxy and security pages; application, database and service backup uploads; a service's update, delete, stop, deploy, sensitive values and environment; team viewAny, create, delete and Admin View). Changes to the roles, to match: **builder loses `deploy.resource.write`** (Coolify's member creates, changes, deploys and deletes nothing and sees no secrets; with the old roles 38 member rows failed — an API key with the builder role can no longer write the deploy console either, as a Coolify token is bounded by its user's role), and a new known action **`deploy.team.read`** (owner, admin, builder, viewer: the team page and member list, TeamPolicy::view). Team update, manageMembers and manageInvitations ask the existing `organization.settings.update`, `organization.member.update_role`/`remove` and `organization.invitation.create`/`list`/`revoke`, which were already owner and admin only, as Coolify's. viewer reads as a member does; billing_admin sees nothing of the deploy console (pinned in the same test, outside Coolify's matrix). The policy-worker's runbook is touched so its component plans and redeploys (DP-40); `orun plan --changed` lists policy-worker. **API.** `GET|PATCH /v1/organizations/{org}/team` (Team\Index: name and description with its rules and "The Name field is required.", "Team updated."), `GET …/team/members` (the members with name, email, role, "You", and — for who can manage members — the 2FA column and count), `PATCH|DELETE …/team/members/{member}` (`makeOwner`/`makeAdmin`/`makeReadonly` and remove with Member.php's rules: an admin or owner, an owner for makeOwner, never on a member who outranks you, else "You are not authorized to perform this action."), `GET|POST …/team/invitations` and `DELETE …/team/invitations/{inv}` (InviteLink::viaLink and Invitations: "Members cannot invite admins or owners.", "Admins cannot invite owners.", "<email> is already a member of <team>.", "Pending invitation already exists for <email>.", an expired one replaced, "Invitation link generated.", "Invitation revoked.", "Invitation not found."), `GET|POST /v1/team-invitations/{inv}` (showInvitation and acceptInvitation: "You are not allowed to accept this invitation.", "Invitation expired." after Coolify's 3 days — 72 hours and one second, Carbon's float `diffInDays` —, already a member: dismissed). The validators' messages and the expiry are held to Coolify's own answers (`goldens.json`: 17 team and 15 invite inputs, 6 ages). **Migration 710** (`710_membership_org_description`): a nullable `membership.organizations.description` — Coolify's team has one and the org had none. **Pages.** `/team`, `/team/members` (Members with search, role filter, sort and pages of 10; "Invite a member" and "Pending invitations" below for who can manage invitations, as Coolify places them) and `/invitations/{id}` (`invitation/accept.blade.php`; a refusal is Coolify's 400 page), hand-ported; accepting makes the team the current one (the profile's last org) and opens /team. Departures: **invitations are links only** — email is not delivered on stage (WAITING W5), so "Send email" stays hidden as Coolify hides it without transactional email (the instance-admin callout is not shown: a tenant is no instance admin), and `POST …/invitations` with `via: "email"` answers 501 "Email delivery is not configured" and stores nothing; never is a send claimed. The link is `/invitations/inv_…` on the console's own origin (Coolify: `base_url()`); it is no secret, as Coolify's is not — only the signed-in user whose email the invitation names may open it. Coolify creates a passwordless account with a magic link for an address that has none; Freeploy does not create accounts — the invitee registers or signs in with the address, then opens the link — and answers 400 rather than Coolify's 404 when the address has no account. Coolify deletes an accepted, revoked or expired invitation; Freeploy keeps the row as history (`accepted`, `revoked`, `expired`) and lists only pending ones. Freeploy **keeps the last owner** (Lumen's "Cannot change role of the last active owner" / "Cannot remove the last active owner"); Coolify has no such rule. A demotion does not revoke the member's API tokens (Coolify's RevokeUserTeamTokens: Freeploy's keys are identity's, bound to a role of their own). The org is renamed, its slug kept. A denied ability answers 404, as every membership route does (Coolify: "This action is unauthorized."), and the team form is authorized before it is validated. The member list reads display name, email and two-factor state from `identity.users`/`identity.two_factor` (read only, as the support repository does); a member without a display name shows the email. The plan's `limit.members` gate still applies to a new invitation. The billing, membership, notifications and events binding loop is unchanged (no binding added). Not ported: the team avatar (upload, remove — the initial shows), the MCP server, build server fallback and two-factor requirement listboxes (no storage yet), a member's access scopes (Member\Access, "Edit access", "Scopes", "Access: …"; every member has Coolify's default grants), the audit log, Image Scanning, Admin View and Danger Zone pages (in the page search as Coolify lists them, not ported), the error page's "Contact support" link. Found: Coolify's own personal team name ("<name>'s Team") fails its team name rule (no apostrophe), so a personal team must be renamed before its General page saves — ported as is.
- **The team's Danger Zone and audit log, and Boarding (DP-75).** **Danger Zone** (`/team/danger`, Team\DangerZone, `danger-zone.blade.php`, DeleteTeam): membership-worker's `GET …/team/danger` (the page's state) and `DELETE …/team`, both `organization.delete` — a new known action, the owner's alone (TeamPolicy::delete); the role matrix now compares `team.delete` (**63 compared, 15 skipped**). The blade's branches and copy word for word ("Only team owners can delete this team.", "Your last or personal team cannot be deleted.", "This team still owns: … Remove or move these resources before deleting the team.", the Resources table, "Confirm Team Deletion?" with the team name to type), "Coolify" read as "Freeploy"; DeleteTeam's refusals ("Delete all team servers before deleting this team.", "Delete all team resources before deleting this team."). A **personal** team is identity's `personal-<user id>` organization (solo-mode.ts; Freeploy has no `personal_team` column), and the last-team rule counts the owner's active memberships of active orgs; Freeploy refuses both on DELETE too (Coolify only hides its button). What the team owns is **read only through `withOrg()`** as `deploy_app` (`packages/db/src/membership/team-danger.ts`, the pattern of identity's account deletion, but under row-level security): membership-worker has no binding to deploy-core-worker and one would grow the pinned loop, so **no binding was added**. Coolify's blockers are projects, servers (not soft-deleted) and non-instance-wide Git sources; Coolify deletes the team's private keys, S3 storages, tags, shared variables, cloud tokens… with it (Team::deleteOwnedRecords), which membership-worker cannot, so in Freeploy those **hold the team as well**, listed with the blockers under Freeploy's labels and refused with DeleteTeam's "Delete all team resources…". Deleting marks the organization `deleted` (the row stays: events, billing and audit entries name it), ends every membership, revokes every role assignment (users and API keys' service principals) and the pending invitations, and records `organization.deleted`; the confirmation says "permanently deleted from Freeploy" without Coolify's "and the database". Not here: Coolify's root team (id 0) and Cloud subscription branches; a billing subscription on the org is left to billing-worker; the console then opens the user's last team or their first (Coolify's select-team screen is not ported); there is no lock against a resource created between the count and the deletion (Coolify's lockForUpdate). **Leaving a team**: Coolify at 91b7e3e has no such action (DangerZone deletes only; NavbarDeleteTeam is the same deletion), so none is ported. **Audit log** (`/team/audit-log`, Team\AuditLog): events-worker's `GET /v1/organizations/{org}/audit/team-log` (through api-edge's audit facade), `audit.read` — owner and admin, as Coolify's `isAdminOfTeam` (a denial is a 404, shown as Coolify's 403 page). It reads the org's `events.audit_entries` of the last **90 days** (AuditEvent::pruneExpired), at most 5,000, with the actor's and a named member's display name and email from `identity.users` (read only), and does AuditLog::render(): the search (event, description, actor, email; case-sensitive like Coolify's Postgres LIKE), the action options (the visible actions, Str::headline), the source filter (All sources, Web UI, API, MCP, Webhook), the four sorts, 25 a page clamped to 10–100, "No activity found". Freeploy's team events Coolify records too are shown as Coolify records them — `organization.created|updated|deleted` → `ui.team.*` ("<team name> Updated"), `membership.updated` → `ui.team_member.role_updated` ("<member> Role Updated"), `membership.removed` → `ui.team_member.removed`, `invite.created|revoked` → `ui.team_invitation.*` ("Team Invitation Created"), `invite.accepted` → `ui.team_invitation.accepted` ("<accepting user> Accepted") — held to the rows Coolify's own PHP writes for them (`tests/events-worker/fixtures/audit-log/goldens.json`, emitted by `emit.php` in the scratch copy) and to Str::headline's answers. **Every other event** (API keys, billing, webhooks, config, support actions, `membership.added`, which Coolify folds into the accepted invitation) keeps Freeploy's own event name and description, its action the name's last part. Freeploy records no request origin, so the source is the actor's: a user is `ui` (a user's /api/v1 token too), an API key's service principal `api` (actor "Api Token"), a workflow or the system `system` (no Coolify filter lists it). Freeploy stores no field changes and no token name, so "View changes" and "Token: …" never show. **Boarding** (`/onboarding`, Boarding\Index, its view and components, hand-ported, "Coolify" read as "Freeploy"): welcome, the server type, the SSH key (an existing non-Git key, a pasted one — POST private-keys — or a generated ED25519 one), the server (POST servers with Coolify's random name), validation, the project ("My first project" or an existing one) and "Setup complete" → the new resource page of the project's first environment; the step is in the URL (`?step=`). **Not available yet** (the convention): "This machine" (Coolify's localhost — Freeploy runs on no server of yours), the four cloud providers, and "Start validation" (validating the server and installing Docker need the SSH executor); so that a team can reach the project step, the validation step offers a Freeploy "Continue without validation", and "Setup complete" shows Docker Engine "Not validated yet." instead of "Installed and running". A generated key is stored when its step opens (Freeploy's API generates and stores in one call): its public key shows with "Action Required", its private key stays on the server, and "Save SSH Key" saves the name and description. **show_boarding**: Freeploy stores none and registration does not set one, so a new team is **not sent to /onboarding** (as DP-18's seed skips it); the page is reached by its URL, "Skip setup" and "Go to dashboard" just open the dashboard, a member is sent there. Not ported: "Contact support" (Need Help) and the server-unreachable panel (no validation to fail). **Subscription pages: not applicable** — at 91b7e3e both Subscription\Index and Subscription\Show redirect to the dashboard unless `isCloud()`, and the sidebar shows Subscription only on Cloud; Freeploy's reference is self-hosted (DP1). No migration. The binding loop is unchanged.
- **The subscription pages (DP-82).** DP-75 found Coolify's pages redirect on a self-hosted instance; Freeploy is a hosted service, so they are ported as Coolify **Cloud** renders them, over Freeploy's billing: `/subscription/new` (Subscription\Index with PricingPlans) and `/subscription` (Subscription\Show with Actions), hand-ported (`apps/deploy-web/src/pages/Subscription.tsx`, the decisions in `src/lib/subscription.ts`), "Coolify" read as "Freeploy". **Backed by** billing-worker over api-edge's existing billing facade, no new route: `GET …/billing/summary` (the org's active subscription and plan; a child org's is its billing parent's) and `…/billing/plans`, `POST …/billing/checkout` (Subscribe; an existing subscriber is sent to the portal, as billing-worker already does), `…/billing/portal` (Open billing portal) and `…/billing/subscription/cancel` (Cancel at Period End). **One addition**: the summary answers `selfServe` — whether `BILLING_PROVIDER` resolves to a configured adapter (Polar with its access token and webhook secret; Stripe has none) and which plans it sells (the `POLAR_PRODUCT_MAP` codes of priced, recurring catalog plans), from env only (`src/self-serve.ts`, an optional field of `GetBillingSummaryResponse`; tested in `tests/billing-worker`). Stage's wrangler vars carry a product map, but no Polar secret is in the deployment's inventory, so stage is expected to answer `available: false`: the pricing then shows "Not available yet" ("Self-serve billing is not available yet: no billing provider is configured for this environment.") and no subscribe button, and the plan page offers no portal or cancellation — nothing is faked; a checkout would have answered 503. **Roles.** Coolify shows these pages to `isAdminOfTeam` / `isAdmin()` (owner, admin) and sends a member away; billing-worker already enforces `billing.manage` / `billing.read`, which policy-worker grants **owner and billing_admin** — not admin, not builder or viewer. That is kept (no role changed): an owner or billing_admin sees the pages; an admin, builder or viewer gets billing-worker's 404, which Index shows as its "Insufficient Permissions" section ("You are not an admin so you cannot manage your Team's subscription.") and Show as its redirect to the dashboard. **States.** Index: the skeleton ("Loading your subscription status"), Insufficient Permissions, a paid and paid-up subscription → `/subscription` (`isSubscriptionActive()`), a `past_due` one → "Payment failed" with the portal, else "No active subscription" and the pricing. Show: who may not read billing → the dashboard, a team without a paid subscription (Free is every team's starting plan, not a subscription) → `/subscription/new`, else Actions' "Plan and billing" (the plan's name, "Monthly billing", "Active" or "Cancelling at end of period", "Next billing"/"Cancels on" the period end as Carbon's `M j, Y`) and "Manage subscription". **Departures.** Polar for Stripe (Actions' `subscriptionProvider() === 'stripe'` branch is Freeploy's Polar branch; "Unable to confirm checkout with Polar…"); the catalog's plans for Coolify's one pay-as-you-go plan: a section per plan sold (Pro $20, Business $99, per month, cheapest first) with "Subscribe monthly" and the features its entitlements grant (held to `PLAN_CATALOG` in `apps/deploy-web/test/subscription.test.ts`), no Monthly/Yearly switch (no yearly prices), Free and Enterprise (contact sales) not sold; the warning reads "Your team is on the Free plan. Choose a plan to raise its limits." where Coolify's says one must subscribe to keep using Cloud (a Freeploy team keeps working on Free). Not ported, for want of a Freeploy counterpart: "Active servers: n / m paid", the server-limit callout and "Adjust server limit" with its price preview (Freeploy's plans are not priced per server), "Cancel Immediately" with its refund and delete-account options and the Refund section (billing-worker cancels at period end only and has no refunds), "Resume Subscription" (no endpoint: the Polar portal resumes), Coolify's password step (the console's confirmation has none; the team name is typed as Coolify asks), "contact us" / "Need help? Contact us." (no contact page), and the sidebar's Subscription entry (Coolify shows it only on Cloud; the reference is self-hosted, so adding it would change every page's sidebar against the reference's capture — the pages are reached by URL). The confirmation's actions are Coolify's, with "at Polar" added to the first: Polar keeps the subscription to the end of the period, and since DP-83 billing-worker does too — Polar's `subscription.canceled` (cancel at period end) keeps the plan and records `cancel_at`, so the page shows "Cancelling at end of period"; the team moves to Free when access ends (`subscription.revoked`, or a subscription that has ended). The visual gate cannot compare these two routes with the reference (its capture redirects them); their markup follows the blades. No migration; no binding added — the billing, membership, notifications and events loop is unchanged.
- **/select-team (DP-102).** routes/web.php's `team.select` (App\Livewire\SelectTeam, `select-team.blade.php` in `<x-auth.shell>`) is `SelectTeamPage` in `pages/ConfigSubpages.tsx` (its decisions in `lib/config-subpages.ts`), on the org switching the console already has: the org a user works in is `users.last_org_slug` (the frame falls back to their first org), set with `PATCH /v1/auth/profile { lastOrgSlug }` as accepting an invitation (DP-69) does. mount(): with a current org (the last one, when it is still theirs) or at most one org there is nothing to pick — resolveStoredTeam()'s team (the last, else the only one) is stored when it is not already, and the page goes to `/`; otherwise the orgs as Coolify lists them (initial, name, arrow) and selectTeam() stores the picked one and goes to `/`, an org the user is not in doing nothing. No endpoint is added. *Departures*: Coolify's current team is the session's, which a sign-in leaves unset for a user of several teams so that DecideWhatToDoWithUser sends them here; Freeploy has no per-session team and the console always resolves one, so nothing sends a user to /select-team — it is reached by its URL. The title reads "Select a team | Freeploy".

## Notes on DP11

- **The environment canvas, read only (DP-91).** Coolify's environment canvas (Beta) — `/project/{uuid}/environment/{env_uuid}/canvas` (`project.environment.canvas`, `livewire/project/environment-canvas.blade.php`, mounted by `app.js` into `[data-environment-canvas]` and fed by `Api\EnvironmentCanvasController::show` at `/canvas/api`) — is ported to React, not mounted as a Svelte island: the design (§3.1) planned Svelte 5 islands, but deploy-web is React and the task asked for React components, so no Svelte dependency is added. Coolify's canvases use no canvas library (Svelte only; no Svelte Flow at `91b7e3e` despite `app.js`'s comment), so nothing else was needed and no dependency was added. *Ported* (`apps/deploy-web/src/components/canvas/**`, `src/lib/environment-canvas.ts`, `src/lib/canvas-pan-zoom.ts`, `src/pages/EnvironmentCanvas.tsx`, each file marked "Derived from …"): `BetaBadge`, `CanvasControls` (zoom, fit, Refresh, Cmd/Ctrl +/-/0), `environment/Icon`, `StatusDot`, `NodeIcon` and `Zone` whole; `EnvironmentCanvas`, `Card`, `Panel` and `Toolbar` for the environment view only; `environment-canvas.js`' helpers that view uses (zones, the grid layout with saved positions winning, zone frames, link curves, status tone, label and word, summary, unreachable servers, fit, saved view) and `canvas-core.js`' pan and zoom. Markup, classes and copy are Coolify's (brand words swapped); the toolbar docks in the zoom controls bar on desktop and sits in a box above the canvas below 1024px, as `placeBar()` does; the views group (List view, Shared variables, Settings, New resource) is the Blade page's. `Layout` gains `fullBleed` (layouts.app's `$fullBleed`: `app-shell-canvas flex h-dvh flex-col` and `app-main-full`), through a new `shellClass` rule in `tools/parity/port/patches/AppShellMarkup.json` applied by hand to the generated `LayoutMarkup.tsx` (no capture was regenerated). *Data*: `GET /v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/projects/{uuid}/environments/{env_uuid}/canvas` in deploy-core-worker (`applications/canvas.ts`, a third environment page beside `new` and `resources`, `deploy.resource.read`, inside `withOrg`, a denial 404), in `EnvironmentCanvas::payload()`'s shape: zones per server (uuid, name, `is_reachable`, `is_usable`, link; `ip` always null), cards in `loadResources()`' order (applications, services, then databases engine by engine) with Coolify's subtitles (`BUILD_PACK_NAMES` · branch or image, `Service · N containers`, `DATABASE_ENGINE_NAMES` · image), URLs, a service's containers and its status from its parts (`serviceStatus`), links to their pages and `resource_logo()`'s build or engine logos. It is new because no existing read gives each resource's server identity and reachability or a service's parts by name; `packages/db` gains `listEnvironmentServiceCanvasRows` beside `listEnvironmentServiceResources` (tests/db). No migration, no binding, no api-edge change (the deploy facade forwards the path). *Departures*: **view only** — Freeploy stores no canvas positions or links yet (the `canvas_positions` column and `resource_links` table exist but nothing writes them), so `can_edit` is false, `positions` and `links` are empty, and "View only" says "You can look around. Moving cards and drawing links are not available yet." instead of the admins-only wording; dragging cards and zones, the link port, drawing, labelling and removing links are not ported. **No live status**: Coolify listens to `ApplicationStatusChanged`, `ServiceStatusChanged` and `DatabaseStatusChanged` over Echo; Freeploy has no realtime channel yet, so Refresh and the tab's return reload. **New resource** (and the empty state's "Add resource") opens the New Resource page, since the deploy canvas is not ported; the view is not remembered in a cookie (RemembersCanvasView). Cards have no `extra_zones` (additional servers), image scan marker, domain favicon or service template logo; a Git application shows `public-repo`'s logo (Freeploy keeps no `source_type`). The canvas has no deploy, start or stop action of its own (Coolify's environment canvas has none), so no SSH-backed action is called. *Not ported*, left `planned` in `parity/svelte.json`: the deploy canvas (`canvas/deploy/*`: the `/canvas` flow builder that creates and deploys resources, with its run panel), `AddSection` and `Select` (the connect and server views), `Deployments` (the dashboard canvas) and `EmptyCard` (project and server views), and every other canvas route (projects, project, servers, server, dashboard, shared variables, tags). Tests: `apps/deploy-web/test/environment-canvas.test.ts` (the model from an API graph) and `routes.test.ts`; `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/environment-canvas.test.ts` (graph, routing, authorization); `tests/db` (the new query and its org isolation). Not yet run against the reference or on stage.
- **Editing on the environment canvas (DP-92).** Those who may edit the environment — Coolify's `$user->can('update', $environment)`, i.e. `deploy.resource.write` (owner and admin; a builder is Coolify's member and reads only) — move cards and zones and draw, label and remove visual-only links; everyone else sees "View only" with Coolify's own wording again ("You can look around. Only team admins can move cards and edit links.", exact for Freeploy's roles). *Endpoints* (deploy-core-worker `applications/canvas.ts`, routed by `handleEnvironmentCanvas` in `handlers/applications.ts`; each asks `deploy.resource.write` first and answers a denial with the 404 every denial gets, then runs inside `withOrg`; the projects route now takes six segments, for these only): `PATCH …/projects/{uuid}/environments/{env_uuid}/canvas/positions` (`updatePositions`: `positions` present|array|max:1000 and no other field, then — for the environment's own cards only, other uuids dropped first — `positions.*` required|array and each `x`/`y` required|integer|between:-100000,100000 in Laravel's wildcard order; the environment's row locked, the saved positions merged with these and `filterPositions()`ed to integers of the environment's cards, answered as `{positions}`), `POST …/canvas/links` (`storeLink`: `source`/`target` required|string|max:255, `different:source`, `label` nullable|string|max:64, "This field is not allowed."; "The source must be a resource of this environment.", "The target must be a resource of this environment.", "These resources are already linked." — also when `resource_links_unique_direction` refuses a second request, through `ON CONFLICT … DO NOTHING` so the transaction lives on; 201 `{id, source, target, label}` with the label trimmed, empty as null), `PATCH …/canvas/links/{link_uuid}` (`updateLink`: `label` present|nullable|string|max:64; "Link not found." also when an end is no longer a resource of the environment) and `DELETE …/canvas/links/{link_uuid}` (`destroyLink`: "Link deleted."). Every body goes through `validateIncomingRequest()` (400 "Invalid request." with `details.error`: "Content-Type must be application/json.", "Invalid JSON.", "Empty JSON.") and TrimStrings/ConvertEmptyStringsToNull (the existing `readApiBody`, `validateRules` with Laravel's messages); "Project not found." and "Environment not found." are Coolify's. Answers are Freeploy's envelope: `{data}`, or `{error: {message: "Validation failed.", details: {fields}}}`. `GET …/canvas` now carries `positions` (the stored ones of its cards) and `links` (ordered by id, both ends among the cards, by uuid), and `can_edit` is the caller's `deploy.resource.write`. *Data*: `packages/db` `deploy/canvas.ts` — `listEnvironmentCanvasResources` (`resourceIndex()`: applications, services, then the standalone databases in `DATABASE_RELATIONS`' order, soft-deleted ones left out, with their morph classes), `findEnvironmentCanvasPositions`, `lockEnvironmentCanvasPositions` (`FOR UPDATE`), `saveEnvironmentCanvasPositions` (with `updated_at`, as `save()` does), and the links' list, find, exists, insert, relabel and delete. `deleteEnvironment` and `deleteProject` now remove the environment's links (Coolify's `resource_links.environment_id` is `cascadeOnDelete`; Freeploy's schema has no foreign keys). No migration: `deploy.environments.canvas_positions` (JSON) and `deploy.resource_links` (Coolify's 11 columns, `resource_links_unique_direction` from 660) already have Coolify's shape. *Console* (`components/canvas/environment/*`, `lib/environment-canvas.ts`, new `lib/canvas-api.ts`): `EnvironmentCanvas.svelte`'s editing ported — a card drag (4 px threshold, it stops before it covers another card or zone: `canvas-core.js`' `freeSpot` and `dragRules` without the server view's groups), a zone drag by its header (its cards move with it; it stops at another zone), `queueSave`/`flushSave` 400 ms after a move and with `keepalive` on `pagehide` and on leaving the page, the `settling` bookkeeping (saved moves stay until a load that started after the save returns them; loads during a drag wait for it to end), the card's drag dot that draws a dashed link onto a drop-target card (`canConnect`, `canLink`; "These resources are already linked." before asking), pending links drawn as moving dashes, a new link opening its panel with the label field focused, the label saved on blur and Enter (Escape restores it) with "Label saved.", "Remove link" with "Link removed.", and `api()`'s `ApiError` message (the API's message, then each field error). `Card` is ported whole (also the connect view's source badge and the dashboard's deployment pill, which this canvas never shows). *Departures*: the environment is found by uuid only (Coolify's `environment_name_or_uuid` also takes its name; the console sends the uuid); `created_by` stays null (Freeploy's users are identity's, not deploy rows); a denial is 404, not Coolify's 403. *Cut, rows left `planned`*: **the deploy canvas** (`canvas/deploy/*`: DeployCanvas, Dropdown, Edges, Field, Icon, Node, PaletteItem, RunPanel, with `deploy-canvas.js` and `state.svelte.js`) — a flow builder of its own that creates resources through Coolify's `/api/v1` create, settings and environment-variable endpoints and follows the deployment logs; porting it onto Freeploy's New Resource services and the 501 deploy is a task of its own, so New resource and "Add resource" still open the New Resource page. **AddSection and Select** belong to the shared variable, tag and server canvases (the connect view's add lists, the project / environment filters), **EmptyCard** to the project, server and dashboard canvases (an empty environment's placeholder card; the empty environment canvas shows its own "No resources in this environment yet" box, which DP-91 ported) and **Deployments** to the dashboard canvas: none of those canvases is built, so these are not ported. `EnvironmentCanvas`, `Panel` and `Toolbar` stay `in_progress` (taskKey DP-92): complete for the environment canvas, but their server, connect and dashboard branches are not. Still not ported: the live status over Echo. Tests: `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/environment-canvas-edit.test.ts` (each action's rules and messages, the saved layout in GET and `can_edit`, every save a 404 that runs nothing for a reader, another org's project not found, routing); `tests/db/src/deploy-canvas.test.ts` (the queries, the unique direction, the cascades, row-level security); `apps/deploy-web/test/environment-canvas-edit.test.ts` (freeSpot, card and zone drags, saved positions, canConnect/canLink, the error message). Not yet run against the reference or on stage.
- **The deploy canvas (DP-94).** Coolify's `/canvas` (`deploy-canvas`, `App\Livewire\DeployCanvas`, `livewire/deploy-canvas.blade.php`, mounted by `canvas/deploy/index.js`) is ported to React at the same route (`routes.ts` `deploy-canvas`, `App.tsx`, `pages/DeployCanvas.tsx` in the full-bleed layout), opened with `?project=…&environment=…` from the New Resource page's "Deploy canvas" card and, now as in Coolify, from the environment canvas's New resource and "Add resource" (DP-91 sent those to the New Resource page). *Ported* (`components/canvas/deploy/*`, `lib/deploy-canvas.ts`, each file marked "Derived from …"): `DeployCanvas`, `Dropdown`, `Edges`, `Field`, `Icon`, `Node`, `PaletteItem` and `RunPanel` whole, `styles.js` verbatim (injected as Coolify injects it, so `light-dark()` survives the build), `deploy-canvas.js`' step types, fields, flow rules (`canFollow`, `planOf`, `sourceAbove`, `withDeploy`, `withTarget`, `withoutSecrets`, `uniqueLabels`, `logoOf`) and storage (`coolify-canvas:flow|view|result|deployed|collapsed|mobile-notice-dismissed`, secrets never stored), and `state.svelte.js`' runtime (palette click and drag, card drags that stop before another card — `freeSpot` —, port drags with the "Add the next step" menu, "Nothing can follow this step.", "That step cannot follow this one.", "Connection removed.", the only-option selects, the "New project" / "New environment" rows with `"…" added.`, fit, the result pill, Reset through Coolify's confirmation, `validate()`'s toast and marks, and the run panel's steps, tabs and logs). Coolify's Svelte `$state` is a plain object with a version counter read through `useSyncExternalStore`. Copy is Coolify's with the brand swapped ("Open in Freeploy", "Saved in Freeploy. Deploy them from there or from here.", the Domains note, Reset's warning). *Server side*: Coolify has no plan controller — the browser runs each step against `/api/v1` at `/canvas/api` with the session, which `UseSessionTeamApiToken` turns into an in-memory token of the page's team. Freeploy keeps that shape in deploy-core-worker (`handlers/deploy-canvas.ts`, `applications/deploy-canvas.ts`, routed by `ORG_DEPLOY_CANVAS_RE`): each call is answered **in-process by its own `/api/v1` handler** (`handleApiV1`, DP-70…DP-90) for the console's actor with the abilities `read`, `write` and `deploy` (not `read:sensitive`: nothing the canvas shows is sensitive), so the rules, messages and policy checks are `/api/v1`'s (DP-71/DP-73/DP-74/DP-87, over the services New Resource uses). *Endpoints* (each asks `deploy.resource.write` first and answers a denial with the 404 every denial gets; every `/api/v1` call runs inside `withOrg`): `GET /v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/canvas` (`render()`'s data: `can_add`, `serviceTemplates()` as `[key, headline]` by name, `serviceLogos()` without the default logo), `GET …/deploy/canvas/options/{projects, projects/{uuid}/environments, servers, servers/{uuid}/destinations, security/keys}` (the `LOADERS`' lists, mapped on the server to `[value, label]` so no key material reaches the browser; build-only servers left out, " (unreachable)", "name · network"), `POST …/deploy/canvas/projects[/{uuid}/environments]` (`/api/v1`'s create with the body as sent; `{uuid}`, 201), and `POST …/deploy/canvas/run` (`{action: save|deploy, plan}` — the plan `planOf()` builds: start, servers, lines `{server, source, settings}`, deploy; read raw, so a Dockerfile or Compose file reaches `/api/v1` byte for byte). The run checks the body ("The action field is required.", "The selected action is invalid.", "The plan is not valid." — Freeploy's), that each step follows the one before (`canFollow()`: "That step cannot follow this one."), then `validate()` ("Connect a Server right after Start.", "Connect a source step (…) to a Server.", "`<Title>`: fill in `<labels>`.", answered as 422 with `problems` and the `invalid` steps), then runs `runFlow()`'s order: Start ("Project and environment set."), each Server ("Server picked."), then each line — its source (`POST /applications/public` or `/private-deploy-key` with `common()`'s `project_uuid`, `environment_uuid`, `server_uuid`, `destination_uuid`, `instant_deploy: false`, `build_pack`, `ports_exposes`, and a Domains step's `domains` with `autogenerate_domain: false`; `/applications/dockerimage` with `splitImage()`; `/applications/dockerfile` in base64; `/services` with `type` or `docker_compose_raw`; `/databases/{engine}`) then its settings (Build: `PATCH /applications/{uuid}` or "Nothing to change."; Variables: `PATCH /{applications,services}/{uuid}/envs/bulk` with `parseEnvVars()`, "N variables saved." or "No variables."; Domains: "Set at creation: …" or `PATCH` "Domains saved: …") — and the first failure stops the run with `ApiError`'s message (the API's message, then each field error), the steps after it "Waiting". Last the Deploy step: "Save only" answers "Saved N resources. Not deployed."; Deploy calls `POST /deploy {uuid}` per resource (`deployAll()`), which answers **Freeploy's 501** ("Deploying the application needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet.", "Deploying the service …", "Starting the database …") before anything is queued, so the Deploy step fails with the messages joined by " · " and each resource's tab is failed with its own — as Coolify shows a failed deployment. Nothing is faked: no deployment row, no "Deployed.". The answer carries each step's state and message, the resources created (source, label, kind, uuid) and the deploys; the console shows them on the cards, the edges and the run panel. *Departures*: the console sends the plan once, so the statuses arrive together when the run returns (Start shows "Running…" meanwhile) instead of one by one; the option lists answer `[value, label]` pairs instead of `/api/v1` rows; a plan has at most 100 steps (one run is one request; otherwise "The plan is not valid."); the logos are Freeploy's files (no versioned URLs); a created resource's Open goes to its Freeploy page; the Dockerfile application's port is what `/api/v1` derives (`get_port_from_dockerfile()`, else 80), as in Coolify. *Cut*: the Git repository step's **GitHub app** access (wired since by DP-95, below); **following a deployment's logs** (`followDeployment()`, `GET /deployments/{uuid}`, the 2 s poll, "Logs are hidden: …"): no deployment starts, so the resource's tab shows the 501's line; the result pill's addresses after a successful Deploy (never reached while deploying is a 501; "Save only" shows "Open in Freeploy", as Coolify does). SQLite stays in the engine list as Coolify's (it has no `/databases/sqlite` route; the step fails with `/api/v1`'s 404). The eight `canvas/deploy/*` rows are `ported` (taskKey DP-94); `deploy-canvas.js`, `state.svelte.js` and `styles.js` have no rows. No migration, no new query (so no tests/db change), no binding, no dependency, no api-edge change (the deploy facade forwards the path); `handlers/projects.ts`' and `handlers/private-keys.ts`' SQL stores are exported for the tests. Tests: `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/deploy-canvas.test.ts` (over the API goldens' PGlite fixture: the run's order and the rows it creates — an image application with its variables and domain, a deploy-key application with its build settings, a one-click service with variables, a Dockerfile in base64, a PostgreSQL database, a destination —, the 501 deploy step with nothing queued, a failed step stopping the run, `validate()`'s problems and marks, the body's rules, the page's data, the option lists, New project and New environment, every route a 404 that runs nothing for a reader, another org's project not found, routing); `apps/deploy-web/test/deploy-canvas.test.ts` (Coolify's `deploy-canvas.test.js` cases, `validate()`, the plan sent, a restored flow, the option lists) and `routes.test.ts`. Rendered once in headless Chrome with a stubbed API (cards, edges, palette, the run panel's failed Deploy step and tab). Not yet run against the reference or on stage.
- **The deploy canvas's GitHub app access (DP-95).** DP-94's cut, wired over DP-93's routes as `deploy-canvas.js` does. *Console* (`lib/deploy-canvas.ts`): the Git repository step's Access offers Public, **GitHub app** and Deploy key again, with Coolify's fields in its order — `ghApp` ("GitHub app"), `ghRepo` ("Repository") and `ghBranch` ("Branch") selects, shown for `mode === "github"`, required, the first two `rerender` (a new App empties the repository and branch, a new repository the branch) — and `LOADERS.ghApps`, `repos(app)` and `branches(app, repo)` with `loaderKey()`'s `gh`, `repos:{app}` and `br:{app}:{repo}`; the App's value is Coolify's `"{id}|{uuid}"`, the id naming it in the lists, the uuid in the create; `logoOf()` already showed the GitHub App logo and `resourceLabel()` the repository. *Server* (`handlers/deploy-canvas.ts`, `ORG_DEPLOY_CANVAS_RE` now seven segments deep): three more option lists, built on the server as `[value, label]` like DP-94's, asking `deploy.resource.write` first (a denial 404 that reaches neither `/api/v1` nor GitHub) and answered in process by `/api/v1` — `GET …/deploy/canvas/options/github-apps` (`GET /github-apps`, the Apps that are not public and have an App ID, `["{id}|{uuid}", name]`), `…/github-apps/{id}/repositories` (`[full_name, full_name]` of DP-93's `load_repositories()`: the installation token, every page, sorted by name) and `…/github-apps/{id}/repositories/{owner}/{repo}/branches` (`[name, name]` of `load_branches()`); GitHub is reached through `/api/v1`'s injectable fetch (`ApiV1Context.providers`), and its failures are `/api/v1`'s message and status ("Error loading branches from GitHub.", "GitHub app not found", the token's 500 "Server Error"). The run (`applications/deploy-canvas.ts`): `validate()` asks for the App, the repository and the branch in GitHub mode ("Git repository: fill in GitHub app, Repository, Branch."), and the Git step calls `POST /applications/private-github-app` with Coolify's body — `common()`, `build_pack`, `ports_exposes`, `github_app_uuid` (the value's uuid), `git_repository` (the full name), `git_branch` — so DP-93's checks, `githubAppRepository()` and its messages ("Repository not found or not accessible by the GitHub App.", the 429) are the step's; DP-87's departures stay (Docker Compose, instant deploy: 501, never reached from the canvas, which sends `instant_deploy: false`). Tests: `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/deploy-canvas.test.ts` (GitHub faked per case: the Apps' list and its filter, the repositories' pages and their JWT and installation token, the branches, GitHub's 404, an unknown App, a run creating the application from `acme/private` with its source, repository id and variables, a repository the App cannot read stopping the run with nothing created, `validate()`'s problem, the three lists a denied 404 that asks GitHub nothing, the seven-segment route); `apps/deploy-web/test/deploy-canvas.test.ts` (the three modes and the fields each shows, `validate()`, the plan sent, the three loaders' paths and keys). No migration, no binding, no dependency, no api-edge change. Not yet run on stage.
- **The projects, servers, dashboard, shared variable and tag canvases (DP-98).** The canvases Coolify serves from `EnvironmentCanvas.svelte`'s server and connect branches are ported to deploy-web over DP-91/DP-92's React canvas, each a full-bleed page (`pages/CanvasViews.tsx`, `routes.ts` `CanvasViewsPageKey`, `App.tsx`): `/projects/canvas` (`project.canvas.index`, `ProjectsCanvas`), `/project/{uuid}/canvas` (`project.canvas`), `/servers/canvas` (`server.canvas.index`), `/server/{uuid}/canvas` (`server.canvas`, with the server's navbar docked in the top bar), `/dashboard/canvas` (`dashboard.canvas`, `data-poll="5"`), the four shared variable canvases (`shared-variables.{team,project,environment,server}.canvas`, `data-mode="variables"`) and `/canvas/tags` (`tags.canvas`, `data-mode="tags"`). The pages' views are the Blade ones moved into the toolbar: `<x-canvas-view-toggle on-canvas>`'s "List view" link (`?view=table`, or `?view=list` where the page has one list view) and the actions — New project, Settings and New environment, New server, Add variable, New tag — opening the list pages' own dialogs (`AddEmpty`, `NewEnvironmentForm`, `AddVariable` and `NewTagForm`, now exported). *Console* (`components/canvas/environment/*`, `lib/environment-canvas.ts`, each file marked "Derived from …"): `EnvironmentCanvas` gains the server view (`data-endpoint`: project / environment group frames from `groupFrames()`, a group dragged by its header and a card dragged inside its group — `startGroupDrag`, `movableGroupOf`, the group's `fits` — both saved on the cards' environment canvas through DP-92's `…/canvas/positions` with the group's `shift` (`saveGroup`), zones dragged and saved as the project's or server's place (`saveZones`, the zones still placed automatically saved where they are), the `settling` bookkeeping for card, saved and zone moves, a server's unmanaged containers and usage loaded after it renders and the usage polled every 30 s, `data-poll`, `showNode()`, EmptyCard placeholders, `statusText`) and the connect view (`data-mode`: Coolify's `CONNECT_TEXT` wording, links that add a variable or a tag through the canvas's own `…/canvas/links`, `removeLink`, `setLinks` regrouping the cards at once, the link titles "A → B (click to open)", the source's "used by" badge, `?select=`), and the project / environment filters kept in the URL (`filterOptions`, `filterGraph`). `lib/environment-canvas.ts` gains `environment-canvas.js`' server and connect helpers: `layoutBlock`, `groupKey`, `layoutServerView` (zones with a saved place first, the stacked ones clear of them), `layoutServerZone`, `layoutConnectView`, `groupByUse`, `groupFrames`, the group-aware `zoneBoxes` and `dragRules`, `canConnect`'s server and connect rules, `filterOptions`, `filterGraph`, `CONNECT_TEXT`, `environment-order.js`' `compareEnvironmentNames`, and `Deployments.svelte`'s status labels and `ago()`. `Panel` gains the connect view's source, resource and source → resource panels; `Toolbar` its filters through the new `Select` (Coolify's listbox); new `AddSection`, `EmptyCard` and `Deployments` (the folded state in `coolify-canvas-deployments-open`). The seven rows `EnvironmentCanvas`, `Panel`, `Toolbar`, `AddSection`, `Select`, `EmptyCard` and `Deployments` are `ported` in `parity/svelte.json` (taskKey DP-98). *Endpoints* (deploy-core-worker `applications/canvas-views.ts`, `handlers/canvas-views.ts`; routed from `ORG_DEPLOY_CANVAS_RE` when the first segment is `api`, so the paths are Coolify's `/canvas/api` ones): `GET …/deploy/canvas/api/projects/canvas` (`ProjectCanvas::teamPayload()`: one zone per project by name with `position`, cards in their project's zone with the environment as `context`), `GET …/projects/{uuid}/canvas` (`payload()`: a zone per server, the placeholders on the server most of the project's cards use, or "Empty environments"), `GET …/servers/canvas` and `…/servers/{uuid}/canvas` (`ServerCanvas::payload()`: every server by name with `details()` — os, arch, cpus, memory, uptime from `server_metadata`, the proxy unless NONE, `usage_endpoint` when reachable with metrics — and `position` on the every-server canvas, cards with "Project / Environment", placeholders for empty environments and for servers without cards, the links with both ends shown, `containers_endpoint` on one server), `GET …/dashboard/canvas` (`dashboardPayload()`: each application's oldest in-progress or queued deployment on its card, the active deployments by status then id and the 8 latest others), `PATCH …/{projects,servers,dashboard}/canvas/positions` (`updatePositions()`: positions required|array|max:1000, each x and y required|integer|between:-100000,100000 in Laravel's wildcard order, the org's projects or servers saved, others left out; `{positions}`), `GET …/servers/{uuid}/canvas/containers` (none for a server that is not functional, else **the SSH executor's 501** "Listing the containers on the server needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet.") and `…/usage` (nulls without metrics, else 501 "Reading the server's usage from Sentinel needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet."), `GET …/shared-variables/{team | project|environment|server/{uuid}}/canvas` (`SharedVariableCanvas::payload()`: the scope's variables by key — the server's `COOLIFY_SERVER_UUID`/`_NAME` left out — and deleted ones as `missing:KEY`, the resources by name — a server's, those whose main server it is (a service's `server_id`) — and one link per variable and resource from the `{{scope.KEY}}` references in the resources' saved variables, decrypted on the worker and never sent; "Project not found.", "Environment not found.", "Server not found."), `POST …/canvas/links` (`storeLink()`: source and target required|string|max:255, "The source must be a shared variable of this scope.", "The target must be a resource that can use this shared variable.", "X already uses KEY.", "X already has a variable named KEY. Rename or remove it first."; the new variable through `createVariable()` — encrypted, with the shared variable's is_multiline and is_literal and an application's preview copy, as the model's hooks do — answered as its link, 201), `DELETE …/canvas/links/{link_id}` (`destroyLink()`: "Link not found.", "KEYS on X uses {{scope.KEY}} inside a longer value. Edit it on the resource.", `EnvironmentVariableProtection::isEnvironmentVariableUsedInDockerCompose()`'s "Cannot remove KEY from X. …", the variables and their preview copies deleted, "Link removed."), and `GET /tags/canvas`, `POST /tags/canvas/links`, `DELETE /tags/canvas/links/{link_id}` (`TagCanvas`: the tags by name, a link per tag on a resource; "The source must be a tag of this team.", "The target must be a resource of this team.", "X already has the T tag.", the tag attached or detached, "Link removed."). Reads ask `deploy.resource.read` and `can_edit` is `deploy.resource.write`; every write asks `deploy.resource.write` first; a denial is 404 that runs nothing; every read and write runs inside `withOrg`; bodies go through `validateIncomingRequest()` (DP-92's `incoming()`). *Data*: `packages/db` `deploy/canvas-views.ts` (`listCanvasEnvironments` with their `canvas_positions`, `listCanvasProjects`, `listCanvasServers`, `saveProjectCanvasPosition`, `saveServerCanvasPosition`, `listOrgResourceLinks`, `listOrgTaggables` in attach order (`ctid`, as DP-81 reads the pivot), `listCanvasEnvironmentVariables`, `listCanvasDeployments`) and `listOrgApplications`, `listOrgDatabases`, `listOrgServiceCanvasRows` beside their environment versions; `applications/canvas.ts`' `canvasGraph()` takes a base per row and its node's `saved`. No migration (`projects.canvas_position`, `servers.canvas_position`, `taggables`, the variables and the deployment queue have Coolify's shape), no binding, no dependency, no api-edge change (the deploy facade forwards the path). *Departures*: the paths are under `/v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/canvas/api` and answer Freeploy's envelope; a denial is 404, and a write is refused before its body is validated (Coolify validates, then authorizes); every `@can` of the pages (`createAnyResource`, `update` on the project or the variables' owner, `create` on Tag) and the team-wide routes' `access.team-wide` are `deploy.resource.write` / `deploy.resource.read`, so the actions show once the canvas has loaded its `can_edit`; zones carry no IP (the console is never granted `read:sensitive`), cards no `extra_zones`, image scan marker, favicon or service template logo (DP-91), and a service with a queued or running start is not shown "starting" (`startingServiceUuids()` reads Spatie's activity log, which Freeploy has not); a service's zone is its destination's server, as on the environment canvas; no live status over Echo (Refresh, the tab's return and the dashboard's poll reload); the server canvas shows the server's navbar but not its menu (Freeploy's server menu is the settings column, which the full-bleed canvas has no room for); the shared variable canvases do not carry `<x-shared-variables.sidebar />`'s search items; the view is not remembered in a cookie (RemembersCanvasView); an owner of another org sends the page back to its list (Coolify's `firstOrFail()` 404 page); the dashboard canvas's "List view" leads to `/dashboard`, which deploy-web has not ported. *Cut*: no canvas; only what runs a command on the server answers 501 (above). Tests: `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/canvas-views.test.ts` (every payload, the positions' rules, the tag and shared variable links' rules and messages, the 501s, authorization, org isolation, routing); `tests/db/src/deploy-canvas-views.test.ts` (the queries and row-level security); `apps/deploy-web/test/canvas-views.test.ts` (Coolify's `environment-canvas.test.js` server, variables and projects canvas cases, the wording, the deployment rows), `routes.test.ts` and `resource-pages.test.ts` (their "not ported" canvas assertions now name the pages). Not rendered in a browser, not run against the reference or on stage.
- **Maintenance Mode and an application's Swarm (DP-102).** routes/web.php's `project.application.maintenance`, `project.service.maintenance` (Configuration with `<livewire:project.shared.maintenance :resource>`) and `project.application.swarm` (Configuration with `<livewire:project.application.swarm>`, rendered only when `$application->destination?->server?->isSwarm()`) are `ApplicationMaintenancePage`, `ServiceMaintenancePage` and `ApplicationSwarmPage` in `pages/ConfigSubpages.tsx`, over `GET`/`PATCH /v1/organizations/{org}/deploy/{applications|services}/{uuid}/maintenance` and `…/applications/{uuid}/swarm` (`handlers/config-subpages.ts`, the rules in `applications/config-subpages.ts`; withOrg, deploy.resource.read / write, a denial a 404 that runs nothing). No migration: `applications.swarm_replicas`, `swarm_placement_constraints` and `application_settings.is_swarm_only_worker_nodes` are in 500, `is_maintenance_enabled` and `maintenance_page` of applications and services in 660; `packages/db/src/deploy/config-subpages.ts` reads them, the servers that run the resource (Application::maintenanceServers(): the destination's server, then the additional ones, each once; a service's server) and writes them. *Maintenance*: the section with its helper, the On/Off badge and the Enable/Disable maintenance confirmation (its title, button, warning and actions, word for word) only when getIsSupportedProperty() holds (a Traefik or Caddy proxy on a server that is not a Swarm one), else its notice; the custom page with Maintenance's rule (`nullable`, `string`, `proxyPageSizeRule('maintenance page')`: "The maintenance page must not be larger than 1024 KB.", in bytes), `filled()` storing a blank page as null, Reset to default, and "Maintenance mode enabled." / "Maintenance mode disabled." / "Maintenance page saved." / "Default maintenance page restored.". syncMaintenancePage() writes the proxy's maintenance files on every functional server that runs the resource: a toggle, and a save or reset while maintenance is on, answer 501 "Applying the maintenance page on the server needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." after the rules, with nothing stored; with no functional server Coolify's sync does nothing and the row is stored, as here. *Swarm*: Replicas, Node placement ("Worker nodes only" / "Manager and worker nodes", saved on change as instantSave()) and Custom placement constraints (stored base64-encoded when PHP-truthy, decoded on load), the deprecated badge and callout, Swarm's `#[Validate]` rules with Laravel's messages and "Swarm settings updated.". *Departures*: a value Swarm's typed properties cannot hold (an int, a ?string, a bool) gets Laravel's message for that type ("must be an integer", "must be a string", "must be true or false") where Livewire fails to hydrate; a Swarm save for an application whose server is not a Swarm one answers 404 (Coolify renders no component there); the Swarm deprecation notice drops Coolify's v5 plans as the server's Swarm page does; "the Coolify default maintenance page" reads "the Freeploy default". *Cut*: the audit log entries of Swarm's saveApplicationSettingsWithAudit() and the `configurationChanged` event (Freeploy has no configuration checker yet).

## Notes on DP12

- **Platform admin (DP-79).** Coolify's instance-level pages (`/settings/*`, `/admin`, Team → Admin View: `isInstanceAdmin()`, and `/admin` the root user's) are the **platform operator**'s and no customer org's. *The operator*: Freeploy had no such role (admin-worker's support roles are header claims of an internal caller, and admin-worker is not reachable from api-edge), so one is added without a migration: the Freeploy user ids (`usr_…`) in policy-worker's runtime secret `PLATFORM_OPERATORS` (`orun secrets set`, pushed by the secrets-live step; unset = nobody). Three platform actions, `platform.settings.read|update` and `platform.admin.read` (packages/policy-engine `PLATFORM_ACTIONS`), are decided by policy-worker from that list alone — never from org roles or the memberships a caller sends — and only for a user subject; `authorize()` denies them to every org role. *Routes*: api-edge `platform-facade.ts` forwards `/v1/platform/*` (GET, PATCH) to deploy-core-worker with the actor and answers every failure to authenticate (none, unknown session, a Coolify API token — `/v1/auth/resolve` refuses them) with the same 404 as an unknown route; deploy-core-worker `handlers/platform.ts` asks policy-worker first and answers a denial with the platform's 404 (`GET /v1/platform/operator`, `GET|PATCH /v1/platform/settings`, `GET /v1/platform/admin`, `GET /v1/platform/admin/users`); membership-worker gains two internal, read-only routes for it over the existing binding (`POST /v1/internal/membership/platform/users`, `GET …/platform/subscribers`). No binding added; the billing, membership, notifications and events loop is unchanged. *Proof*: `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/platform.test.ts` (policy-worker's real router as the decision) — every route is a 404 that opens no store and asks membership nothing for a user who is owner, admin, builder, viewer or billing_admin of every org, for an API key's service principal with an owner role (Coolify's `root`), with the operator's id too, and with no actor; 200 for the operator; `tests/api-edge/src/platform-facade.test.ts` — a root token gets 404 on `/v1/platform/*` and Coolify's 404 fallback on `/api/v1/settings…` and `/api/v1/admin` (no such API route); `tests/policy-engine` and `tests/policy-worker` `platform.test.ts`; `tests/db/src/platform-admin.test.ts`. **Role matrix**: `team.viewAdmin` is `isAdminOfTeam()`, which Coolify asks only for the audit log's action and source filters (`canGate="viewAdmin"`), so it is compared against `audit.read` (DP-75's audit log;
