owner and admin): **64 compared, 14 skipped**; the Admin View page is `isInstanceAdmin()`, i.e. `platform.admin.read`. *Storage*: design.md's `deploy.platform_settings` is Coolify's own `deploy.instance_settings`, its row 0 (`InstanceSettings::findOrFail(0)`) with the nil UUID as org_id, which no org has: row-level security already hides it from every org and lets `asPlatformAdmin()` read and write it — no migration (750 not used); it is created on first read with the table's defaults, the sponsorship reminder off. **Each setting** — *ported* (stored, the operator's alone, Coolify's rules and Laravel's messages, `submit()` "Instance settings updated successfully!", `instantSave()` "Settings updated!"): General's Name (`instance_name`, nullable|string|max:255); Advanced's Registration (`is_registration_enabled`), Destructive action confirmation (`disable_two_step_confirmation`), Passkeys (`is_passkey_enabled`), DNS validation (`is_dns_validation_enabled`), Sponsorship reminders (`is_sponsorship_popup_enabled`, off; Freeploy shows no popup either way). **Stored, not yet read** by what they govern: identity-worker's registration and passkeys, deploy-web's confirmations, DP-52's domain DNS check (still Coolify's default, on), Coolify's `[Name]` title prefix (a tenant cannot read the row). *The edge's or the deployment's configuration* (not duplicated): URL `fqdn` (deploy-web's own origin, deploy-core's `DEPLOY_CONSOLE_URL`, api-edge's CORS origins); API access and Allowed IPs (`is_api_enabled`, `allowed_ips`: api-edge's `API_ENABLED`, `API_ALLOWED_IPS`, DP-70); Password registration with OAuth and the whole Authentication page (`disable_registration_when_oauth_enabled`, `/settings/oauth[/{provider}]`: identity-worker's `GITHUB_|GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID|SECRET`, `OAUTH_*`); the Email page (`smtp_*`, `resend_*`, `/settings/email`: notifications-worker's `NOTIFICATIONS_PROVIDER`, `EMAIL` binding, `EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS|NAME`); Docker registry (`docker_registry_url`, Updates: deploy-core's `DEFAULT_ENGINE_CONFIG.helperImage`); image storage, Image CDN URL and File downloads (`avatar_storage_*`, `image_cdn_url`, `file_download_s3_storage_id`: profile pictures are identity's, served by the edge, DP-25; downloads are R2's, design §4.4). *Not applicable to a hosted service*: Redirect HTTP to HTTPS (Workers serve HTTPS only); public IPv4/IPv6 and Detect, and the domain-conflict check of the instance URL (the addresses of Coolify's own host, server 0 — Freeploy runs on no host of its own); Instance timezone (only the update schedule reads it); the public port range (`public_port_min|max`: validated by Settings\Index, read nowhere at 91b7e3e); Development helper (`isDev()` only); MCP server (Freeploy has no `/mcp`); Custom DNS servers (a Worker resolves over DNS-over-HTTPS and cannot use resolver addresses); Allowed internal targets and Localhost targets (`webhook_allowed_internal_hosts`, `webhook_allow_localhost`: a Worker's fetch has no route into private networks, DP-67); Navigation (`is_wire_navigate_enabled`: deploy-web is a single-page app); Anonymous telemetry (`do_not_track`: Freeploy reports nothing to coollabs.io); Domain Connect and Server validation limits (`isCloud()` sections; the reference is self-hosted, and validating a server waits for the SSH executor, W3); the whole Updates page (`/settings/updates`: auto-update, its frequency, update checks, channel and custom tag, "Check now" — Freeploy is not an installed instance; a merge to `main` deploys it); the whole Backup page (`/settings/backup`, backups of Coolify's own database and `is_backup_before_update_enabled` — Freeploy's database is Supabase Postgres, backed up by Supabase). *Deferred*: Instance Image Scanning (`/settings/image-scanning`, every team's default rules) with image scanning (DP11); the audit line Coolify writes on a save (`ui.instance.settings.updated`). **Pages** (hand-ported from the Blade views, "Coolify" read as "Freeploy"): `/settings` and `/settings/advanced` in `<x-settings.layout>` with only the ported fields; `/admin` (current user, Active and Inactive subscribers — teams whose Freeploy subscription is `active`/`trialing`, and teams with one that is not, Coolify's paid `stripe_invoice_paid` —, User lookup: Coolify's case-sensitive `like` on name or email, the Subscription badge, "N matching users"); `/team/admin` (Instance users: search, All users / Current team / Outside team, the four sorts, pages of 10 clamped to 1–100, the operator left out). Every page shows Coolify's 404 page to anyone else (Coolify redirects a non-admin to the dashboard, or aborts 403 on `/admin`). The sidebar shows Settings (General, Advanced) after Tags, Admin below the menu and Team → Admin View after Image Scanning to an operator only (`layout/PlatformMenu.tsx` through new `platform` slots of the generated layout, added as AppShellMarkup.json patches; Layout asks `GET /v1/platform/operator` on each page). **Not ported, and why**: *Switch user* (Admin\Index::switchUser, its "Impersonation is active" callout and "Return to root user") — signing in as a customer would let the operator act inside their org outside that org's own roles, sessions and audit, with no consent; the User lookup's description reads "Find an account for support or administration." and its Actions column is empty. *Deleting a user* from Admin View (password confirmation, "The root user cannot be deleted."…) — an account is deleted by its owner from the profile, under identity's rules (DP-25). A lookup lists at most 100 users (Coolify: all). Not verified: the visual gate (the reference capture signs in as a tenant; these pages need a capture as Coolify's instance admin) and stage (no operator is set yet).
- **Updates, Backup, Email and Authentication (DP-101).** The instance settings pages DP-79 left out, ported for the platform operator: `/settings/updates` (Settings\Updates), `/settings/backup` (SettingsBackup), `/settings/email` (SettingsEmail) and `/settings/oauth[/{provider}]` (SettingsOauth), hand-ported from their Blade views (`apps/deploy-web/src/pages/PlatformSettingsMore.tsx`, the decisions in `src/lib/instance-settings.ts`; `routes.ts` `InstanceSettingsMorePageKey`; the sidebar's Settings submenu now lists Updates, then Authentication after a divider, Email and Backup, as navbar.blade.php does; the settings layout's page search lists them), "Coolify" read as "Freeploy". **This reverses DP-79's call** that the Email and Authentication pages are the deployment's configuration and the Updates and Backup pages do not apply: they are now stored settings of the operator's, *stored, not yet read* by what they govern (identity-worker still signs in with its own GitHub and Google configuration; notifications-worker still sends with its own provider; nothing updates or backs up the platform from them). *Endpoints* (deploy-core-worker `handlers/platform-settings-more.ts`, reached from DP-79's `handlers/platform.ts` behind the same gate; the component logic in `src/platform/{updates,email,oauth}.ts`): `GET|PATCH /v1/platform/settings/updates`, `…/backup`, `…/email`, `…/oauth[/{provider}]`. api-edge's platform facade forwards GET and PATCH only and is unchanged, so every action is a PATCH with the action's name (`{ action, …properties }`). A read asks policy-worker for `platform.settings.read`, an action for `platform.settings.update` first (Coolify's `$this->authorize('update', …)` before `validate()`); a denial is DP-79's 404 and opens no store. An action runs in one `asPlatformAdmin()` transaction and its failure is answered after the commit, so what Coolify saves before it fails (a provider `ensureProviderCanBeEnabled()` turns off) is kept. *Storage, no migration*: the update, email and registration-policy columns of DP-79's row 0 of `deploy.instance_settings`, and `deploy.oauth_settings` (Coolify's table, its global unique `provider` index) with OauthSettingSeeder's eleven providers created on first read, owned by the nil UUID — row-level security hides both from every org (`packages/db/src/deploy/platform-settings-more.ts`). *Secrets*: the SMTP password and username, host, the Resend API key, the SMTP, Resend and notification senders (Coolify's encrypted casts on InstanceSettings) and each `oauth_settings.client_secret` (OauthSetting encrypts it in an accessor the schema generator does not see) are fp1 ciphertexts under the platform row's data key (`deploy.org_keys` for the nil UUID, wrapped by `DEPLOY_ENCRYPTION_KEY`); the API returns them decrypted only with `platform.settings.update`, as Coolify fills them into its instance admin's password inputs, and `null` ("Hidden (only admins can view)") otherwise. **Updates**: Channel (Stable, Preview, Nightly, Custom image, their callouts), Image tag, Check frequency, Automatic updates (`onChange="instantSave"`), Update frequency; `instantSave()` stores `is_auto_update_enabled`, both frequencies, `update_channel` with `switchUpdateChannel()`'s `previous_update_channel`, and `update_custom_tag` with Coolify's rules (`required` of the update frequency when automatic updates are *saved* on, `required|string|in:stable,preview,nightly,custom`, `nullable|required_if:update_channel,custom|string|max:128|regex:…`) and "Settings updated!"; `submit()` first runs the #[Validate] rules and `validate_cron_expression()` (DP-65's shared `helpers/cron.ts`) with "Invalid Cron / Human expression for Auto Update Frequency." / "… Update Check Frequency.", storing nothing, and the page refills an empty field as Coolify does (a random `N 0 * * *`, `0 * * * *`). *Departures*: Freeploy has no server 0, so — as on Coolify Cloud — no `REGISTRY_URL` is synced into a `.env` and no dynamic proxy configuration is written; a changed channel runs no `CheckForUpdatesJob` (it returns at once on Coolify Cloud too); "Check now" answers 501 "Checking for updates is not available yet: Freeploy is not an installed instance, and a merge to main deploys it."; `isUpgradeAvailable` is only a custom image with a tag (no version is compared), and its "Upgrade now" answers 501 "Upgrading the instance needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." with no upgrade modal or progress (`UpdateCoolify` runs on server 0). *Cut*: the Docker registry listbox (DP-79: the deployment's configuration); the `AUTOUPDATE` environment override; the audit line. **Backup**: Coolify's `isFunctional()` and instance-database branches cannot be reached — Freeploy has no server 0 and no `StandalonePostgresql` 0 (its database is Supabase's) — so the page is the "Backup is not configured" empty state; Configure backup (`addCoolifyDatabase()`: `docker inspect coolify-db` on server 0) answers 501 "Configuring the instance backup needs the SSH executor, which is not available yet." and creates no database or schedule. *Cut*: the instance database form, `backup-edit`, Backup before update (`is_backup_before_update_enabled`) and the executions, which Coolify shows only with a configured backup; the "Localhost is not ready" callout. **Email**: Email delivery with the provider list (`selectEmailProvider()`: an incomplete provider shows its fields and "Not active yet" without an error and stores nothing), SMTP's Sender, Connection, Authentication and Advanced groups, Resend's fields, the notification sender; `submit()` with `enableSelectedEmailProvider()` (the saved provider stays on when the new one fails), every #[Validate] rule, ValidHostname on the EHLO domain and "Transactional email settings updated."; the SMTP and Resend rules and messages are DP-67's (`notifications/settings.ts`, now exported for reuse). "Send test" (shown once a provider is saved, else disabled "Save an email provider first.") runs `sendTestEmail()`'s checks and messages ("Test email address is required.", "Please enter a valid email address.", "Notification from address is required to test the notification sender.", then the saved provider's rules) and answers 501 with the repo's hosted-email wording, "The hosted email service is not available yet: Freeploy has no sending domain. Use the team email settings with Resend." — Coolify saves the page's sender before it sends; nothing is stored here, and no rate limit is counted. *Cut*: the named SMTP servers (`ManagesSmtpServers`: add from a template, switch, rename, delete; `smtp_servers`), as on DP-67's team page; `toggleSmtp()`, `toggleResend()`, `submitSmtp()`, `submitResend()`, which the page does not call; the audit line. **Authentication**: Registration (`saveRegistrationPolicy()`, "Authentication settings updated successfully!") and one section per provider, OpenID Connect first, labelled by `providerLabel()` ("OpenID Connect", "GitLab", else `Str::headline()`, so "Github"), with the blade's fields and checkboxes per provider; Enable checks the required fields in the browser, then `toggleProvider()` (`validateProviderCanBeEnabled()`: "OAuth settings are not complete for {provider}.<br/>Please fill in all required fields.", "OIDC scopes must include 'openid'.", nothing stored) and "OAuth settings for {provider} updated successfully!"; `submit()` of the route's provider, or of every provider with "Instance settings updated successfully!" and the joined errors of the providers it disabled; every rule of `validationRules()` with Laravel's messages (":attribute" of `oauth_settings_map.github.client_id` is "oauth settings map.github.client id"); an unknown `{provider}` is a 404. *Departures*: the redirect URI must end with Freeploy's callback, `/v1/auth/oauth/{provider}/callback` on the API (identity-worker's), not Coolify's `/auth/{provider}/callback`, and its placeholder is the API's; a validation error is shown at its field and as a toast (Coolify's `handleError()` toasts it). *Cut*: the provider list teleported under the sidebar entry and its brand icons (`/settings/oauth/{provider}` scrolls to the provider's section instead); the audit lines. "Auto-join new users to Root team" keeps Coolify's words though Freeploy has no root team. **Proof**: `tests/deploy-core-worker/src/platform-settings-more.test.ts` (PGlite at head with row-level security, policy-worker's real router): every route is a 404 that opens no store for a user who is owner, admin, builder, viewer or billing_admin of every org, for an API key with the operator's id, and with no actor; each page's rules, messages, toasts and stored rows; the ciphertexts at rest; the 501s leaving row 0, the providers and the backup tables as they were; no org reading or changing the rows. `tests/db/src/platform-settings-more.test.ts`; `apps/deploy-web/test/{routes,instance-settings,platform-sidebar}.test.ts`. Not verified: the visual gate (it needs a capture as Coolify's instance admin, as DP-79's pages do) and stage (no operator is set yet).

## Departures from the design

- **No sponsorship popup.** Coolify shows a monthly "Love Coolify? Support our work" popup (`livewire/layout-popups`, instance setting `is_sponsorship_popup_enabled`). Freeploy does not: it asks users to sponsor coollabs.io, which would imply affiliation. The setting exists as a platform-admin setting defaulting to off, and the reference turns it off before capture so screens compare like with like.
